Grab = the shared `resolveGrab` throw. **Spin-Punch command chain** (Fwd+Heavy, cancel-on-hit rekka, **FREE** — recovery-committed): Turn1 40→**24** → Turn2 46→**28** → Turn3 82→**49** launcher = **~101 EFF** full 3-stage confirm (≈ the roster's other rekka strings; whiff/block ENDS it via shared `rekkaContinue`).
> - **Tiered tap/hold punch-combo** (neutral Special, resolved on RELEASE — the Madara-ult tap/hold pattern): TAP → "Consecutive Punches 10×" (13/hit, 20 Serious) · HOLD → "20×" (20/hit, 35 Serious). A genuine multi-hit flurry (hasHit re-armed every 3 active frames), but **combo-decay caps the total** (observed ~50–90 EFF regardless of hit-count) — self-limiting, and bounded by the active window (can't combo-lock; recovery releases the foe). The HOLD tier out-damages the TAP via a bigger per-hit + longer window, for a higher cost.
> - **6 specials + Side Hop (RAW → EFF ×0.60, cost)** — GROUND: **Serious Punch** (Fwd) 130→**78** melee + a traveling shockwave projectile 62→**37** (`spawnProjectile`, separate ranged hitbox), **superArmor** through the long windup, 45 — **~115 EFF combined = the marquee payoff, the design's intended power spike** · **Two-Handed Punches** (Back) 96→**58**, 30 · **Today Is Bargain Sale** (Up) 92→**55**, 30 · **Serious Table Flip** (Down) 78→**47** launcher, 25. AIR: **Headbutt** (neutral) 72→**43**, 20 · **Up→Down Combo** (Fwd) 100→**60** spike, 35 · **Side Hop** (Back/Down) = **0 dmg** evasive i-frames + hop, 15. Every per-hit value sits in an existing special band (the top, Serious Punch's 78, ties Zaraki-tier singles; the rest are 43–60).
> - **Ultimate = "Death Punch"** (Serious Series) — an INLINE camera-focus / freeze cinematic on the **LIVE fighter** (charge pose → impact pose; built inline to sidestep the duplicate-instance bug, verified) + a hi-res FULLSCREEN backdrop overlay. **340 RAW → 204 EFF — routed through `applyScaledDamage` (the ×0.60 choke-point), NOT a manual-subtract unscaled sure-hit** (a held block chips to 25%), cost 100. Effective **204 = Isshiki Daikokuten Barrage 204 / Madara Tengai 204**, just below Pain Chibaku 216 — **squarely the cinematic-ult band, and honestly SCALED (the conservative side of §"global damage scale").**
> - **Does the SHEER NUMBER of moves create a concern? NO — it's VERSATILITY, not power (Madara/Isshiki/Pain precedent).** Every individual number is in-band; there is no bypass anywhere (even the ult is scaled); and the whole kit draws on ONE 150-Serious pool priced 15–45 per special — he physically cannot chain the marquee moves without running dry (2–3 big specials empties the bar). More *options* widen his mixup/answer coverage; they do not raise his ceiling, because the ceiling is set by per-hit EFF (all in-band) and the pool throttle. This is the same verdict reached for the other large kits.
> - **Overall verdict: FAIR — a durable powerhouse whose identity is TAKING hits and ending fights with the Serious/Death Punch payoff, with an honest fully-scaled pipeline, no stat record, and a large-but-energy-throttled kit.** **No fix applied (diagnosis-only).** **Watch-items (two, minor):** (1) **HP 1280 × Def 92 = the tuning surface** — 2nd-tankiest fighter (behind only Superman 1450×92), by deliberate design; **knob: trim HP toward ~1220 if playtests read him as oppressive** (still above the 1260 original-8 ceiling). Same class as Superman's flagged watch-item. (2) **Serious Punch's superArmor** lets it shrug one hit through the wind-up — strong, but the **45 cost + a long 24-active-frame commitment (heavily punishable on whiff/block)** is the built-in counterweight; the knob is the armor window, not the damage. Full file-utilization in `SAITAMA_ASSET_MAP.md`; canonical `test:saitama` covers registration/stats/portrait/"Serious" label, movement/state + intro, the normals + grab, the command chain, both combo tiers, all 6 specials + Side Hop + the Serious Punch shockwave, the Death Punch ult (live-fighter cinematic + hi-res backdrop + guaranteed payoff), and a 29-action fallback-box sweep.
>
> - **⚠ SPEED-TIER RETUNE — 2026-08-15 (FLAGGED OUTLIER, not silently shipped).** Saitama's movement was matched to **Toji's speed tier**: **Spd 84 → 98, dashSpeed 16 → 22, dashCooldownMax → runtime ~14f (speed-derived), mobility medium → very_high (DOUBLE air-dash), + dashTeleport blink** (his own `saitama_dash_uniform.png` art). **This creates a REAL balance outlier and is called out here rather than buried:** Toji's identical top speed (98) is explicitly justified by a **glass-cannon 1050 HP / 82 Def**; Saitama carries the SAME top mobility on **1280 HP / 92 Def** (2nd-tankiest fighter) **PLUS the largest kit in the project** — i.e. Toji's speed with **+230 HP, +10 Def, and vastly more options, and none of the fragility downside**. His base mobility (dash/air-dash/teleport) is **NOT** gated by the "Serious" meter, so the neutral/approach game is now top-tier *for free*. **Reads as strictly-better-than-Toji's-speed-tier.** Possible justification (deliberate, per canon): Saitama is *meant* to be absurd. **DECISION (2026-08-15): SHIP AS-IS** — accepted as a deliberate outlier (Saitama is canonically absurd; the versatility is already the design's flagged exception). **No stat nerf applied, by choice — this is a conscious call, not an oversight.** **Compensating knobs kept on file if playtests later read him as oppressive:** trim HP toward **~1120–1180** (turns the bulk into a real tradeoff for the speed) and/or Def 92 → ~86. (NOTE: the `mobility → double air-dash` gate in `physics.js` read `stats.mobility` which was always undefined, so the double air-dash had been **dead for every very_high char**; the same commit fixed it to read `traits.mobility`, so zenitsu/shinobu/nezuko/killua/flash/maki/toji ALSO gain their intended 2nd air-dash — a roster-wide mobility change to keep in mind.)
>
> **Orochimaru (Naruto — the immortal Sannin, 36th sprite char) — added 2026-08-15.** The manipulative, technique-hoarding schemer — a bottomless bag of forbidden jutsu (snakes, the Kusanagi blade, body-transfer forms) who wins on TOOLS and mixups, not raw power. **A LARGEST-CLASS kit (Madara/Ichigo/Hashirama/Pain/Isshiki/Saitama scope exception, confirmed by the build mandate — the source sheets carry this many genuinely distinct real techniques): 5 normals + a throw-weapon grab + Forward Strong + Aerial Strong + a 3-stage Fwd+Heavy command chain + 8 direction-branched specials + 3 alternate FORMS + a freeze/camera-focus Summon ultimate.** **ZERO damage-scale bypass — the ENTIRE kit is honestly ×0.60 scaled (normals/strongs/command-chain via `createAttackFromMove`, ranged specials via `spawnProjectile`, the grab via shared `resolveGrab`, the ult via `applyScaledDamage` — no manual-subtract, no summons-as-damage, no DOT). The 3 forms add ZERO power (cosmetic body-swaps). Breadth is VERSATILITY, throttled by a shared 210 chakra pool + per-move costs — NOT raw power. NO stat is a roster record.**
> - Core: **HP 1180 · Energy 210 chakra · Atk 90 · Def 86 · Spd 92 · mobility medium · spriteScale 2.6 (~107px).** All INSIDE existing bands, NO records — HP **1180** = Sasuke/Madara (the durable-shinobi band, below Jason 1250 / Isshiki 1300 / Superman 1450); Energy **210** = Pain 210 (between Ichigo/Goku 200 and Gojo/Madara 220 — a deep pool to FEED the large kit + the SELF-LIMITING throttle); Atk **90** = Obito/Tobi/Pain (mid; base Atk does NOT scale damage — flavor; the 94–100 top is untouched); Def **86** = a touch above the shinobi 84, below Toji 89 / Superman 92 (a slippery technician, not a wall); Spd **92** = Madara/Rengoku (upper-mid — an agile snake, deliberately NOT the teleport-tier 96–98).
> - Offense (RAW → EFF ×0.60): **normals** light 30→**18** (snake-whip auto-combo) · heavy 60→**36** (Kusanagi thrust, long reach) · up 46→**28** launcher · air 42→**25** · down_air 50→**30**. **Bonus strongs:** Aerial Strong (air+Heavy) 64→**38** · Forward Strong (Fwd+Heavy command normal) 66→**40**. **Grab (throw-weapon)** = shared `resolveGrab` throw, 90→**54**. **3-stage command chain** (Fwd+Heavy, cancel-on-hit rekka via shared `rekkaContinue`): Forward Strong 66→**40** → Chain2 50→**30** → Chain3 82→**49** launcher = **~119 EFF** full confirm (whiff/block ENDS it). All in-band.
> - **8 specials (RAW → EFF ×0.60, cost)** — GROUND: **Snake Spit** (neutral, ranged `spawnProjectile`) 60→**36**, 24 · **Kusanagi Sword Lunge** (Fwd, melee advance) 78→**47**, 26 · **Kusanagi Sword Throw** (Back, ranged `spawnProjectile`) 74→**44**, 28 · **Snake-Tail Sweep** (Up, anti-air launcher) 70→**42**, 22 · **Slam** (Down, low spike) 76→**46**, 24. AIR: **Striking Shadow Snake** (neutral, melee dive) 82→**49**, 26 · **Hidden Shadow Snakes** (Fwd, 3-snake ranged barrage) 34/hit→**~20/hit**, 34 · **Snake-Form Coil** (Back, melee) 66→**40**, 28. Every per-hit value sits in an existing special band (36–49 EFF).
> - **KUSANAGI 04-vs-09 STRUCTURAL DECISION (build mandate):** sliced + compared — `special_move_04` and `09` are **TWO INDEPENDENT snake moves** (04 = a close-range single-snake *lunge* anchored to the caster; 09 = a *ranged multi-snake barrage* of detached traveling projectiles), NOT one technique's tap/hold paths (different mechanics + frame structure). **Naming correction:** the `[PATTERN-MATCH]` "Kusanagi" (his *sword*) guess for 04/09 was WRONG — both are SNAKE techniques; the actual Kusanagi *sword* moves are `06` (throw) + `07` (lunge). Built as separate specials accordingly.
> - **3 alternate FORMS (Stage 4) — COSMETIC, ZERO power.** Charge-TAP cycles base → **Host Body** → **White Snake** → **Serpent Sage** → base (his canonical body-transfer), each sharing one shed-skin transition. A form is a FULL merged copy of base `animationData` + host-body art overlaid on idle/run/dash (everything else falls back to real base sheets — verified, no 128² box). **No stat buff, no damage mult — a 15-chakra mixup/flavor swap only.** Deliberately power-free so an already-large kit gains NO ceiling from the forms.
> - **Ultimate = "Summoning: Twin Serpents"** — an INLINE freeze/camera-focus cinematic on the **LIVE fighter** (a giant Manda-class serpent strike; built inline to sidestep the duplicate-instance bug — verified: the summon timer + cast pose are on the real fighter, no dup). **350 RAW → 210 EFF — routed through `applyScaledDamage` (the ×0.60 choke-point), NOT a manual-subtract sure-hit** (a held block chips to 25%), cost 100. Effective **210** = between Isshiki/Madara 204 and Pain 216 — squarely the cinematic-ult band, honestly SCALED.
> - **Does the SHEER SIZE of the kit create a concern? NO — VERSATILITY, not power (Madara/Isshiki/Pain/Saitama precedent).** Every individual number is in-band; no bypass anywhere (the ult is scaled, the forms cosmetic); the whole offensive kit draws on ONE 210-chakra pool priced 22–34/special + 100/ult — he cannot deploy everything at once. More *options* widen mixup coverage; they do not raise his ceiling.
> - **DUPLICATE-MOVE RESOLUTION (build mandate):** `p2_special_move_01` was **confirmed a byte-near duplicate** of `p1_strong_attack_forward` (94.6% alpha-silhouette match, 1px canvas offset). The Forward Strong is sourced from **`p1_strong_attack_forward.png` ONLY**; `special_move_01` is **not imported** and excluded from the 8-special count — verified in `test:orochimaru` (no orochimaru sheet derives from it; no special re-registers the `orochimaruFwdStrong` command-normal).
> - **Overall verdict: FAIR VERSATILITY outlier (Madara/Ichigo/Pain/Saitama class), not a power outlier — no per-move nerf needed.** Fully-scaled pipeline, no stat record, cosmetic forms, a scaled cinematic ult, all throttled by the shared 210 chakra pool. **No fix applied (diagnosis-only).** **Watch-items (two, minor):** (1) the **air Fwd Snake Barrage** (3 projectiles) can chunk if all 3 land (~60 EFF) — the 34 cost + air-only input is the counterweight; the knob is cost/projectile-count before damage. (2) the forms are **free of downside** (cosmetic, cheap) — harmless by design, but if a form is ever given a buff it must be re-audited (currently ZERO power). Canonical `test:orochimaru` = **60/0** (registration/stats/portrait/Chakra label, movement/state + 4 hit tiers + 2 knockdowns, 3-part intro, 5 normals + grab + strongs, the 3-stage command chain, all 8 specials + a projectile connect, all 3 forms + the fallback-chain sweep, the Summon ult live-fighter cinematic + guaranteed payoff, the duplicate no-double-register check, and a 38-action fallback-box sweep).

> **★2026-08-23 4-FORM SPRITE REBUILD (S0-S7) — balance re-scope.** Goku was rebuilt from a procedural box into
> a real 4-form Extreme Butoden sprite char, which changed the kit shape (BALANCE-relevant):
> - **MELEE-ONLY.** **Kamehameha is CUT** (no beam art exists on any EB sheet) → Goku has **NO ranged special**.
>   His only special is **Dragon Fist** (the same 150→90 EFF melee rush). This is an unusual shape — a fighter
>   with zero zoning tools. Deliberately NOT compensated with a fabricated projectile; instead:
> - **Command chain** — "Meteor Combination" Fwd+Heavy 3-stage rush (rush1 42 → rush2 50 → rush3 84 launcher,
>   ~101 EFF cumulative, cancel-on-hit). FREE (commits via recovery). Comparable to the Piccolo/Iron-Man rekkas.
> - **Transform ladder (Charge)** — re-scoped from the old numeric 6-tier to the **4 real forms**
>   base → **SSJ** (×1.2/1.1/1.05, thr40, drain0.14) → **SSG** (×1.5/1.25/1.15, thr90, drain0.22) → **SSB**
>   (×2.0/1.4/1.2, thr150, drain0.36). Threshold-gated, no up-front cost, continuous Ki drain, auto-revert at 0
>   — SAME stamina model as Vegeta/Frieza. The ×2.0 SSB ceiling is Ki-bounded to a ~11.5s window from full.
> - **Kaioken (Ultimate button, BASE-form only)** — a stacking burst buff (tier1 ×1.3/1.2, tier2 ×1.55/1.4)
>   whose cost is **HP-STRAIN** (0.15/0.30 HP per frame, auto-off at 10% HP), NOT Ki. This is the intended
>   answer to the melee-only/no-zoning shape: it gives BASE Goku a high-risk burst tool (spend your own life
>   bar for damage) that's mutually exclusive with the Ki-drain transforms. **Two separate resources** (Ki for
>   forms, HP for Kaioken) → the player can't stack both ceilings. ★PLAYTEST-FLAGGED: the Kaioken HP-drain
>   rates + the SSG/SSB thresholds are a design call, deliberately distinct; tune order = drain rates → mults →
>   thresholds. Core stats unchanged (HP1200/EN200/Atk92/Def86/Spd88, now spriteScale 1.1). No stat outlier.

## 6. Risk indicators — payout vs. commitment

**Low-risk / instant for the payout (flagged):**
- 🚩 **Rick Self-Destruct** — **0 startup**, resolves same-frame, 180 RAW (bypass), **no self-damage, no vulnerability state**. Only gates: 220px proximity + 87.5%-meter cost. The purest "no-risk-window" burst in the roster.
- **Toji tojiTeleportStrike** (startup 3) / **rapidStrike** (startup 4/rec 10, 65 dmg) — very fast, zero energy, low recovery. Near-free pressure.
- **Sukuna cleave** — 160 RAW (96 eff) at startup 10 — a lot of damage for a fairly quick special, though recovery 20 is punishable.
- **Naruto Kurama TBB** — guaranteed sure-hit cinematic; block only chips it to 120. Effectively unavoidable payout of 600 once cast.

**High-risk / punishable (the intended-cost side):**
- **Sasuke Two-Strike Lightning** — 30-frame rooted handseal; a hit during it cancels the move AND eats the 24 energy. Most self-punishing move in the set.
- **Naruto rasenshuriken** — recovery 30, runtime whiff cooldown 50 frames. Most punishable projectile.
- **Gojo hollowPurple** — 20 startup / 30 recovery for 200 RAW. Big but heavily telegraphed both ends.
- **Megumi maxElephant** — 20 startup / 26 recovery (slowest summon), though 145 RAW makes it worth it.

**Contrast to flag:** Rick's *ultimate* has the roster's lowest risk-window (instant, no self-damage) while Sasuke's *specials* carry the highest (30f handseal). Two opposite ends of the risk/reward axis with no shared tuning basis.

---

## 7. HP side-by-side & compensating-strength check

| Char | HP | vs Rick | Compensating strengths for lower HP? |
|---|---:|---:|---|
| Toji | 1260 | +210 | N/A (top HP) — also best normals, zero-cost kit, 98 speed |
| Sukuna | 1240 | +190 | N/A |
| Goku | 1200 | +150 | N/A |
| Naruto | 1180 | +130 | 600-dmg bypass ult, clone routes |
| Sasuke | 1180 | +130 | ~302 Susanoo sword, sustained form |
| Gojo | 1160 | +110 | biggest meter (220), Infinity auto-dodge ult, hollowPurple |
| Megumi | 1120 | +70 | **ult → 1600 HP**; best summon dmg/energy; RAW-bypass summons |
| **Rick** | **1050** | — | **See below** |

**Rick (1050 HP) — is he compensated, or strictly worse?**

Against the field Rick WAS the **simultaneous floor** on HP (1050), attack (82), defense (78), speed (80), basic-attack damage (light 34 / heavy 60), and damage-per-energy (bottom 4 slots). His genuine edges:
- **Self-Destruct** — instant panic/kill button. **Stage 3d:** scaled to 108 EFF (1a) AND now costs 15% self-HP — a committed high-risk nuke, not a free one.
- **Uncapped Meeseeks** — summons that can stack (`maxSimultaneous 99`); now scaled (27 EFF each, 1a).
- **Portal-Behind teleport** + full projectile zoning kit (Rocket / Portal-Laser / Pull / Push) — all now scaled (1a).

**Verdict — RESOLVED in MK-feel Stage 3d (2026-08-10):** the diagnosis held — Rick's compensations were gimmick-concentrated and his neutral was under-tuned, made worse once 1a scaled his whole kit down. Stage 3d **redistributed** rather than piled on: the risk-free gimmick nuke gained a real cost (15% self-HP), and his neutral was lifted off the floor (light 34→40, heavy 60→72, speed 80→84). He keeps the frail-zoner identity (HP 1050 / def 78) but is no longer the simultaneous floor on normals + speed, and his power is now spread across the kit instead of concentrated in two gimmicks. `test:stage3d-rick` 15/0.

---

## 8. Outliers — flagged, not fixed

1. 🚩 **The 0.60 scale bypass (systemic).** Summons, manual-subtract ultimates, and DOT bypass `GLOBAL_DAMAGE_SCALE`, so **Megumi, Rick's ult, and Naruto's Kurama/DOT hit 1.667× harder relative to every scaled melee/projectile character.** The scale comment's "relative balance untouched" is false. This warps *every* cross-character comparison below and is the highest-priority item.

2. 🚩 **Megumi's summon damage-per-energy.** 4 of the top 5 non-ult DPE slots (3.50–4.75), and because summons are RAW/bypass she delivers those at full value while paying only 9.5–16.7% of a 210 meter. Statistically out of line vs the ~2.2–2.4 DPE the scaled cast pays.

3. 🚩 **Rick Self-Destruct — zero-startup, no-self-damage, bypass-scale burst.** 180 RAW delivered instantly with no vulnerability window. Uniquely low-risk payout; only lever holding it is the 87.5%-meter cost + proximity. Explicitly the kind of move the brief asked to flag.

4. ✅ **Toji pays nothing, ever** — *FIXED (MK-feel Stage 3a).* Was: `maxEnergy: 0` → inventorySmash, rekka, and the 1.6×/1.8× ultimate all cost 0; infinite DPE + best normals + top HP/speed. Now: keeps `maxEnergy: 0` (flavor) but his **special-button specials + ultimate draw a cursed-tool DURABILITY meter** (max 100, ~0.12/frame regen ≈ 14s empty→full; costs inventory 40 / chain 34 / curse 24 / rapid 18 / ult 55 — below cost the move fizzles), the **ultimate is on a 45s lockout** (2700f, vs the 20s universal), and **HP 1260 → 1120** (no longer the roster ceiling). Effective per-move damage is UNCHANGED (still RAW×0.60); the fix is the resource economy, not the numbers. His DPE is now finite and rationed like everyone else. (The stance command-normals + the teleport-strike movement tech stay free — they are normals, not specials.)

5. 🚩 **Naruto Kurama ult = 600 RAW, guaranteed, bypass-scale.** ~3× the effective damage of the next-biggest scaled hit (Sasuke's ~302 sword), unavoidable (block only chips to 120), for 95 energy (DPE 6.32, highest in roster). The single most valuable button in the game — *per cast*. Its old **80s recast** (4× the universal 20s) was the counterweight, but it over-corrected: on a damage-per-cooldown basis the TBB sat BELOW Rick/Sasuke ults at 80s, so it *felt* nerfed despite the huge payload. **Retuned to 40s** (§Naruto-ult-retune) — the per-cast value stays flagged-high, but the availability is now in line with the pack.

6. **Sasuke Susanoo economics. ✅ RESOLVED (MK-feel Stage 3e).** One up-front cost bought ~20s of free giant attacks topping ~302 eff/sword — a sustained-form value structure that didn't compare cleanly to the per-cast meter economy. Fixed by shortening the free window: `SASUKE_SUSANOO_DURATION_FRAMES` 1200→800 (~20s→~13.3s), Sasuke-only (Itachi/Netero keep the shared 1200). Swings stay free by design (per-swing energy would brick Lv2's drain-to-0); the ~1/3 shorter window is the lever. `test:stage3e-sasuke` 10/0.

7. **Rick — low floor. ✅ RESOLVED (MK-feel Stage 3d).** Was bottom of the roster on HP/atk/def/spd/normals/DPE at once with gimmick-concentrated compensations. Fixed by redistribution: Self-Destruct gained a 15% self-HP cost (no longer a risk-free nuke) and his neutral was lifted off the floor (light 34→40, heavy 60→72, speed 80→84). Keeps the frail-zoner identity; no longer the simultaneous floor.

8. **Data-block vs runtime discrepancies (accuracy, not balance):**
   - Naruto ult `cost:100` declared, **95 spent** in code (`abilities.js:833`).
   - Chidori Koiten header comment says "55 raw"; code spends **35** and deals **95** (`abilities.js:1842-1845`).
   - `abilities.js` hardcodes special damage rather than reading the `characters.js` block (they happen to match except Big Ball charge-scaling and base-rasengan `active` 5-vs-4). If numbers get retuned, **both** places need editing or they'll silently diverge.

---

## §Naruto-ult-retune — Kurama TBB recast cooldown 80s → 40s (2026-07-24, applied)

**Reported problem:** ~80s of ultimate lockout for ~half a health bar reads as clearly overtuned vs the rest of the roster; Naruto feels "nerfed" because of this specifically.

**Investigation (numbers pulled, not guessed):**
- Naruto ult: **600 raw** (guaranteed sure-hit; block → 120), cost **95** (50% meter), recast **4800f = 80s** (`NARUTO_KURAMA_RECAST_FRAMES`, Naruto-only).
- Every other character: universal **1200f = 20s** (`ULTIMATE_COOLDOWN_FRAMES`). Naruto was the sole outlier at **4×** the pack.
- Damage-per-cooldown (raw/s): Naruto @80s = **7.5**, which is *below* Rick self-destruct (180/20 = 9.0) and Sasuke Susanoo (~302/20 = 15.1) — i.e. despite owning the highest *per-cast* payload in the game, its *availability-adjusted* value sat at the bottom of the ultimate pack. That is exactly the "feels nerfed" report.

**Decision — cooldown REDUCTION, not damage increase.** The per-cast damage (600 raw) is already flagged as the roster's highest (§8 item 5); raising it would deepen an existing outlier. The problem is availability, so cut the cooldown. Chosen value **2400f = 40s (2× the universal 20s baseline)**:
- 600 / 40s = **15.0 raw/s ≈ Sasuke Susanoo's 15.1** — dead in line with the pack's premium ultimate, not an outlier either direction. (30s → 20 raw/s would overshoot to the roster's highest; 20s → equal-cooldown *and* highest-damage = clear outlier.)
- A **2× premium over baseline** is justified by it being the single hardest, unavoidable hit; but it's no longer the near-once-per-match famine 80s produced.
- The **50% meter cost already gates recast** (~20–27s to regen 95 energy), so 40s is the effective cadence: reliably once per 90s round, occasionally twice with real meter setup.

**Cross-check (no reverse outlier):** at 40s Naruto's ult-DPM and damage-per-cooldown match Sasuke's Susanoo and sit above Rick's — premium but not dominant. Damage/cost unchanged, so §5 DPE (6.32) and the §8-item-5 per-cast flag still stand; only availability moved. **Verified in-engine:** TBB connects for 600 (1180→580) and `ultimateCooldown` is set to 2400f/40s on cast (`harness` screenshot `naruto_ult_impact.png`).

**Re-verification (2026-08-02, no change).** The ult was re-flagged as a suspected outlier ("~70s cooldown for ~half a health bar, never confirmed fixed"). Re-pulled the live values: cooldown **2400f/40s** (`abilities.js:92`, applied `abilities.js:2183`), damage **600 raw** (`kurama.js:70`), cost **95** (50% meter, `abilities.js:2166`). The re-flag's premise is **stale** — those are the post-retune numbers, not the pre-retune 80s/famine state. Against the current (grown) roster Naruto is the **highest raw per-cast ult** (next band Superman 380 / Rengoku·Omni-Man 340) held in check by the **longest cooldown** (pack baseline 20s; cooldown-gated chars 8s), landing at 15 raw/s ≈ Sasuke Susanoo — in line on damage-per-cooldown. The re-flag asked to *reduce* cooldown / *raise* damage; either would push the roster's most valuable button into a genuine outlier, opposite the stated "bring him in line" goal. **Decision: no change.** Flag closed. Re-verified in-engine (`naruto_ult_impact.png`).

---

*End of diagnosis. Naruto Kurama recast retuned 80s→40s (§Naruto-ult-retune); re-verified 2026-08-02, no change. All other values unchanged.*

## §Feedback — Omnitrix form balance (2026-07-28, added)

Feedback is a Ben 10 **Omnitrix transform form** (energy-absorption specialist), sibling to XLR8 /
Diamondhead. This is the first Ben 10 balance entry (the family predates this doc). All Feedback damage
routes through the honest **×0.60 GLOBAL_DAMAGE_SCALE** — normals via `createAttackFromMove`, every
projectile (discharge / redirect / overload) via `spawnProjectile` (`combat.js:934`). **No bypass.**

- **Core (BEN10_ALIEN_POOL.feedback):** HP 980 · Energy 100 · dmg 0.95 · spd 8 · role zoner. HP 980
  ties Netero (2nd-frailest tier); moderate offense/mobility — a defensive counter-zoner, not a bruiser.
- **Normals (RAW→EFF):** light ~38→24 · heavy ~76→48 · up ~62→39 (verified in-engine). All reuse the
  electric-shot pose (no melee art) with per-move tuning — shape reuse, standard poke damage.
- **Down Special — Energy Discharge:** projectile RAW 90 → **EFF 54**, cost 22 (22%). DPE 2.45. A plain
  ranged poke, mid-tier.
- **Neutral Special — Energy Absorption (reactive counter):** opens a timed window; an incoming hit is
  full-negated (like Sasuke Absolute Defense / Gojo Infinity) → refunds 20 energy → fires a redirect
  projectile RAW `min(180, 80 + absorbed×1.4)` → **EFF ~98** off a 60-dmg absorb, cost 12.
  **Shape-outlier, not power-outlier:** it is a *timed, one-hit* window with committed recovery
  (whiff = punishable) — STRICTLY more limited than the existing always-on full-negate mechanics it
  mirrors. The redirect is a blockable/whiffable scaled projectile. Counterplay = bait it and punish the
  recovery. Filed as a deliberate, internally-consistent reactive tool (like Sasuke's `combat.js:312`).
- **Ultimate — Overload:** 3 staggered orbs, RAW 110 each → **EFF ~66**, total **~192** if all connect
  (spacing-dependent), cost 70. DPE 2.74. In line with XLR8 Sonic Blitz (RAW 280 → EFF ~168) and
  Diamondhead Crystal Storm; scaled (no bypass), not guaranteed-full like the manual-subtract ults in §8.

**Verdict:** honest-pipeline character on the scaled side of the roster; no GLOBAL_DAMAGE_SCALE bypass.
The absorb counter is the only notable mechanic and is well-counterplayed. No fixes required.

## Combo-String Standardization (2026-08-10) — balance impact

Roster-wide combo-string standardization (COMBO_STANDARDIZATION_AUDIT.md, Stages A–G). Full-effort
verdict: **standardization TOWARD the roster norm, not power-creep.** No damage values were changed;
the net buffs move under-equipped characters UP to parity with peers who already had the same tool, and
every gain stays bounded by the roster's shared systems: combo-decay (`COMBO_DAMAGE_CURVE`), the single
−26 launch floor (all launchers), `maxAirHits=3`, the jump-cancel execution requirement, and cancel-on-hit
gating (a whiff/block ends any string).

- **Stage B — 8 opener conversions (netero/killua/hisoka/flash/gon/batman/zenitsu/ghostface):** Down+Heavy
  → Forward+Heavy. Input-direction ONLY — zero frame/damage change; the deterministic step-in glide
  (`COMBO_STEP_IN_VX`) is retained. **Balance-NEUTRAL.**
- **Stage C — 5 finishers now LAUNCH (shinobu/inosuke/tobirama/netero/ghostface):** was `category:"heavy"`,
  now `launcher:true`. Grants each a grounded-chain → air-combo conversion it lacked. Finisher DAMAGE is
  unchanged (only the launch flag); it pops to the shared −26 floor, needs a jump-cancel, and juggle hits
  are combo-decay-scaled. This brings the 5 to **parity** with the ~20 rekka chars whose finishers already
  launched. Maki stays a heavy-ender **exception** (her tight cancel-window is the tradeoff). **Fair — parity.**
- **Stage D — 8 new melee strings (itachi/yuji/goku_black/cell/tobi/morty/albedo/omololu):** added to the
  shared `STANDARD_STRING_CHARS`, gaining the Light→Light→Heavy(→launcher) dial-a-combo. It reuses each
  char's OWN existing normals (light/light/upAttack) — **no new or stronger moves** — cancel-on-hit,
  L,L,H-capped (no third light, no loop), launcher = their existing up-attack (same damage). Matches the 6
  chars who already had it and the rekka roster. **Fair — parity.**

**Verdict:** no per-move damage change anywhere; the only power deltas (Stage C ×5, Stage D ×8) equalize
under-equipped melee characters to the roster's existing combo/juggle baseline, all in-band under the
shared decay/launch/cancel systems. No fixes required. True zoners (rickPrime/evilMorty/beerus/piccolo/
frieza) intentionally keep no combo string.

---

## Combo Breaker + Comeback Finisher (two universal systems) — added 2026-08-12

Two roster-wide defensive/comeback systems, built Stages 0–4. **Diagnosis-only pass — nothing rebalanced
here; the two watch-items below are FLAGGED, not changed.** Stage 0 audit confirmed the exclusion set
(chars with a bespoke below-threshold comeback keep THEIRS): **Toji** (2-stage save), **Maki** (≤25% HP
ult unlock), **Gon** (adult-form sudden-death). No double-comeback for anyone.

### A. COMBO BREAKER (universal, hybrid cost) — `combat.js` `tryComboBreaker`
Deals **no damage** — a defensive hitstun-escape (block+special, only while stunned vs an attacker's
`comboCounter ≥ 3`; i-frames + attacker blast). Cost = a per-round **STOCK (2/round, unchanged)** PLUS a
second currency by kit type: **energy chars spend 40 meter**, **meterless chars pay a 360f (6s) cooldown**
(`comboBreakerCd`, the Zenitsu/Rengoku currency model). Meterless detection uses `traits.hasEnergy`, not
runtime `maxEnergy` (which `createFighter` clamps to ≥1).

> **⚠️ WATCH-ITEM — the flat-40 meter cost is UNEVEN across the 100–220 energy range.** 40 meter is **40%
> of the bar for the 100-energy chars** (flash/batman/ghostface/ben10/albedo — who start each round at 50%
> = 50 energy → **~1 break then must regen**) but only **~18% for the 220-energy chars** (gojo/hashirama/
> madara → 2 breaks, i.e. stock-capped, not meter-capped). Net effect: **low-energy chars effectively get
> fewer breaks/round than high-energy chars** — the opposite of a flat cost's intent. Arguably acceptable
> (a glass-cannon trading offense-energy for a premium defensive escape is a real cost), but it is an
> unintended asymmetry. **Knob, if playtests read it as unfair: switch the meter cost from flat 40 to a
> PERCENTAGE of max energy (~20%)** → ≈20 for a 100-bar, ≈44 for a 220-bar, even relative burden across the
> roster. The meterless 360f cooldown is already even (no meter to vary). Filed as flag-don't-adjust.

### B. COMEBACK FINISHER (universal, Fatal-Blow-style) — `combat.js` `tryComebackFinisher`
Once per MATCH, only below **30% HP**, on a dedicated **block+grab** (no motion, no meter, separate from
the special/ult economy). A committed lunge (16f startup i-frame armour; whiffing wastes the one use;
blockable → ~20% chip). Damage = **fixed `round(min(maxHealth×0.32, 360))`**, applied via a
`_comebackFinisher` override in `resolveAttackHit` (bypasses combo/counter/defense/global-scale → exact
EFFECTIVE number; on the "bypass side" of §"global damage scale", same as the cinematic ults it is
benchmarked against).

**Damage side-by-side vs the existing top-end band (EFFECTIVE):**

| Tier | chars | finisher EFF | % of own HP |
|---|---|---:|---:|
| Frail (HP 960–1120), sub-cap | shinobu 960 … yuji/megumi/tobirama 1120 (21 chars) | **307 – 358** | flat **32.0%** |
| HP ≥ 1125, capped | rengoku 1140 … superman 1450 (30 chars) | **360** | 31.6% → **24.8%** |
| Reference — top-end cinematic ults | Omni-Man/Rengoku 340 · Superman 380 · Sasuke Susanoo ~302 · **Kurama 600 (extreme)** | 302–380 (600) | — |

- **The cap works: no char exceeds 360.** It specifically neutralizes the high-HP outlier the design
  warned about — Superman would have been `0.32×1450 = 464` (2nd-hardest hit in the game), capped to 360.
- **360 sits at the high-mid of the cinematic-ult band** (= Omni-Man/Rengoku 340 +20; below Superman 380;
  far below Kurama 600). For a **once-per-match, <30%-HP-gated, committed, blockable** move, that is
  **in-band, not an outlier**.
- **The cap makes it a genuine comeback shape:** relative payoff is *bigger* for frail users (32% of their
  own bar) and *smaller* for tanks (Superman 24.8%) — the underdog-favouring curve a comeback tool should
  have. (Note the flip side of a *fixed* number: 360 is 37.5% of a 960-HP opponent's bar but 24.8% of a
  1450 bar — it bites harder into frail opponents. Inherent to fixed damage, not a fix.)

> **⚠️ WATCH-ITEM — a universal ~30% once-per-match swing raises the whole roster's comeback ceiling.** This
> is the *design intent* (a comeback system, MK-Fatal-Blow class ≈ 30% below 30% HP), and it is heavily
> counterplayable (HP-gated, one-time, whiffable, blockable→chip, armour only through startup). But every
> eligible fighter now carries a guaranteed-ish 300–360 payoff. **Verdict: deliberate UNIVERSAL comeback
> mechanic, internally consistent, capped WITHIN the existing top-end band — NOT a power outlier** (same
> class as filing Toji's two-stage save as a "deliberate mechanic-outlier"). **Knobs, in order, IF
> playtests read the swing as too strong:** (1) tighten the **HP gate** 30% → 25% (fires less often); (2)
> lower the **cap** 360 → ~320 (Omni-Man-minus, still band); (3) lower **dmgPct** 0.32 → ~0.28. Damage last,
> gate first. The exclusion set (Toji/Maki/Gon) is the one correctness invariant — verified they cannot
> also fire the generic finisher.

**Regression:** `test:stage2d-breaker` 34/0, `test:comeback-finisher` 34/0, live `test:breaker-roster`
16/0 + `test:finisher-roster` 18/0; full-roster suite sweep clean apart from pre-existing/flaky failures
unrelated to these systems (grep-proven 0 code overlap: inosuke/shinobu energy-label flavor, flash
Godspeed ult, madara Gunbai reflect, sharingan giant-sizing, zaraki/ichigo/yuji flaky).

---

> **Onoki, the Third Tsuchikage (Naruto — 33rd sprite char, "Onoki of Both Scales") — added 2026-08-15.** The Dust-Release levitating zoner/technician. **A LARGE kit (Madara/Hashirama-class scope exception, confirmed by the build mandate): 5 normals + a Fwd+Heavy command normal + 6 Dust-Release specials (Rock Fist Transform / Lunge / Arm Swing / Cape-Spin+rock-projectiles / Taunting Combo Finisher launcher / a dual-use CHARGE-hold Jutsu blast) + 3 air/flight-mode specials + a Rock Platform Ride mobility tool + a PERSISTENT stone-golem ultimate.** **ZERO damage-scale bypass — the ENTIRE kit (normals, specials, projectiles, and the golem's every strike) runs through the ×0.60 choke-point (`applyScaledDamage`); NO manual-subtract, NO sure-hit cinematic. The breadth is VERSATILITY, throttled by a shared Particle pool that flight time competes with — NOT raw power. He is deliberately FRAIL (no offensive/defensive stat is a roster record; two are roster-LOW-leaning). TWO mechanic watch-items flagged (the persistent auto-attacking golem; the dual-use charge button), neither a stat outlier.**
>
> - Core: **HP 1120 · Energy 200 Particle · Atk 88 · Def 82 · Spd 84 · mobility medium · canFly · spriteScale 1.55 (~96px, reads short — canon ~140cm).** **NO stat is a ceiling, and the frail side is deliberate:** HP **1120** is low-mid (well below the tank band Sukuna/Jason 1240–1250, and miles below Superman 1450); Def **82** is among the LOW real-fighter values (frail ancient Kage — the counterweight to his versatility + mobility); Atk **88** is mid (below the shinobi 89–94, and base Atk does NOT scale damage — flavor); Spd **84** is low-mid. Energy **200** funds the option-set (flight drain + 6 specials + jutsu + the 100-cost ult all draw ONE bar). **His edge is versatility + ranged Dust Release + flight mobility, explicitly NOT durability or burst — the opposite of a stat outlier.**
> - Effective damage (all ×0.60 scaled): normals light ~25 / heavy ~50 EFF; command normal ~42; specials RockFist ~55 / Lunge ~49 / ArmSwing ~56 / TauntFin ~60 (launcher) / Cape-Spin melee ~35 + 2 rock projectiles ~26 ea / Jutsu blast ~57. All sit in the ordinary metered-special band — no single hit approaches the cinematic-ult tier.
> - **Flight (Up/movement, canFly):** reuses the EXISTING shared flight-toggle system verbatim (Omni-Man/Superman precedent — `toggleOmniManFlight`/`_flightActive`, Particle-pool drain). Onoki is the first canFly char with DEDICATED flight art, but the mechanic + drain are unchanged and already-audited. **Utility/mobility, already in-band.**
> - **Rock Platform Ride (air Up+Special, 18 Particle):** a 0-damage instant vertical reposition + brief i-frames. Same accepted 0-dmg mobility/utility CLASS the audit already carries (Kamui Warp / Hashirama's climbable pillar / Ichigo air-dash). **Utility, NOT power.**
> - **⚠️ WATCH-ITEM #1 — the PERSISTENT auto-attacking golem ultimate ("Detachment of the Primitive World", 100 Particle).** The roster's FIRST persistent, auto-striking summon: unlike the one-hit assist shikigami (Meeseeks/Divine Dogs) and the decoy shadow-clones (destroyed in one hit), the golem lives ~10s (600f) and strikes on a ~0.9s (54f) cadence for **~36 EFF per hit**. **Why it is NOT degenerate:** it is the 100-cost ULTIMATE (once per meter fill, and the whole 200 pool competes with flight + every special), each hit is summon-class low (~36 EFF), it is SLOW (speed 3 — the opponent out-paces it, moves around it, and simply isn't struck if they don't sit next to it), it is telegraphed (a lumbering giant), and its damage runs through the same ×0.60 choke-point. **The theoretical if-every-strike-lands ceiling (~11×36 ≈ 400 EFF over the full 10s) requires the opponent to stand next to a slow golem for ten seconds — it will never occur in real play; realistic output is a few connects (~100–180 EFF), which is IN-BAND for an ultimate that produces ONGOING pressure instead of a one-shot nuke.** Flagged because "a summon that keeps hitting" is a new shape for this roster. **Knobs, no fix applied (diagnosis pass), in order:** (a) shorten `duration` 600→~450 if the field-presence overstays; (b) lengthen `attackInterval` 54→~72 if the cadence reads as too frequent; (c) trim per-hit `damage` last. It cannot be stacked (`maxSimultaneous: 1`) and it is not re-castable while the meter refills.
> - **⚠️ WATCH-ITEM #2 — the DUAL-USE charge button (P).** A P-TAP toggles flight; a P-HOLD→release fires the Jutsu blast (~57 EFF). This overloads one button with two actions. **Why it is not a problem:** the tap/hold split is the SAME disambiguation the charge-release system already uses across the roster (Rengoku/Hashirama/Nezuko), the two actions are non-conflicting (a mobility toggle vs a metered projectile), and both are individually cost/So-gated (flight drains the pool; the jutsu spends 40). **Verdict: an input-ergonomics note, not a balance lever.** (Harness-proven: a hold fires the jutsu AND leaves flight state unchanged; a tap toggles flight.)
> - **Verdict: a VERSATILITY schema-exception (Madara/Hashirama class — breadth of options, not power), honestly ×0.60 scaled end-to-end, on a deliberately FRAIL frame with no stat record. The only genuinely new mechanic is the persistent golem, which is ult-gated, slow, low-per-hit, and non-stacking. No fix applied (diagnosis pass); watch the golem's field-presence + total-if-camped ceiling in playtests.**
>
> **Regression:** `test:onoki` canonical 31/0 (full-kit: gate + stats + flight + normals + command + 6 specials + air specials + platform ride + golem ult + 30-action fallback-box sweep); per-stage `test:onoki-stage1..5` all green; Rick/Meeseeks `test:rick` 35/0 (shared `updateSummons` clean); Hashirama 35/0. `test:credits` 14/2 = PRE-EXISTING (`toji`/`pain` unattributed, not Onoki). GAPS by design: no skins batch yet (row_01 palette-swatch reference banked), no voice (zero source clips).

> **Kiba Inuzuka (+ Akamaru, Naruto — 37th sprite char) — added 2026-08-16.** The Inuzuka-clan close-range RUSHDOWN — every tool is point-blank, escalating through a 3-tier beast-fusion ladder (Four Legs → Two-Headed Wolf → Three-Headed Wolf ult) with Akamaru present only inside the merged technique art (never a separate entity — his standalone art is unfinished grayscale, banned from gameplay). **A MEDIUM kit (NOT a largest-class schema exception): 5 normals + 2 bonus directional command-normals (Fwd Strong / Aerial Strong) + Gatsuga (2 tiers, Weak/Strong) + Four Legs (timed self-buff transform) + Two-Headed Wolf (drill rush) + the Three-Headed Wolf freeze-cinematic ultimate.** **ZERO damage-scale bypass — the ENTIRE kit is honestly ×0.60 scaled: normals/strongs/Gatsuga/Two-Headed via `createAttackFromMove`, and the ult's two guaranteed sure-hits via `applyScaledDamage` (no `bypassScale`) → exactly 198 EFF. NO manual-subtract, NO summons-as-damage, NO DOT. NO stat is a roster record. The escalation is the character's identity, not a power spike — each rung is metered by the shared chakra pool.**
>
> - Core: **HP 1180 · Energy 180 chakra · Atk 90 · Def 82 · Spd 92 · mobility high · spriteScale 2.5.** **NO stat is a ceiling.** HP **1180** = the Naruto-tier median (ties Naruto/Sasuke; below the tank band 1240–1300, far below Superman 1450); Def **82** is below-median (the aggressor's tradeoff — he has NO ranged safety, every tool commits him to point-blank); Atk **90** is mid (base Atk does not scale damage); Spd **92** is high (ties Naruto, above the bruisers) but below the very-high 98 teleport tier (Toji/Netero) — a fast rushdown, NOT a speedster. Energy **180** is mid-low, and the WHOLE kit draws it (both Gatsuga tiers + Four Legs + Two-Headed Wolf + the 100-cost ult) → he cannot spam the ladder.
> - Effective damage (all ×0.60 scaled): normals light ~26 / heavy ~49 / up ~40 (launcher) / air ~32 / down_air ~43; Fwd Strong ~47 / Aerial Strong ~42; **Weak Gatsuga ~38 (cost 22) / Strong Gatsuga ~58 (cost 42, superArmor)**; **Two-Headed Wolf ~67 (cost 40, superArmor, tier-2)**; **Three-Headed Wolf ULT 198 EFF (cost 100, two guaranteed sure-hits 200+130 raw → ×0.60)**. Every number sits in its expected band — the ult (198) is squarely the top-ult tier (Saitama 204, Isshiki/Ichigo class), NOT an outlier; no normal/special approaches it.
> - **⚠️ WATCH-ITEM #1 — Four Legs Technique (Down+Special, 28 chakra): a timed SELF-BUFF transform (+25% damage / +20% speed, 480f ≈ 8s).** Uses the existing Vegeta-SSJ multiplier fields (`damageMultiplier`/`speedMultiplier`) as a lightweight *timed* buff (no form/skin swap). **Why it is NOT degenerate:** it is a committed cast (fully locked through the ~34f transform), energy-priced (28 from a mid-low 180 pool that everything else also draws), finite (auto-reverts at 8s, does not refresh for free — a re-cast pays again), and it does NOT stack. **It boosts only the melee/special offense path (via `offenseMult`); it does NOT touch the ultimate — `applyScaledDamage` is buff-agnostic, so the ult stays a flat 198 whether or not Four Legs is active (verified: the buff is read only through `createAttackFromMove`'s offenseMult).** Flagged because a +25%/+20% buff for 28 energy is *cheap-strong* on a high-speed rushdown. **Knobs, no fix applied (diagnosis pass), in order:** (a) raise cost toward ~36 if the uptime reads as too frequent; (b) trim the multiplier to ~1.20/1.15; (c) shorten the 8s duration. It is a real, in-character offense window, not a permanent stat inflation.
> - **⚠️ WATCH-ITEM #2 — the drill-rush pair (Strong Gatsuga + Two-Headed Wolf) both carry superArmor.** Two committed advancing drills that plow through chip (58 / 67 EFF). **Why it is fine:** each is energy-priced (42 / 40), has real startup + long recovery (whiff = heavily punishable), and is a HARD commit (travels a fixed distance, no cancel) — superArmor is the counterweight that makes them usable as approach tools on a defense-82 frame with no projectile. Two armored moves is not unusual (Saitama Serious Punch, Jason Relentless Slash also armor). **Knob: drop superArmor from Strong Gatsuga first if the armored approach reads as too safe — not the damage.**
> - **Verdict: FAIR — an aggressive close-range RUSHDOWN, honestly ×0.60 scaled end-to-end (ult included), with NO stat record and NO scale bypass. This is a MEDIUM kit, NOT a versatility schema-exception (fewer tools than Madara/Onoki/Orochimaru) — the identity is the beast-fusion escalation, not raw options. The two watch-items (cheap-strong Four Legs buff; twin armored drills) are the tuning surface; neither is a stat or damage outlier. No fix applied (diagnosis pass).** GAPS by design: no skins batch yet; no voice (zero source clips); Frog Mode DROPPED (source art is non-Kiba toad-summon creatures — a mislabel, confirmed with the user); `strong_attack_air` off-palette green RECOLORED to his dark outfit (user-approved).
>
> - **Stage-0 investigation resolutions (all 3):** (1) **Four Legs "BLUE BLOCK"** = a solid blue *background wedge* behind the discardable tail frames (uncolored white-wolf line-art + a technique-diagram panel), NOT corruption/color-key — the transform was built from the CLEAN colored frames only (`four_legs_technique_1`+`2`), blue-block frames dropped. (2) **`special_move_5`** = CONFIRMED joke/troll content (burned "URINA/XIXI/PISS", a wrong-character Naruto sprite + "Bomba Fumaça") — EXCLUDED entirely, never wired. (3) **`stance.png` single-frame idle** = held STATIC (no engine breathing-idle exists; consistent with the roster's other single-frame idles).
>
> **Regression:** `test:kiba` canonical **14/0** (static sheet/beat-art/portrait sweep + stats + attribution + full-kit wiring + sprite gate + 22-action fallback-box sweep + live-connect smokes for a normal/Gatsuga/Four-Legs/ULT); per-stage `test:kiba-stage1..5` all green (22/18/12/13/11). LIVE-fighter-performs-the-ult verified explicitly (no duplicate-instance bug). `test:credits` unaffected (kiba newly attributed, project-adapted).

> **Mayuri Kurotsuchi (Bleach — 38th sprite char, 12th-Division captain / mad scientist) — added 2026-08-16.** The eccentric RANGED/DoT TECHNICIAN. **A LARGE kit (Madara/Onoki/Ichigo-class scope exception, confirmed by the build mandate): 5 normals + a Fwd+Heavy 2-stage cancel-on-hit command chain + 5 specials (Finger-Gun Blast projectile / Energy Slash projectile / Rising Cut launcher / Poison Cloud+DoT / Lab Coat Open self-buff) + a Bankai construct freeze-cinematic ultimate + the bespoke NEMU assist (2 moves: rush strike / rising launcher).** **ZERO damage-scale bypass on the repeatable kit — normals, command chain, all 3 projectiles, Rising Cut, and BOTH Nemu-assist strikes run through the ×0.60 choke-point (`createAttackFromMove` / `spawnProjectile` / `applyScaledDamage`); the Bankai ult's guaranteed payoff is `applyScaledDamage` (no `bypassScale`) → exactly 198 EFF. The two UNSCALED bits (the poison DoT; the Lab Coat damage-multiplier) are precedented and bounded. NO stat is a roster record — he is deliberately FRAIL (Def 78 is among the LOWEST real-fighter values). The breadth is VERSATILITY, throttled by a shared 200 Reiatsu pool — NOT raw power.**
>
> - Core: **HP 1080 · Energy 200 reiatsu · Atk 82 · Def 78 · Spd 84 · mobility medium · spriteScale 1.15 (~113px).** **NO stat is a ceiling, and the frail side is deliberate:** HP **1080** is roster-LOW-leaning (below Onoki 1120, far below the tank band Sukuna/Jason 1240–1250 and Superman 1450); Def **78** is among the FRAILEST real-fighter values (the scientist's counterweight — his edge is tools/attrition, not durability); Atk **82** is LOW (below the shinobi 88–94; base Atk does not scale damage — flavor); Spd **84** is low-mid (a deliberate, unhurried technician, NOT a rusher). Energy **200** funds the whole option-set (5 specials + the buff + the 100-cost ult all draw ONE bar; Nemu is cooldown-gated, not metered). **His edge is versatile ranged/poison tooling + a construct payoff, explicitly NOT durability or speed.**
> - Effective damage (repeatable kit, all ×0.60 scaled): normals light ~24 / heavy ~47 / up ~36 (launcher) / air ~31 / down_air ~42; command chain cmd1 ~19 → cmd2 ~32 (launcher) ≈ 51 total; specials Finger-Gun Blast ~34 (projectile) / Energy Slash ~36 (projectile) / Rising Cut ~38 (launcher) / Poison Cloud direct ~20 + **DoT ~50 raw over ~1.8s**; Nemu assist ~31 (attack) / ~34 (uppercut launcher). Every repeatable number sits in the ordinary metered/summon band — no single hit approaches the cinematic-ult tier.
> - **⚠️ WATCH-ITEM #1 — the KIT BREADTH (versatility schema-exception).** More tools than the standard 2–4, same shape as Madara/Onoki/Ichigo. **Why it is not a power outlier:** every tool is scaled/in-band with no stat record, the strongest options (Bankai 198; Poison DoT) are the most gated (100-reiatsu ult; a slow committal projectile), and the **shared 200 Reiatsu pool is the self-limiting throttle** — specials cost 26–40 and the ult 100, so he cannot deploy the whole kit at once (the Madara/Onoki shared-pool finding). **Knob: raise special costs to force selection before touching damage.**
> - **⚠️ WATCH-ITEM #2 — the POISON DoT (Down+Special, Poison Cloud, 28 reiatsu): ~50 raw UNSCALED over ~1.8s.** The one unscaled repeatable payoff. **Why it is fine:** it uses the EXISTING `_dot` subsystem (Shinobu's Wisteria Poison precedent — an accepted, bounded exception), it OVERWRITES rather than stacks (a fresh cast resets, never compounds), it lands only on a CLEAN projectile hit (blocked = no DoT), and the slow spore-cloud is easily avoided. The direct hit itself IS scaled (~20 EFF). **This is the honest attrition side of a frail low-burst technician — the counterweight to Def 78. Knob: trim the tick count (5) or per-tick (10) if the chip reads as too strong.**
> - **⚠️ WATCH-ITEM #3 — Lab Coat Open (Back+Special, 40 reiatsu): a timed SELF-BUFF (+30% damage, 360f ≈ 6s).** Uses the Maki-Power-Charge `currentFormData` clone pattern (no form/skin swap). **Why it is NOT degenerate:** it is a committed cast, energy-priced (40 from the same 200 pool everything draws), finite (auto-reverts at 6s, 10s recast — a re-cast pays again), and it does NOT stack. **It boosts only the melee/special offense path (via `offenseMult`); it does NOT touch the ultimate — `applyScaledDamage` is buff-agnostic, so the Bankai stays a flat 198 whether or not the buff is up (same finding as Kiba's Four Legs).** **Knob: raise cost / trim to +20% if the uptime reads as too frequent.**
> - **THE NEMU-ASSIST / BANKAI "power spike" — specifically assessed (per the build mandate), and NOT an outlier:** (a) **Nemu (Charge+Down / Charge+Up)** is a `oneHit` summon pair on the Pain "Six Paths Summon" architecture — each strike is summon-class low (**~31 / ~34 EFF, scaled**, exactly the Pain-assist band 31–38), it is a 168f (~2.8s) shared cooldown (NOT metered, but not spammable), and it despawns after one hit. It's an extra cooldown-gated poke, not a damage engine. (b) **Bankai: Konjiki Ashisogi Jizō** is a 100-reiatsu cinematic ULTIMATE dealing a guaranteed **198 EFF** — squarely the top-ult tier (Saitama 204 / Kiba 198 / Isshiki-Ichigo class), NOT above it; block cuts it to 25%. It is an inline live-fighter freeze cinematic (Orochimaru pattern — **no duplicate-instance bug**, verified). **Neither is a spike above its class; the "extra" power is breadth (an assist + a summon-flavoured ult), throttled by the meter + cooldown, on a frail frame.**
> - **Verdict: a VERSATILITY schema-exception (Madara/Onoki/Ichigo class — breadth of options, not power), honestly ×0.60 scaled across the whole repeatable kit AND the ult, on a deliberately FRAIL frame (Def 78, HP 1080 — no stat record). The two unscaled bits (poison DoT, Lab Coat buff) are precedented (Shinobu / Maki) and bounded. The Nemu assist + Bankai are in-band for their classes, not spikes. No fix applied (diagnosis pass); watch the poison chip + buff uptime + total metered output in playtests.**
>

TWO mechanic watch-items flagged (the i-frame teleport-strikes; the i-frame Shunpo blink), neither a stat outlier.**
> - Core: **HP 1080 · Energy 200 reiatsu · Atk 92 · Def 86 · Spd 92 · spriteScale 1.1.** All INSIDE existing bands, NO records — **HP 1080 sits LOW** (between Zenitsu 1000 / Yuji 1120; well below Ichigo 1160 / Sasuke·Madara 1180) — the deliberate glass-cannon counterweight; Energy 200 = Ichigo/Goku (below Gojo/Madara 220); Atk 92 ties Ichigo/Rengoku/Minato (below Sukuna 95 / Toji 96; base Atk does NOT scale damage — flavor); Def 86 = Madara (above the shinobi 82–84, below Toji 89 / Superman 92) — mid, NOT a record; **Spd 92 = Madara/Rengoku, upper-mid** (below the teleport-tier 98 Toji/Flash/Maki/Minato) — a fast swordsman, NOT a speed outlier.
> - Normals (RAW → EFF ×0.60): light 40→24 · heavy 76→46 (katana reach rangeX 92) · up 62→37 (launcher) · air 52→31 · down_air 70→42. The crouch-variant reuses the light's data. All mid-band, below the heavy-hitters.
> - Specials (RAW → EFF): Petal Cast 3× 22→~13 each (spread scatter, rarely all connect) · Straight Thrust 66→40 · Re-form Overhead 78→47 · Re-form Thrust 70→42 · Jump Slash 58→35 · Airborne Vault 54→32. Costs 18–34 from the shared 200 pool. Every value scaled and in-band.
> - **Ultimate = Bankai: Senbonzakura Kageyoshi** (2-phase live-fighter freeze cinematic — charge + reiatsu wings → transform → release thrust + blast): **340 RAW → 204 EFF via `applyScaledDamage`** (SCALED, block→25%), cost 100. Squarely the scaled-cinematic-ult band (= Mayuri 198 / Orochimaru 210; the honest side of §"global damage scale") — **not an outlier.** Duplicate-render guard: none needed (no dup instance — the live fighter animates the poses; wings/blast are frame-locked overlays).
> - **⚠️ WATCH-ITEM 1 — UTSUSEMI RE-FORM (i-frame teleport-strike, Fwd/Up).** A single branching special that vanishes (16f i-frames), teleports into striking range of the foe, then hits for ~42–47 EFF. **Why it is NOT degenerate:** (a) **cost 34 each** from the shared 200 pool (can't be thrown repeatedly alongside the rest of the kit); (b) the vanish i-frames END before the strike's active frames, and the teleport-in commits Byakuya to a **punishable recovery on block/whiff** (no super-armor, frail statline); (c) it closes distance but grants no unblockable/knockdown-loop. **Verdict: strong confirm/gap-close pressure, not a power outlier.** **Watch-item, not a fix:** the levers are the **cost** and the **i-frame window** if playtests read the guaranteed-close-with-invuln as too safe.
> - **⚠️ WATCH-ITEM 2 — SHUNPO (i-frame retreat blink, Back).** A 20f-invuln reposition AWAY from the foe, cost 18. **Why it is NOT degenerate:** it deals **zero damage** and only RETREATS (no offensive payoff), carries a real `attackCooldown` recovery tail, and burns the shared pool. It's an evasive-reset tool, not a safe-and-winning state. **Watch-item, not a fix:** the **cost / cooldown** is the knob if it reads as too spammable a panic button.
> - **Overall verdict: fair VERSATILITY + mobility outlier (Madara/Ichigo/Pain/Mayuri class), NOT a power outlier — no per-move nerf needed.** Mirrors the established large-kit findings: (a) every tool is scaled/in-band with no stat records, (b) the strongest options (the teleport-strikes) are the most expensive (34), (c) the **shared 200 Reiatsu pool is the self-limiting throttle**, and (d) unusually for a large kit, he carries a **genuine FRAILTY downside (HP 1080, roster-low-leaning)** rather than the mid/high HP most versatility outliers get — so the breadth is paid for in survivability. The two i-frame mechanics are both self-limited by cost + the frail statline. Filed as a deliberate, internally-consistent outlier — flagged for playtest scrutiny, not fixed.

Idle-variant rows (12/34/36), row_35 (charge windup pose), and single poses 45/48/50 (three of which fill the up/air/down_air normal slots) are accounted for.

> **Boruto — MOMOSHIKI KARMA ULTIMATE FORM + Chakra Absorption (novel mechanic) — added 2026-08-16.** A genuine TRANSFORMATION STATE (SSJ-Rose/Vegeta-SSJ architecture: charge-TAP toggle, ~90% energy GATE, continuous per-frame drain, instant auto-revert at 0) that grants an ongoing NEW ability while active — NOT a one-shot finisher. The form is a magenta-recolor art form-swap (`_skinAnim` → `__karma` sheets) + a procedural blue-Karma-seal/glowing-eye overlay.
> - **FORM BUFFS: dmg ×1.25 / spd ×1.12 / def ×1.08** — one notch above Vegeta SSJ (1.20/1.12/1.05), one notch below the SSJ-Rose ceiling. **In-band with the transformation roster, NOT an outlier:** it is gated behind a ~90%-energy entry (`KARMA_THRESHOLD 162` of 180), bleeds ~0.23 energy/frame net (~9 s from full), and is bought on a FRAIL frame (HP 1120/Def 80). Same shape/limits as every other declarative form.
> - **⚠️ NOVEL MECHANIC (no exact precedent — flagged, as mandated): CHAKRA/ENERGY ABSORPTION.** Karma-only reactive absorb (GRAB in form → `borutoAbsorbAttempt`). Cousins exist (Ben-10 Feedback absorb / Sasuke Absolute Defense per-negate / Rengoku counter) but the combination is new. Contract: **(a) COST 60 HP EVERY attempt** (~5.4% of max HP, floored non-lethal), win or lose — paid up front; **(b) SUCCESS vs a PROJECTILE/energy attack** (projectile-path negate ONLY — combat.`shouldBorutoAbsorb`, reuses the Feedback/Sasuke negate-and-splice pattern) = full negate + energy refund `max(20, round(0.6×dmg))` (≈36 for a 60-dmg shot); **(c) FAILURE vs melee or a whiff** = no negate, no refund, the 60 HP is simply gone. Verified: `test:boruto-karma2` 13/0 covers all four points (unavailable in base form / HP cost every time / projectile success negate+refund+no-dmg / melee NOT absorbed 940→907).
> - **Outlier assessment (novel → scrutinised, not silently shipped):** reads as a FAIR high-risk/high-reward reactive tool, NOT a passive shield, because FOUR limiters stack: (1) Karma-gated (itself energy-gated + draining), (2) projectile/energy-ONLY — a melee opponent shuts it off entirely, (3) 60 HP on EVERY attempt including whiffs (the real risk — it self-damages), (4) one absorb per 16-frame window + committed recovery (no spam). **The one WATCH-ITEM:** the energy refund (~36) can partly self-sustain Karma against a pure-zoner (absorb → refund offsets ~1.5 s of drain), so vs an all-projectile opponent Boruto could over-extend the form while hard-countering their plan. **It is NOT free — each sustain costs 60 HP — and any melee pressure ends it. Knob if it over-performs in playtest: lower the refund (0.6→0.4) or cap refund below the drain-per-window so the form can't be net-sustained by absorbing. No nerf pre-applied (diagnosis pass); flagged for playtest.**
> **Regression:** `test:boruto-karma1` **13/0** (energy gate / art form-swap / buff / drain / revert-on-toggle / auto-revert-at-0) + `test:boruto-karma2` **13/0** (absorb contract). Neighbors clean: `test:vegeta-ssj` 76/0 + `test:vegeta` 72/0 (shared charge-TAP transform handler), `test:feedback` 34/0 + `test:ben10-stage4` 14/0 (projectile-absorb neighbor), `test:goku-black-ssjrose` 14/0 (round-reset revert hook), `test:up-attack-roster` 79/0, `test:boruto` canonical 15/0 (base form unaffected). NEW `tools/gen_boruto_karma.py` (crimson→magenta recolor, 29 `__karma` sheets) + `game.drawBorutoKarmaOverlay` (markings/eyes). Hand-tracked forearm markings are approximate (hand pose not per-frame tracked) — flagged, face markings + eyes are exact.

> **Light Yagami (Death Note — 41st sprite char, "Kira") — added 2026-08-16.** The intellect-driven ZONER/SUMMONER — he does not brawl; his power is a deep toolbox of one-shot call-ins (Ryuk / L Lawliet / an unidentified gunman) and the Death Note itself. **A DELIBERATELY SPECIAL-HEAVY kit shape (flagged, Toji-style, per the build mandate): a LIGHT 3-move normal set (light = B / heavy = B+Forward / a Down+Light crouch-swipe variant — NO up/air/down_air normals) against 7 special-tier call-ins + 2 ultimate-tier finishers.** **ZERO damage-scale bypass — the ENTIRE repeatable kit runs through the ×0.60 choke-point: the 3 normals via `createAttackFromMove`; all 7 summon call-ins via `spawnProjectile` (the summon rides the projectile as its own sprite — the Ghostface phantom-hitbox idiom, NOT a persistent assist entity); and BOTH ultimates' guaranteed payoffs via `applyScaledDamage` (no `bypassScale`, no manual-subtract, no summons-as-persistent-damage, no DoT, no buff). NO stat is a roster record — HP 1080 (roster-low-leaning), Def 82, Spd 88, Atk 86 are all average-or-below (the audit's own "average durability" read). The breadth is VERSATILITY, throttled by a shared 200 Kira pool — NOT raw power.**
> - **⚠️ WATCH-ITEM #1 — the KIT SHAPE (special-heavy / thin normals), flagged exactly as Toji's was.** 7 specials + 2 ults is a lot of options against only 3 normals. **Why it is not a power outlier:** (a) the thin normal set is a GENUINE WEAKNESS, not a hidden strength — with no up/air/down_air normals, Light has NO conventional anti-air or air-to-air outside of spending meter (Ryuk / the air call-ins), so raw up-close pressure and juggles are weak; he is forced to play the zoning game the kit is built for. (b) every call-in is metered (20–30 Kira) and the shared 200 pool is the self-limiting throttle — he cannot deploy the whole kit at once (the Madara/Onoki/Mayuri shared-pool finding). (c) the summons are **one-shot phantom hitboxes that despawn within the move** (short `lifetime`, `spawnProjectile`), NOT persistent assist characters that keep attacking (deliberately avoided per the Stage-0 audit + the duplicate-instance bug). **Knob: raise special costs to force harder selection before touching per-hit damage.**
> - Effective damage (all ×0.60 scaled): normals light/crouch ~25 / heavy ~46; **specials — Y vortex ~34 (cost 24, widest hitbox) / L divekick ~36 (22) / Ryuk anti-air ~40 (30, launcher) / L rising burst ~35 (24, launcher) / violet burst ~30 (20, low) / gunman rocket ~31 (28, long-range projectile) / air punch ~38 (26, highest air)**. Every number sits in its expected special band; the two launchers (Ryuk / L-rising) are the priciest ground tools.
> - **⚠️ WATCH-ITEM #2 — TWO ultimate-tier finishers ("As Planned" writing, neutral U / "I Am Kira" scythe, Down+U).** Unusual to carry two ults. **Why it is not double-power:** both cost the FULL 100 Kira and share ONE meter → they are **mutually exclusive** (firing one empties the pool; you cannot have both ready at once), so it is a *choice* of finisher, not extra output. Both deal an identical guaranteed **204 EFF** (340 raw → ×0.60 via `applyScaledDamage`, block chips to 25%) — squarely the top-ult band (Saitama 204 / Byakuya 204 / Isshiki-Ichigo class), NOT above it. Both are inline live-fighter freeze-cinematics (Isshiki pattern — **no duplicate-instance bug**, verified): the real fighter plays the writing / scythe cast under a camera-focus, the opponent is frozen, the "JUST AS PLANNED" / "THAT'S RIGHT. I'M KIRA." manga cut-in flashes as an overlay. **Neither is a spike above its class; the "extra" ult is breadth (a second flavour of the same 204-EFF finisher), meter-gated to one-at-a-time.**
> - **Verdict: a FAIR special-heavy ZONER, honestly ×0.60 scaled end-to-end (both ults included), with NO stat record and NO scale bypass. The special-heavy shape IS the flagged outlier (Toji-style) — but it is paid for by a genuinely thin normal set (no anti-air/air normals) and throttled by the shared 200 Kira pool; the summons are one-shot phantom hitboxes, not persistent assists; the two ults are mutually-exclusive same-band finishers, not stacked power. No fix applied (diagnosis pass); watch the total-if-all-specials-land zoning ceiling + whether the thin normals leave him too helpless up-close in playtests.**
> - **AUTHORED / REPURPOSED per the Stage-0 audit + user decisions:** NO deck/support-panel system exists in this engine (JUS `koma` = a rekka-chain term here), so the koma sheets were repurposed as CONTENT — `koma_special_attacks` → the scythe 2nd-ult, `koma.png`'s "JUST AS PLANNED" panel → the writing-ult cut-in. Ryuk + L credited by name; the `jump_b` gunman is an UNIDENTIFIED placeholder. Ryuk takes the Up-special slot (its anti-air role) so Y+Up's rising L-burst moved to Back. The 124px "composite" ultimate frame was confirmed to be 4 touching writing poses (not a single panel) → equal-column split into a writing animation.
> **Regression:** `test:light` canonical **12/0** (sprite gate + stats + real face portrait + win/lose keys + an 18-key full-kit fallback-box sweep + a special-spawns-summon check + an ultimate-lands-204 check); per-stage `test:light-stage1/2/4/56` all green (21 / 12 / 24 / 14).
