> **Yamamoto Genryūsai Shigekuni (Bleach — Captain-Commander of the Gotei 13) — added 2026-08-17.** The ancient, nearly-unkillable technique-master: a LARGE Ryūjin Jakka fire kit fronted by top-tier DURABILITY and paid for by the roster's near-slowest movement. **A LARGE kit (the Madara/Onoki/Saitama/Byakuya VERSATILITY schema-exception class): 5 normals + a Fwd+Heavy multi-hit slash command-normal + 5 directional specials (neutral Ground-Sweep Beam / Fwd Large Ground-Stab / Down Ground Eruption Stab / Up Overhead Slam / Back Horizontal Thrust) + a Shunpo teleport-behind movement special + the Overhead-Slam ULTIMATE.** **ZERO damage-scale bypass — the ENTIRE kit runs through the ×0.60 choke-point: the 5 normals + the `yamamotoCombo` multi-hit command via `createAttackFromMove`; all 4 melee specials (Stab/Eruption/Overhead/Thrust) via `createAttackFromMove`; the Ground-Sweep Beam via `spawnProjectile` (a real travelling fire-beam projectile with genuine range, not a melee hitbox); and the Ultimate's guaranteed payoff via `applyScaledDamage` (no `bypassScale`, no manual-subtract, no persistent summon, no DoT, no buff). The breadth is VERSATILITY, throttled by the shared 200 Reiatsu pool — NOT raw power.**
> - **RAW→EFF spot-checks (all ×0.60, harness-measured):** light 44→26, heavy 88→52, up-launcher 70→42, air 56→33, down_air 78→46; command-chain `yamamotoCombo` ~78 raw over ~3 hits →**~47 EFF**; **specials — Beam 72→43 (cost 28, ranged) / Large Ground-Stab 110→66 (50, FLAGSHIP) / Eruption 90→54 (34, launcher) / Overhead 96→58 (32) / Thrust 88→52 (30, long active window)**; Shunpo 0-dmg (18 Reiatsu); **Ultimate 340 raw→204 EFF** (block chips to 25%). Special costs 28–50 + ult 100 all draw the shared 200 pool → the whole kit cannot deploy at once (the Madara/Onoki/Mayuri shared-pool throttle).
> - **⚠️ WATCH-ITEM #1 — the DURABILITY STACK (the defining outlier, flagged).** **HP 1300 × Def 92 is the tankiest pairing on the roster** — HP 1300 TIES the Isshiki ceiling (above Saitama 1280 / Jason 1250, below the Superman 1450 / Omni-Man 1400 giants); Def 92 TIES the non-giant ceiling (Hashirama/Madara/Superman/Saitama). Neither is a NEW single-axis record, but the *combination* is the strongest survivability on the roster. **Why it is FAIR, not a spike:** it is the character's canonical identity (the Captain-Commander is in-universe near-unkillable) and it is bought with a genuine, stacked counterweight — **Spd 74 = the 2nd-slowest real fighter** (only Jason 72 is slower; the 88–98 rushdown band is far above), plus a low jump (27) / slow dash (dashSpeed 12, cooldown 48) and a deliberately committal, slow-startup kit (heavy 10f, specials 9–12f startup). He is a wall that cannot chase — hard to kill, easy to keep at range and out-maneuver. **Knob if playtests read him as too safe: trim HP toward ~1240 (still tank-band) or lengthen the slow-startup recoveries — NOT per-hit damage (already ×0.60 and in-band).**
> - **⚠️ WATCH-ITEM #2 — KIT BREADTH + the flagship Stab.** A large tool-for-every-range kit (beam zones, Stab/Eruption/Overhead cover the close/AOE/anti-air angles, Thrust spaces, Shunpo repositions). **Why not a power outlier:** every tool is ×0.60-scaled and in its expected band (the strongest, Large Ground-Stab at 66 EFF, is also the most expensive at 50 Reiatsu and the slowest to start), and the shared 200 pool is the self-limiting throttle. **Knob: raise special costs to force selection before touching damage.**
> - **⚠️ NOTE — Shunpo is NOT a free escape.** He rides the shared teleport-behind (`dashTeleport`) mechanic used by Toji/Gojo/Sukuna/Sasuke — but UNLIKE those (free, cooldown-only), Yamamoto's Shunpo costs **18 Reiatsu** (falls through to a normal dash with no meter) + i-frames + the 48f dash-teleport cooldown. This is deliberate: a free burst-teleport would erase his Spd-74 counterweight, so his mobility competes with his kit for the pool. Not a mobility outlier — a resourced Flash Step on a slow frame.
> - **Verdict: a FAIR schema-exception VERSATILITY TANK — durability is the honest identity (canon Captain-Commander), honestly ×0.60-scaled end-to-end INCLUDING the ult, and paid for by the 2nd-slowest movement + a committal slow-startup kit + a resourced (not free) Shunpo. The HP1300×Def92 stack is the one genuine outlier (tankiest on the roster) and is flagged as WATCH-ITEM #1 with HP as the tuning knob; no single offensive axis is a record and there is no scale bypass. The Ultimate is a live-fighter inline freeze-cinematic (no duplicate-instance bug, verified explicitly) in the ~204 EFF top-ult band. No fix applied (diagnosis pass); watch the durability-vs-safety read + total-if-all-specials-land ceiling in playtests.**
> - **STAGE-0 CLEANUP (JPEG-damage) HELD:** the source was confirmed JPEG-damaged (3,000–13,000 unique RGB per row where a DS rip = a few dozen); `tools/prep_yamamoto.py` (despeckle + two-population median-cut quantize, body 24 / fire 10, per row) collapsed every row to ≤34 opaque colors. The canonical suite's compression-artifact-residue check confirms **every shipped sheet is ≤128 opaque colors** (composited body+prop+fire palettes; peak 81 on the 3-row dash composite) — a 25–100× reduction from raw, proving no artifact residue survived into the final assets.
> - **AUTHORED / repurposed per the confirmed design:** the 3-layer source (Body + detached Prop/cane + Fire) has NO stored offsets → the reslicer composites the paired rows at NATIVE coords (they share the source grid) so the cane/fire land correctly with no per-frame math. Fire crescents (specials/up/air/chain/ult) are the source's own art composited onto the body. The 8 palette-header costume variants (rows 1–3) are BANKED (`yamamoto_skin_bank/`, `YAMAMOTO_SKIN_BANK.md`) for a future skins batch, not built. Turnaround (row 07) is banked art (engine has no turn state; facing = flip).
> **Regression:** `test:yamamoto` canonical **17/0** (gate + stats + palette-header portrait + 25-action full-kit fallback-box sweep + light/beam-projectile/ultimate live connects + the Stage-0 compression-artifact-residue check + full-kit data contract); per-stage `test:yamamoto-stage1..6` all green (17/17/15/19/11/9). LIVE-fighter-performs-the-ult verified explicitly (no duplicate-instance bug). Neighbors clean: `test:mayuri` 32/0 + `test:onoki` 31/0 (shared special-dispatch / triggerUltimate-switch / cinematic-render paths), `test:toji` 61/0 (shared dashTeleport path). GAPS by design: no skins batch yet (8 costumes banked for a Stage-7 follow-up); no voice (zero source clips); no walk-vs-run distinct art (run reuses walk); no dedicated jump art (safe idle fallback). Source artist unknown → project-adapted credits bucket (move to SOURCED_ART if one surfaces).

> **Six Paths of Pain (Naruto — multi-identity SWAP character, SEPARATE from the solo `pain`) — added 2026-08-17.** The signature technique made mechanical: Nagato's real body CYCLES THE SIX PATHS mid-match, each a full, distinct kit on its own body art. **A NEW schema-exception AXIS — not one large kit (Madara/Onoki class) but SIX separate kits swapped between via `_skinAnim` (the Vegeta form-swap primitive):** Deva (gravity — Almighty Push / Pull / Rinnegan-defense barrier + the Six-Paths-summon Ultimate) · Animal/Chikushodo (4-creature Kuchiyose menagerie — dog/hawk/rhino/toad) · Preta/Gakido (chakra-absorption shield: eats projectiles + refunds chakra) · Human/Ningendo (Soul-Rip command grab) · Asura/Shurado (Missile/Rocket/Super-Missile artillery) · Naraka/Jigokudo (King of Hell — Judgment strike + Restoration heal). Each Path = 5 normals + movement/state + its own specials; the lean source Paths (Preta/Human, 11 files each) ship LEAN (one special), the rich ones (Asura/Naraka) ship rich. **ZERO stat outliers; the ENTIRE damage kit is ×0.60-scaled (`applyScaledDamage`/`spawnProjectile`/`spawnAssistSummon`); ONE scaled sure-hit Ultimate (~180 EFF, in-band). The power is FLEXIBILITY across six kits — throttled by the SWAP ECONOMY (a real chakra cost + cooldown) — NOT raw per-move power. Deliberately FRAIL-leaning (no offensive/defensive stat is a roster record). THREE watch-items flagged.**
> - **Stats:** HP 1120 (mid, below Naruto-tier 1180) / Atk 88 (moderate) / Def 84 (low-mid — a channeled body, not armored) / Spd 86 (moderate) / maxEnergy 100 (shared chakra pool: specials + swaps + Ultimate all draw it). No axis is a record; three are below the median — the deliberate counterweight to the six-kit breadth.
> - **RAW→EFF spot-checks (all ×0.60):** Deva — Push 0-dmg global shove (28) / Pull 0-dmg global reel (30) / Rinnegan-defense 0-dmg i-frame barrier (24); **Ultimate "Six Paths of Pain" 300 raw→~180 EFF** (block chips to 30%). Animal — summons dog 66→40 / hawk 58→35 / rhino 74→44 / toad 68→41 raw→EFF (cost 34 + a 60f per-summon cooldown ON TOP of the swap gate). Preta — shield 0-dmg (26) but ABSORBS projectiles + refunds 16 chakra/absorb. Human — Soul Rip 130→**78 EFF** (40, block-beating grab). Asura — Missile 60→36 (24) / Rocket 72→43 (30) / Super Missile 94→**56** (42). Naraka — Judgment 88→**53** (36, blockable, reach 240) / Restoration **+120 HP** heal (50).
> - **⚠️ WATCH-ITEM #1 — the SWAP ECONOMY is the whole balance answer to "six kits on one body."** Free mid-match Path access would be degenerate (an answer to every situation, all at once). **The throttle: each swap costs 20 chakra AND arms a 90f (~1.5s) cooldown** — so you cannot fluidly hold all six, and committing to a Path is a real chakra + tempo cost (the canonical suite surfaced this: a bar drained by the Ultimate literally CANNOT swap until it regens). Same-Path re-select is a free no-op; below-cost is blocked. **Why not degenerate:** you play ONE Path at a time for ≥1.5s, each swap competes with that Path's own specials for the same 100 pool, and no single Path is individually overtuned. **Knob if playtests read the flexibility as too fluid: raise the swap cost (20→30) or the cooldown (90→120f) BEFORE touching any per-move damage.**
> - **⚠️ WATCH-ITEM #2 — Naraka Restoration is the roster's rare self-HEAL (+120 HP).** Deliberately the Naraka Path's UNIQUE home — the Human Path's soul-rip was built with NO heal specifically so restoration is not duplicated. **Why not a spike:** cost 50 (half the bar), gated behind being ON the Naraka Path (a swap tempo commitment), and it heals nothing if you are already topped. **Knob: heal amount (120→90) or cost (50→60) if it reads as too sustaining; it is the single most watch-worthy number in the kit.**
> - **⚠️ WATCH-ITEM #3 — the Human Soul-Rip is a BLOCK-BEATING command grab (78 EFF).** It ignores guard (a grab), which is strong — but it WHIFFS out of range AND on any airborne foe, so jump/spacing is real counterplay, and it costs 40. **Knob: reach (116) or cost if it reads as too safe a block-opener.**
> - **Verdict: a FAIR schema-exception FLEXIBILITY character on a NEW axis (six swappable kits, not one large kit), not a power outlier — no per-move damage nerf.** Every tool is ×0.60-scaled and in-band with no stat records; the character is frail-leaning; the Ultimate is a scaled sure-hit in the ~180 EFF band; and the six-kit breadth is self-limited by the swap chakra-cost + cooldown (the primary tuning surface, WATCH-ITEM #1). Two mechanic watch-items (the Naraka heal, the Human grab) are flagged with their knobs. **NO roster stat record; NO scale bypass; NO fix applied (diagnosis pass).** FULLY SEPARATE from the solo `pain` char (own `_path`/`_sp*` namespace + own `sixpaths_*` art — proven by the canonical isolation section). Full per-Path build detail in memory `[[six-paths-pain-build]]`.
> - **Regression:** `test:six-paths` canonical **36/0** (boot-to-sprites + the 6-Path swap matrix + a 66-check fallback-box sweep [11 actions × 6 Paths, zero boxes] + every Path's signature kit connecting + the zero-shared-state-vs-solo-`pain` isolation section); per-stage `test:six-paths-s1..s5` all green (31/26/22/20/36). Neighbor clean: `test:pain` (solo) **42/0** (shares the special-dispatch / triggerUltimate-switch / `activeProjectiles` paths — no coupling). `test:credits` includes the mandatory `six_paths_pain` attribution entry (DasonPikXD + contributors, transcribed from the master sheet's credit block). GAPS by design: no skins batch; no voice; per-Path portrait busts are prepared assets (`sixpaths_<path>_portrait.png`) but char-select uses the Deva base bust (no in-match portrait hook exists — the live body-swap IS the per-Path identity signal).

> **L "Ryuuzaki" (Death Note — L Lawliet, the world's greatest detective) — added 2026-08-17.** The frail capoeira-kicking ZONER/TECHNICIAN — reach, options and call-ins over raw brawling. **A SCHEMA-EXCEPTION VERSATILITY kit (the Light/Madara/Isshiki/Saitama class — breadth of options, NOT raw power), and in-band with his sibling `light` in the SAME `deathnote` universe: 5 normals + a 3-stage capoeira Fwd+Heavy command-normal rekka (cmd1 Low Sweep → cmd2 Rising Crescent → cmd3 Aerial-Dive launcher) + 5 directional specials (neutral Golden Nova / Fwd Bazooka projectile / Back Golden Rising Burst launcher / Down Investigation self-buff / Up Ryuk cameo-attack) + a kick_trail EX multi-hit flurry (cancel-ONLY off a normal/the chain, never from neutral — Vegeta EX idiom).** **NO ULTIMATE — an HONEST art-gap ceiling (Jason precedent): the biggest FX in the source (`row_19` golden-nova) ships as the neutral Nova SPECIAL, deliberately NOT promoted to a cinematic ult; the `ultimate` field is a HUD placeholder (`placeholder:true`), wired to NO `triggerUltimate` path.** **ZERO damage-scale bypass — the ENTIRE damage kit runs through the ×0.60 choke-point: the 5 normals + the 3 command stages via `createAttackFromMove`; all 4 offensive call-ins (Nova / Bazooka / Rising / Ryuk) via `spawnProjectile` (the FX/summon rides the projectile as its own sprite — the Ghostface/Light phantom-hitbox idiom, NOT a persistent assist entity); and the EX flurry via `createAttackFromMove`. NO `bypassScale`, NO manual-subtract, NO persistent summon, NO DoT. The one non-damage tool (Investigation) is a bounded self-buff. NO stat is a roster record — he is deliberately FRAIL. The breadth is VERSATILITY, throttled by a shared 200 Deduction pool — NOT raw power.**
> - Core: **HP 1040 · Energy 200 deduction · Atk 84 · Def 80 · Spd 90 · mobility medium · spriteScale 2.0 (~100px).** **NO stat is a ceiling, and the frail side is deliberate:** HP **1040** is BELOW-MEDIAN / roster-low-leaning (under sibling Light 1080, well under the tank band Jason/Sukuna 1240–1250 and the giants); Def **80** is low-leaning (the counterweight — his edge is reach/options, not durability); Atk **84** and Spd **90** are average (base Atk does not scale damage — flavor). Energy **200** funds the whole option-set (5 specials + the buff all draw ONE bar) — the self-limiting throttle.
> - **RAW→EFF spot-checks (all ×0.60, harness-measured):** normals light 40→~24, heavy 72→~43, up-launcher 66→~39 (launchVy −30), air 58→~34, down_air 62→~37; **command rekka cmd1 40 / cmd2 34 / cmd3 42 raw → ~67 EFF over the landed string** (cmd3 launches, launchVy −30); **specials — Nova 64→~38 (cost 32, widest hitbox, SHORT range) / Bazooka 52→~31 (28, long-range travel) / Rising 56→~33 (24, LAUNCHER vy<0) / Ryuk 66→~39 (30, anti-air LAUNCHER, one-shot phantom, non-persistent) / Investigation 0-dmg self-buff (22)**; **EX kick_trail ~72 raw multi-hit → ~84 EFF measured** (cost 30, cancel-only flurry finisher). Special costs 22–32 all draw the shared 200 pool → the whole kit cannot deploy at once (the Madara/Onoki/Mayuri/Light shared-pool throttle).
> - **⚠️ WATCH-ITEM #1 — the KIT BREADTH (versatility schema-exception).** More tools than the standard 2–4, same shape as Light/Madara/Isshiki. **Why it is not a power outlier:** every tool is ×0.60-scaled and in-band with no stat record; the strongest single hits (Nova/Ryuk ~38–39 EFF) are metered (30–32 Deduction); and the **shared 200 Deduction pool is the self-limiting throttle** (specials 22–32 apiece — he cannot fire the whole kit at once). Unusually for a versatility char he ALSO carries a genuine FRAILTY downside (HP 1040 roster-low-leaning, Def 80) — the breadth is paid for in survivability. **Knob: raise special costs to force selection before touching per-hit damage.**
> - **⚠️ WATCH-ITEM #2 — the INVESTIGATION self-buff (Down special), FLAGGED for tuning.** A NON-LETHAL timed damage bump: **×1.20 for 4 s, 8 s recast** (cost 22). Deals ZERO damage; auto-reverts (`revertLRyuuzakiAnalysis` via `updateMiscTimers`). **Why it is modest:** the multiplier is conservative (≤1.25 cap), the uptime is only 4 s of every 8 s (a hard 480f recast → not spammable, not permanent), and it costs Deduction that then can't fund the call-ins. It is a single-point-tunable knob (`L_ANALYSIS_MULT` / `L_ANALYSIS_DUR` / `L_ANALYSIS_CD` — all named constants). **Knob: drop the multiplier to 1.15 or lengthen the recast if buff uptime reads as too strong.**
> - **⚠️ WATCH-ITEM #3 — Ryuk is a NON-PERSISTENT phantom hitbox, NOT an oppressive assist.** Reuses Light's `LIGHT_SUMMONS.ryuk` mechanics verbatim in shape (cost 30, one-shot `spawnProjectile`, `launcher`, `life 28`) pointed at L's OWN Ryuk art (`l_ryuuzaki_ryuk_uniform`, verified NOT `light_ryuk`). It swoops in for ONE anti-air launcher hit and despawns within the move — it does NOT stay on the field attacking (the deliberate anti-pattern to the persistent-summon oppression + the duplicate-instance bug). An extra metered anti-air, not a damage engine.
> - **⚠️ WATCH-ITEM #4 — command-rekka → EX cancel combo damage.** The max confirmed route is the landed 3-stage capoeira chain (~67 EFF) canceled into the kick_trail EX (~84 EFF) ≈ **~150 EFF for ~30 Deduction + the string commitment.** **Why it is in-band:** it is a single-resource, hard-committal string (whiff/block = heavily punishable — no super-armor, real startup/recovery), the EX is the meter cost, and ~150 for a full landed BnB on a frail 1040-HP frame is squarely normal (below any ult tier — and he HAS no ult). Not out of band; the shared pool + frailty are the counterweights.
> - **Verdict: a FAIR schema-exception VERSATILITY zoner/technician (Light/Madara/Isshiki class — breadth of options, not power), honestly ×0.60-scaled end-to-end, on a deliberately FRAIL frame (HP 1040 roster-low-leaning, Def 80 — no stat record). Unusually he carries NO ULTIMATE at all — an honest content ceiling, not a hidden weakness disguised (his marquee golden-nova is a 38-EFF special). The four watch-items are all self-limited (shared meter / bounded buff / non-persistent Ryuk / committal BnB) and single-point-tunable; none is a stat or damage outlier. No fix applied (diagnosis pass); watch the metered zoning + buff uptime + total-if-all-lands ceiling in playtests.**
> - **Regression:** `test:l_ryuuzaki` canonical **34/0** (STATIC sheet+FX+portrait-on-disk sweep + identity gate [rosterKey/universe/energyType/stats/scale/portrait/NO-ult] + a 26-action fallback-box sweep [every animationData action → real sheet, zero boxes] + 5 normals connecting [up launches] + the 3-stage rekka [fires/advances/cancels] + all 5 specials firing per-direction & spending Deduction [Rising/Ryuk launch, Analysis = non-lethal buff] + the cancel-only EX). Per-stage `test:l_ryuuzaki-stage1..5` all green (27/16/15/26/21). > **Baki Hanma (Baki the Grappler — new `baki` universe) — added 2026-08-17.** The PURE hand-to-hand martial artist: a grounded rushdown grappler who fights entirely on physical striking — **NO ki, NO chakra, NO supernatural technique** (the thematic gate applied to the source; the sheet's Goku-homage + green energy-sword bonus frames were EXCLUDED as non-canonical). **Built to FUNCTION like Toji/Maki — meterless (`maxEnergy 0`, `hideResourceMeter`, HP-only HUD), but COOLDOWN-gated (the Zenitsu/Rengoku currency of real recast windows, explicitly NOT Toji's flagged pays-nothing free kit).** Kit shape is NORMAL-sized (not a versatility schema-exception): 5 normals + a Fwd+Heavy 2-stage "Combination" rekka (bakiG1 body-jab → bakiG2 rising launcher, cancel-on-hit) + 5 directional specials (neutral Mach-Punch Barrage multi-hit / Fwd Rushing Combination gap-closer / Up Rising Rush DP-launcher with startup i-frames / Down Impact Shockwave wide air-pressure AOE / Back Defensive Read counter+riposte) + the Demon Back (Oni no Se) ULTIMATE.
> - **Core: HP 1160 · Energy 0 · Atk 94 · Def 88 · Spd 96 · mobility very_high (but spd 96 < the 98 teleport-blur gate → GROUNDED run-based approach, unlike Toji's blink-dash).** Every value IN-BAND, **no roster record**: HP between Tobirama 1120 / Maki 1180; Atk 94 below the 98 ceiling (Netero/Omni-Man) and below Toji 96 / Sukuna 95; Def 88 upper (below Superman 92 / Toji 89); Spd 96 ties the Tobirama/Zenitsu tier.
> - **ZERO damage-scale bypass — the ENTIRE kit runs through the ×0.60 choke-point:** the 5 normals + both `bakiG1/G2` command stages + all 4 offensive specials (Barrage / Rush / Rising / Shockwave) via `createAttackFromMove`; the Defensive Read riposte via `applyScaledDamage` (`shouldBakiCounter`, twin of Rengoku/Nezuko). NO `spawnProjectile`, NO summons, NO manual-subtract, NO DoT. **On the honest side of the §damage-scale finding** (like Toji).
> - **The Ultimate is a BUFF, not a bypass.** Demon Back = a timed empowered FORM (Maki Power-Charge architecture at ult-tier): dmg ×1.30 / spd ×1.12 for ~12s then auto-reverts, gated by the universal `ultimateCooldown`. ×1.30 sits in the modest transform band (Maki Shibuya / Toji Reincarnated ×1.25). The lever is duration + the ult-cooldown, not the multiplier — and it applies to the ALREADY-scaled pipeline (no raw damage).
> - **⚠️ WATCH-ITEM #1 — Mach-Punch Barrage multi-hit total.** One long active window with 6 `hasHit` re-arms (~7 hits × 12 raw). Verified it runs every hit through the scaled pipeline + respects combo hitstun decay → total ≈29–40 EFF (a pinning pressure tool, NOT a true-blockstring infinite). Knob if playtests read it as too safe: fewer re-arms / longer `_bakiBarrageCd` (currently 120f/2s).
> - **⚠️ WATCH-ITEM #2 — Rushing Combination super-armor.** The Fwd gap-closer carries `superArmor` (set on the attack directly — the Jason `createAttackFromMove`-drops-it gotcha) so he powers through a poke to close distance. It is a committal 96f-cd move (whiff = punishable recovery); if it reads as too oppressive, scope the armor to the dash startup only (not the active) before touching damage.
> - **⚠️ WATCH-ITEM #3 — Demon Back uptime.** ~12s window on the universal ult-cooldown. In-band by the modest ×1.30, but the uptime is the tuning surface — shorten the window before touching the multiplier.
> - **Verdict: a FAIR grounded rushdown grappler, peer to Toji/Maki/Rengoku — meterless-but-cooldown-gated (the constrained currency), honestly ×0.60-scaled end-to-end, no stat record, no damage bypass.** The three watch-items are all single-point-tunable (barrage re-arms / rush-armor scope / Demon-Back uptime); none is a stat or damage outlier. No fix applied (diagnosis pass).
> - **Regression:** `test:baki` canonical **32/0** (sprite gate [idle → repacked `baki_idle_uniform`, flips box→sprite via the `spritesheets.js` registry entry] + HP-only HUD + grounded-tier check + 5 normals connecting [up + Rising launch] + the Fwd+Heavy Combination rekka [opens/continues/whiff-interrupt] + all 5 specials firing per-direction [Barrage multi-hits, Rush lunges+connects, Rising launches, Shockwave connects wide, Defensive Read NEGATES + RIPOSTES] + Demon Back [casts / flex pose / dmg ×1.30 / re-cast no-op while active] + stat sanity). Neighbors clean: `test:toji` **61/0** + `test:maki` **30/0** (shared meterless command-rekka + special-dispatch), `test:rengoku` **41/0** (shared counter-riposte path in `resolveAttackHit`). `test:nezuko` 20/2 = PRE-EXISTING (`noMeterFlavor` label + `nezuko_block` guard-art gap — neither touches the counter path; Nezuko's own riposte test passes). > **Kurapika (Hunter x Hunter — `hunter_x_hunter`) — added 2026-08-17.** The Kurta-clan Nen **Conjurer chain technician/zoner**: a NORMAL-sized kit (not a versatility schema-exception) = 5 normals + a Fwd+Heavy windmill command-normal + 4 directional specials (3 canon-named chains + a status move) + the Emperor Time TRANSFORMATION ultimate. Canon grounding is faithful (Judgment Chain / Chain Jail / Steal Chain / Emperor Time), with two flagged, honest simplifications: the Phantom-Troupe Nen-restriction is DROPPED (impractical in a general roster), and Holy/Dowsing Chain is OMITTED (a lie-detector, not a fighting move). His edge is chain REACH + a bind + a counter, not raw HP.
> - **Core: HP 1080 · Energy 200 · Atk 88 · Def 84 · Spd 92 · mobility high.** Every value IN-BAND, **no roster record**: HP 1080 ties Byakuya/Mayuri (technician tier, below the bruisers); Atk 88 mid; Def 84 average-frail (the versatility/zoner counterweight); Spd 92 agile (below the 96+ rushdown tier, above the slow bruisers). energyType `nen` (label already shipped).
> - **ZERO damage-scale bypass — the WHOLE repeatable kit runs through the ×0.60 choke-point:** the 5 normals + the `kurapikaWindmill` multi-hit command-normal + all offensive specials (Judgment / Chain Jail / Shock) via `createAttackFromMove`; the Steal Chain riposte via `applyScaledDamage` (`shouldKurapikaCounter`, twin of Rengoku/Nezuko/Baki). The Chain Jail bind-column is a **0-damage visual projectile** (no hit). NO summons, NO manual-subtract, NO DoT. **On the honest side of the §damage-scale finding** (like Toji/Baki).
> - **The Ultimate is a BUFF WITH A REAL DOWNSIDE, not a bypass.** Emperor Time = a timed scarlet-eyed transformation (Boruto-Karma / Baki-Demon-Back architecture): dmg ×1.30 / spd ×1.12 / def ×1.10 for ~10 s then auto-reverts, cost 100 Nen. ×1.30 sits in the modest transform band (Baki ×1.30 / Maki ×1.25). It applies to the ALREADY-scaled pipeline (no raw damage). **Uniquely, it carries a CANON-ACCURATE self-cost most buff-ults lack:** a post-revert VULNERABILITY window (`_emperorRevertVuln` ~1.5 s → ×1.25 damage TAKEN, `combat.js`) — the manga's memory-gap disorientation, tied to a real mechanic. The lever is the duration + the vulnerability window, not the multiplier.
> - **⚠️ WATCH-ITEM #1 — Judgment Chain, the flagship.** Long-reach (rangeX 162, the extending chain) MULTI-HIT (3 `hasHit` re-arms × 32 raw ≈ 96 raw → ~58 EFF) for cost 40 — his highest-damage, longest-reach special. In-band vs Boruto Shiden (74 single) given the 40-cost + long startup/recovery commitment + it's the only true zoning tool. Knob: fewer re-arms / higher cost before per-hit damage.
> - **⚠️ WATCH-ITEM #2 — Chain Jail, the BIND.** Low damage (20) but a HUGE 55f hitstun = a hard disable (cost 30, short reach). The value is the restrain, not the damage. Verified it does NOT enable a guaranteed unscaled loop (the bind is committal — short reach, real recovery; a whiff is punishable). Knob: shorten the hitstun or lengthen recovery if it reads as a guaranteed full-combo starter in playtests.
> - **⚠️ WATCH-ITEM #3 — Steal Chain's Nen refund (self-sustain).** A successful counter refunds 30 Nen (the "steal" — a partial nod to the canon copy-mechanic). Same family as the Boruto-Karma absorb-refund watch-item, but MORE constrained: it is REACTIVE (needs a correct read of an incoming melee hit), CD-gated (104f), one riposte per window, and the refunded Nen only funds the already-metered kit. Not a free-energy engine. Knob: drop the refund to 20 or lengthen the CD if counter-into-Judgment loops read as too self-sustaining.
> - **⚠️ WATCH-ITEM #4 — Emperor Time uptime.** ~10 s on the universal ult-cooldown. In-band by the modest ×1.30 AND — unusually — self-balanced by the post-revert vulnerability tax. The uptime + vuln window are the tuning surface before the multiplier.
> - **Verdict: a FAIR Conjurer zoner/technician (chain reach + a bind + a counter), honestly ×0.60-scaled end-to-end, on an average-frail frame (HP 1080 / Def 84 — no stat record). Emperor Time is a modest ×1.30 BUFF that — uniquely — pays a canon self-cost (post-revert vulnerability), so it is a buff-not-spike WITH a downside. All four watch-items are self-limited (committal flagship / punishable bind / reactive-CD-gated refund / vuln-taxed uptime) and single-point-tunable; none is a stat or damage outlier. No fix applied (diagnosis pass).**
> - **Regression:** `test:kurapika` canonical **25/0** (STATIC sheet+Emperor-Time-art+portrait+jailfx on-disk sweep + gate/stats/`Nen`-label/`hunter_x_hunter` + movement + 5 normals connecting [up launches] + the Fwd+Heavy windmill command-normal [fires/multi-hits] + all 3 Nen specials [Judgment multi-hits at range / Chain Jail BINDS peak-hitstun 63 / Steal Chain NEGATES+RIPOSTES+STEALS Nen] + Shock status strike + Emperor Time [buff ×1.30 / whole-moveset __emperor swap / auto-revert + post-revert vuln] + a 21-action fallback-box sweep). Per-stage `test:kurapika-stage1..5` all green (21/15/13/8/13). Neighbors clean — shared counter-riposte path (`resolveAttackHit`): `test:nezuko-stage5` **12/0**, `test:baki` **32/0**, `test:rengoku` **41/0**; shared transformation loop: `test:boruto` **15/0**. `test:chrollo` 15/5 = **PRE-EXISTING** (confirmed by re-running with the Kurapika diff `git stash`ed — identical 15/5 on HEAD; the fails are chrollo's own `guard`-art gap + Skill-Hunter `distinct`-counter + cancel-on-hit timing, none on any path Kurapika touches). GAPS by design: `down_air` + jump/fall reuse the leap-slash art (no dedicated art — flagged honest gaps); rows 10-11 (UI gauge demos) unused; no skins batch (Emperor-Time scarlet variant is a natural future skin); no voice (separate future follow-ups).

> **Spider-Man (Marvel Super Heroes, Capcom CPS2 arcade — FIRST `marvel` universe char, sprite rip by Alvin-Earthworm) — added 2026-08-17.** The acrobatic, EVASIVE web-technician: a mobility/reach fighter whose edge is speed + agility + web-tool options, NOT raw power or durability. **A NORMAL-to-moderate, THEMATICALLY-FOCUSED kit (NOT a Madara/Onoki versatility schema-exception — every tool is web or agility, no "answer to everything" breadth): 5 normals + a Fwd+Heavy "Jump Attack Combo" multi-hit command-normal + a Down+Special Ground Crawl evasive (i-frames + a kick-up exit launcher) + 5 directional specials (neutral Web Impact / Fwd Web Throw / Back Dash Attack / Up Handstand Flip Kick / Down Ground Crawl) + a Web-Throw combo-cancel bridge + the Maximum Web cinematic ULTIMATE.** **ZERO damage-scale bypass — the ENTIRE damage kit runs through the ×0.60 choke-point:** the 5 normals + the `spiderCombo` multi-hit command + the crawl kick-up + both melee specials (Dash Attack / Handstand) via `createAttackFromMove`; both web specials (Web Impact / Web Throw) via `spawnProjectile` (real travelling web projectiles with genuine range — the short puff vs. the full-range web-ball — not melee hitboxes); and the Ultimate's guaranteed payoff via `applyScaledDamage` (no `bypassScale`, no manual-subtract, no persistent summon, no DoT, no buff-mode). Ground Crawl is a 0-damage i-frame utility. The edge is MOBILITY + REACH, throttled by the shared 180 Web-Fluid pool — NOT raw power.
>
> - **Deliberately GLASS-AGILE — NO stat is a durable-side roster record.** HP 1080 (below-median, tied with the frail tier — Mayuri/Kurapika/L) · Def 80 (low) · Spd 96 (the high/agile tier, near Flash/Killua/Zenitsu). **The speed sits on a genuinely FRAIL frame — a real glass-cannon downside — which is the distinguishing line from the flagged Saitama/Toji "top speed on a TANKY body, no downside" outliers.** Atk 88 moderate. mobility `high`, `web_fluid` energy (180).
> - **Normals (RAW):** light 40 · heavy 72 · up 62 · air 52 · down_air 66 → **EFF ≈ 24/43/37/31/40** — mid-low tier (≈ Obito/Shinobu band; heavy 72 is below the shinobi-bruiser 78-90 band, by design — he is a fast poke/mixup acrobat, not a burst bruiser). All `createAttackFromMove` → scaled. `heavy` and `up` are both launcher-shaped (row_04 rising spin-kick / row_05 rising reach-spin), visually distinct.
> - **Command normal + crawl kick-up (RAW→EFF, FREE cooldown-gated):** `spiderCombo` Fwd+Heavy = a continuous MULTI-HIT running-leap→spin string (~3×22 = ~66 raw → **~40 EFF**, the Onoki/Yamamoto committed-command band) · `spiderKickup` = the Ground-Crawl EXIT launcher (58 raw → **~35 EFF**, `launchVy -24`). Both cooldown-only, no Web-Fluid.
> - **Specials (RAW → EFF ×0.60, cost):** **Web Impact** (neutral, quick short-range web-puff projectile) 50→**30** (16) · **Web Throw** (Fwd, SIGNATURE full-range spinning web-ball projectile) 78→**47** (30) · **Dash Attack** (Back, spin-punch gap-closer, superArmor + forward advance) 72→**43** (20) · **Handstand Flip Kick** (Up, anti-air launcher, `launchVy -26`) 64→**38** (20). Every value sits in an existing special band; Web Throw (his highest special, longest reach) is the costliest of the four — the built-in counterweight. **Ground Crawl** (Down) = a 0-damage low-profile EVASIVE: `invulnTimer 24` i-frames + a fast forward reposition, cost 14.
> - **The Web-Throw COMBO CANCEL is a metered combo-extension, not a free tack-on.** During `spiderCombo`, after a CLEAN hit (`_cmdHitLanded`), a Special input cancels the string into a full Web Throw (the row_15 fanning web-net bridge pose) — and it pays the full 30 Web-Fluid. A real BnB extension (chain → web on hit), gated by meter + requiring the hit to land first.
> - **The Ultimate is a SCALED cinematic sure-hit, routed through the choke-point — NOT a manual-subtract.** Maximum Web = an INLINE freeze-cinematic on the LIVE fighter (Mayuri-Bankai pattern, NO duplicate instance): the giant web-net (row_17's own expanding web-net FX — asset reuse, no new art) engulfs + **PINS** the foe (`vx/vy = 0`, a web-trap, not a launch), **340 RAW → ~204 EFF via `applyScaledDamage`** (block chips to 25%), cost 100. 204 = the cinematic-ult band (Madara Tengai 204 / Boruto 196 / Isshiki 204). Duplicate-render guard verified (single struck-once payoff, caster single-bodied, clean end).
> - **⚠️ WATCH-ITEM #1 — Spd 96 on a glass frame.** He is the fastest non-Speed-Force-tier acrobat, which (with the full-range Web Throw + double-air-dash `high` mobility) makes him slippery. **Why it is NOT the flagged Saitama/Toji outlier: the speed is paid for with genuine frailty (HP 1080 / Def 80 — bottom-tier durability), the glass-cannon downside those two lack.** Fair. Knob if playtests read him as too slippery: the dash/air-dash cooldown, not damage.
> - **⚠️ WATCH-ITEM #2 — Ground Crawl is a cheap i-frame evade.** 24 i-frames for 14 Web-Fluid + a mobile reposition + a launcher exit (kick-up). Same family as Hiruzen SPIN (15) / Nezuko Dodge / Saitama Side-Hop, and metered — but it is his lowest-cost defensive option and it converts (crawl → kick-up launcher → combo). Verified it does NOT enable a guaranteed unscaled loop (the kick-up is a normal scaled launcher on a real cooldown). Knob: raise the crawl cost or shorten the i-frame window if the evade-into-launcher reads as too safe.
> - **⚠️ WATCH-ITEM #3 — Web Throw full-screen zoning.** A ~990px-range projectile (78→47 EFF) at 30 cost is a strong keep-out/whiff-punish tool. In-band (scaled, cost-gated, one of only two projectiles, real startup/recovery), but it is his primary zoning threat. Knob: the projectile speed/lifetime (range) or the cost.
> - **Verdict: a FAIR acrobatic evasive web-technician — honestly ×0.60-scaled end-to-end, on a deliberately GLASS-AGILE frame (HP 1080 / Def 80 — no durable-side stat record; the Spd 96 is paired with a real frailty downside). The Ultimate is a scaled cinematic sure-hit in the established 204-EFF band. All three watch-items are self-limited (frailty-taxed speed / metered-committal evade / cost-gated single zoning tool) and single-point-tunable; none is a damage or durable-stat outlier. No fix applied (diagnosis pass).**
> - **Regression:** `test:spiderman` canonical **38/0** (STATIC every-sheet + web-FX/cinematic-art + portrait on-disk sweep + gate/stats/`Web Fluid`-label/`marvel` + the CONFIRMED hurt/knockdown art-gap assertion + movement [idle/intro/walk/jump/dash/both rolls/win] + 5 normals connecting + the Fwd+Heavy `spiderCombo` command-normal + Ground Crawl [i-frames + kick-up exit] + all 5 specials [web projectiles connect / melee gap-closer + launcher / the ★combo-cancel bridge] + the Maximum Web cinematic ULT [renders + guaranteed 204 payoff + no-dup] + a 24-action fallback-box sweep). Per-stage `test:spiderman-stage1..4` all green (26/22/22/13). LIVE-fighter-performs-the-ult verified (no duplicate-instance bug). Neighbors clean — shared triggerUltimate-switch / cinematic-render / special-dispatch paths: `test:mayuri` **32/0**, `test:saitama` **33/0**, `test:onoki` **31/0**, `test:hiruzen` **34/0**. GAPS by design: NO hit-reaction/knockdown art (CONFIRMED source gap → the engine's safe idle-pose procedural fallback, Jason precedent — no invented frames); no skins batch yet (row_25's REAL pre-drawn white/blue alt-costume is BANKED for it, plus recolor candidates); no voice (separate future follow-up). Source is a genuine CPS2 arcade rip → credited to Alvin-Earthworm in `credits.js` SOURCED_ART (MANDATORY named attribution).

> **Naoya Zenin (Jujutsu Kaisen — Projection-Sorcery frame-trap technician) — added 2026-08-17.** The arrogant, blinding-fast execution technician: a glass fighter whose edge is speed + reach + a HARD-EXECUTION frame-trap payoff, NOT durability or raw per-hit power. **A NORMAL-to-moderate, THEMATICALLY-FOCUSED kit (NOT a Madara/Onoki versatility schema-exception — every tool is Projection-Sorcery or speed, no "answer to everything" breadth; the Spider-Man-class framing): 5 normals (from 3 pieces of source art — light jab / heavy+up high-kick / air+down_air aerial-spin, honest documented reuse) + a Fwd+Heavy `naoyaCombo` multi-hit command-normal (row_08 low combo string) + 5 directional specials (neutral Energy Dart spread / Fwd Pitch Throw / Back+Up Frame-Skip blink pair / Down FRAME-TRAP) + the promoted guaranteed-Frame-Trap ULTIMATE.** **ZERO damage-scale bypass — the ENTIRE damage kit runs through the ×0.60 choke-point:** the 5 normals + the `naoyaCombo` multi-hit command via `createAttackFromMove`; both projectiles (Energy Dart / Pitch Throw) via `spawnProjectile` (real travelling orange cursed-energy shots — a 3-dart spread vs. a single fast dart — procedural color, not melee hitboxes); the Frame-Trap's 3 scripted steps + the Ultimate's guaranteed payoff via `applyScaledDamage` (no `bypassScale`, no manual-subtract, no persistent summon, no DoT, no buff-mode). Frame-Skip is a 0-damage i-frame mobility tool. The edge is SPEED + the earned frame-trap, throttled by the shared 180 Cursed-Energy pool — NOT raw power.
> - **Deliberately GLASS — HP 1050 is among the FRAILEST real-fighter values on the roster (below the 1080 frail tier: Spider-Man/Mayuri/Kurapika/L).** Def 80 (low) · Spd 96 (the high/agile tier) · Atk 90 (moderate). **The speed sits on a genuinely frail frame — the glass-cannon downside that distinguishes him from the flagged Saitama/Toji "top speed on a TANKY body" outliers.** mobility `high`, `cursed_energy` energy (180). NO stat is a durable-side roster record.
> - **RAW→EFF spot-checks (all ×0.60, harness-measured):** normals light 38→22, heavy 70→42, up-launcher 60→36, air 50→30, down_air 62→37; **command `naoyaCombo` ~20×3 raw → ~36 EFF** over the landed multi-hit string; **specials — Energy Dart 22/dart spread (~38 EFF point-blank cluster, cost 18) / Pitch Throw 40→24 (16, single FAST dart) / Frame-Skip 0-dmg blink (12, i-frames 16, both directions) / Frame-Trap steps 24+30+46=100 raw → ~59 EFF over the clean chain + 90f FREEZE (open cost 20)**; **Ultimate 330 raw → 198 EFF guaranteed + 90f freeze** (cost 100). Special costs 12–20 + ult 100 all draw the shared 180 pool → the whole kit cannot deploy at once.
> - **⚠️ WATCH-ITEM #1 — THE FRAME-TRAP: a hard-execution, high-payoff special with a genuine FREEZE (the exact category the build mandate flagged — Susanoo-Lv2 free-attack-window / Self-Destruct panic-button).** Down+Special opens a visible telegraph, then a FIXED strict-link string (Light→Heavy→Light) must land inside tight windows (open 22f / step 14f); a clean finish deals ~59 EFF + a 90-frame (~1.5s) full lock. **Why the RISK side is REAL (verified, not assumed):** (a) it is a TRUE strict link — a missed window HALTS the sequence exactly there and dumps Naoya into real punishable recovery (`dropRecovery` 30f, `_ftDropped` telemetry, **NO partial credit** — harness-verified both the clean AND the dropped path); (b) it costs 20 cursed energy just to OPEN (a whiffed telegraph is pure loss); (c) it is a GROUNDED, POINT-BLANK (`reach` 96) commit — every step must connect on a moving opponent up close; (d) critically it lives on the ROSTER-FRAILEST frame (HP 1050 / Def 80), so a dropped trap gets him **hard-punished** — the risk is paid in survivability. **The freeze does NOT combo into a guaranteed unscaled nuke** — it is a set-duration lock that opens a *mixup*; any follow-up is normal ×0.60-scaled damage on a real cooldown (verified: no guaranteed unscaled loop). This is the Susanoo-Lv2 category with the risk side ACTUALLY present (unlike that outlier's free window). **Knob if playtests read the reward as too safe: lengthen `dropRecovery`, shorten the `freeze` duration, or tighten the step windows — NOT the per-hit damage (already ×0.60 and in-band).**
> - **⚠️ WATCH-ITEM #2 — the 90-frame FREEZE status itself.** A ~1.5s full lock (distinct from hitstun; `_naoyaFrozen` marker + big hitstun, live-ticking in `updateMiscTimers`). **Why it is in-band:** it is EARNED (the strict-link Frame-Trap or the full-100-meter ult — never a cheap toggle), it is comparable to Kurapika Chain Jail's bind (55f hitstun-bind) and other big-hitstun binds, and during it Naoya's follow-up damage is normal-scaled (not a free-damage engine). **Knob: shorten the freeze first (90→~60f) if the lock-into-mixup reads as too oppressive.**
> - **⚠️ WATCH-ITEM #3 — the ULTIMATE reuses the Frame-Trap (HONESTY flag, not a power flag).** Stage-0 confirmed NO ultimate-tier art exists; per the owner decision the ult is a PROMOTED guaranteed auto-Frame-Trap (meter-gated, no execution risk) reusing the Stage-4 cast poses — there is **no oversized/unique ult FRAME (art gap, flagged).** Mechanically it is a scaled guaranteed **198 EFF** (top-ult band: Saitama 204 / Mayuri 198 / Kiba 198 — NOT above it) + the same 90f freeze, cost 100, inline live-fighter (Mayuri-Bankai pattern — no duplicate instance, verified). In-band as a finisher; the only "gap" is cosmetic (reused art), documented in code + here.
> - **DEVIATION NOTE (owner decision #3) — freeze applied DIRECTLY, not via `resolveGrab`.** The decision specced the freeze "via `resolveGrab`'s stun payload," but `resolveGrab` bails on `attacker.comboCounter > 0` (always true mid-sequence) and its `updateGrab` release THROWS the foe — both wrong for an in-place freeze. Applied an equivalent direct freeze-lock (identical observable status: full lock, set duration, distinct from hitstun). Flagged for the owner; same spec, different mechanism.
> - **Verdict: a FAIR glass frame-trap technician — honestly ×0.60-scaled end-to-end, on the roster-frailest frame (HP 1050 / Def 80 — the Spd-96 speed is paid for in real frailty). The Frame-Trap is the defining outlier and was scrutinised per the mandate: its high-payoff+freeze reward is counterweighted by a GENUINE, verified risk side (strict-link drop → punishable recovery on a glass frame, no partial credit, no guaranteed unscaled follow-up) — precisely the risk the Susanoo-Lv2 / Self-Destruct outliers LACKED. All three watch-items are single-point-tunable (drop-recovery / freeze-duration / step-windows); none is a damage or durable-stat outlier. No fix applied (diagnosis pass) — flagged for playtest scrutiny of the Frame-Trap risk/reward specifically.**
> - **OPEN ART GAPS (deferred, not invented):** no win-pose or intro art (genuinely absent in the 13-row source); no unique ultimate frame (ult reuses Frame-Trap poses); portrait is a bust cropped from the character-design reference figure (functional, pending a dedicated mugshot); the `row_09` Pitch Throw has no drawn projectile so it reuses the Energy Dart payload (owner decision). row_12 → the lose/downed pose (row_12 was NOT a kick — Stage-0 correction). No skins batch yet; no voice (separate future follow-ups).
> - **Regression:** `test:naoya` canonical **34/0** (STATIC every-sheet + portrait on-disk sweep + gate/stats/`Cursed Energy`-label/`jujutsu_kaisen` + the REAL hurt/knockdown/lose art assertion + win/intro gap assertion + movement [walk/crouch/dash] + 5 normals connecting + the Fwd+Heavy `naoyaCombo` command-normal + all 5 specials [dart/pitch projectiles connect / Frame-Skip blink pair / the ★Frame-Trap CLEAN + DROPPED paths] + the promoted guaranteed-Frame-Trap ULT [white-wing finish + 90f freeze + 198 payoff + no-dup] + a 21-action fallback-box sweep). Per-stage `test:naoya-stage1..5` all green (23/12/7/24/10). Neighbors clean — shared `triggerUltimate`-switch / `updateMiscTimers` / special-dispatch / command-combat paths: `test:spiderman` **38/0**, `test:mayuri` **32/0**, `test:saitama` **33/0**, `test:onoki` **31/0**, `test:boruto` **15/0**, `test:maki` **30/0** (JJK sibling), `test:kurapika` **25/0**. `test:credits` 14/2 = PRE-EXISTING (`toji`/`pain` unattributed — not Naoya).

> **Deathstroke (DC — Slade Wilson, "the Terminator") — added 2026-08-17.** The enhanced-mercenary multi-weapon technician: a well-rounded peak-human whose edge is VERSATILITY (martial arts + katana + sidearm pistol, ground and air) + mobility, NOT a single dominant tool. **A VERSATILITY schema-exception (the Madara/Onoki/Saitama class — flagged for its BREADTH, not its per-hit power): 5 normals + a crouch-stab attack variant + 5 directional specials spanning three weapon modes (neutral Sword Slash / Fwd Draw-&-Cut lunge / Back Gun Shot projectile / Down Running Slash / air Aerial Spin) + the promoted "Killing Stroke" ULTIMATE.** Owner-locked as ONE self-contained moveset (NO stance toggle) — each sword slash draws/cuts on its own, the pistol is one situational tool. **ZERO damage-scale bypass — the ENTIRE damage kit runs through the ×0.60 choke-point:** the 5 normals + the crouch-stab variant + all four melee specials (Sword Slash / Draw-&-Cut / Running Slash / Aerial Spin) via `createAttackFromMove`; the Gun Shot via `spawnProjectile` (a real travelling bullet tracer — procedural, no projectile art exists — with genuine range, not a melee hitbox); and the Ultimate's guaranteed payoff via `applyScaledDamage` (no `bypassScale`, no manual-subtract, no persistent summon, no DoT, no buff-mode, no transformation). The edge is BREADTH + a balanced frame, throttled by the shared 120 Adrenaline pool — NOT raw power.
> - **Deliberately BALANCED peak-human — NO stat is a roster record (neither a glass nor a tank outlier).** HP 1150 (mid — above the 1080 frail tier, below the 1250+ bruisers) · Def 86 (mid) · Spd 92 (fast, agile — below the 96 high/Flash tier) · Atk 92 (good, a master combatant). mobility `high`, `adrenaline` energy (120). **He has no glaring statistical weakness — that absence is itself the thing to watch (see WATCH-ITEM #1), NOT any single high number.**
> - **Normals (RAW → EFF ×0.60):** light 44→**26** · heavy 82→**49** · upAttack 64→**38** (roundhouse high-kick launcher) · airAttack 54→**32** · downAir 72→**43** (reuses the air jump-slash art) → mid tier (≈ the Onoki/Boruto band). The crouch-stab variant (Down+light, `combat.js` `_setCrouchVariant`) is light-tier. All `createAttackFromMove` → scaled.
> - **Specials (RAW → EFF ×0.60, cost from the 120 Adrenaline pool):** **Sword Slash** (neutral, upward→horizontal arc) 82→**49** (22) · **Draw & Cut** (Fwd, committed lunge, +16 advance) 96→**57** (28) · **Running Slash** (Down, diving dash-in, +22 advance) 90→**54** (26) · **Aerial Spin** (air, launcher hitbox) 78→**46** (24) · **Gun Shot** (Back, travelling bullet, ~full-range) 56→**33** (18). Every value sits in an existing special band; Draw-&-Cut (his highest, a committed lunge) is the costliest melee — the built-in counterweight. The Gun is the cheapest but lowest-damage (a poke/zoning tap, not a burst).
> - **The Ultimate is a SCALED cinematic sure-hit, routed through the choke-point — NOT a manual-subtract.** "Killing Stroke" = the PROMOTED `row_09` overhead spin-finish, built as an INLINE freeze-cinematic on the LIVE fighter (Naoya/Mayuri-Bankai pattern, NO duplicate instance): the foe is frozen (hitstop) + camera focus while 3 guaranteed beats resolve → the overhead FINISH is the heavy payoff, **330 RAW → EXACTLY 198 EFF via `applyScaledDamage`** (block chips to 25%), cost 100. 198 = the top-ult band (Saitama 204 / Mayuri 198 / Naoya 198 / Boruto 196 — NOT above it). Verified range-independent sure-hit (lands the full 198 from OUT of melee range); single-bodied caster, single struck-once payoff.
> - **⚠️ WATCH-ITEM #1 — THE BREADTH (the schema-exception flag): three weapon modes + 5 specials + a balanced frame with no glaring weakness.** This is the Madara/Onoki/Saitama "versatility outlier" category — the concern is the *number of answers* (melee arc / gap-closing lunge / full-range gun / low dash-slash / air launcher), NOT any single hit's power. **Why it is assessed IN-BAND, not a power spike:** every individual tool sits in an existing band (no normal or special is a damage record), the whole kit is honest ×0.60, and the shared 120 Adrenaline pool means the specials + ult cannot all deploy at once (18–28 each + ult 100). The counterweight to "an answer to everything" is that NO answer is oversized and the meter gates them. **Knob if playtests read him as too all-purpose: trim a special (fold Running Slash into a Sword-Slash variant) or raise a special cost — NOT the per-hit damage (already ×0.60 and in-band).**
> - **⚠️ WATCH-ITEM #2 — a balanced frame with no glass-cannon downside.** Unlike the flagged Saitama/Toji "top speed on a TANKY body" outliers, Deathstroke is NOT a stat record anywhere (HP 1150 / Def 86 / Spd 92 are all mid-to-good, none extreme) — but he also lacks the frailty that taxes the frail technicians (Onoki/Mayuri/Naoya at 1050–1120). He is a genuinely well-rounded fighter. **Why it is fair:** "no weakness" on a MID frame (not a high one) is a rounded-fighter profile, not an outlier — his versatility is paid for by having no single dominant stat (he loses the pure-speed race to the 96-tier, the durability race to the bruisers, and the burst race to the launcher specialists). **Knob if he reads as too safe: shave Def 86→82 or Spd 92→90 to give a clearer soft spot.**
> - **⚠️ WATCH-ITEM #3 — the Gun as a ranged option on an otherwise-melee bruiser.** A ~full-range bullet (56→33 EFF) at 18 cost gives a mobile melee fighter a poke/whiff-punish keep-out tool. In-band (his lowest-damage special, cost-gated, real cast + recovery, a single procedural round — not a rapid-fire zoning engine), but it is the tool that most widens his coverage. **Knob: the bullet speed/lifetime (range) or the cost.**
> - **Verdict: a FAIR versatility schema-exception — a well-rounded enhanced-human multi-weapon technician, honestly ×0.60-scaled end-to-end, with NO stat a roster record (mid frame, no glass downside AND no tank record). The BREADTH (3 weapon modes) is the defining trait and was flagged per the mandate rather than shipped silently: every individual tool is in-band, the shared 120 Adrenaline pool gates the kit, and the Ultimate is a scaled cinematic sure-hit in the established 198-EFF band. All three watch-items are single-point-tunable (special count/cost / a stat shave / gun range); none is a damage or durable-stat outlier. No fix applied (diagnosis pass) — flagged for playtest scrutiny of the versatility breadth specifically.**
> - **OPEN ART GAPS (deferred, not invented):** NO unique ultimate frame (the ult PROMOTES the `row_09` spin-finish — a finisher pose, not an oversized cinematic frame; owner decision #2); NO bespoke win-pose (repurposed the `row_11` sword-draw-to-ready stance — a real, fitting settle, flagged as not-a-victory-animation); `down_air` reuses the air jump-slash art, and `dash`/`fall`/`guard` reuse `run`/`jump`/`idle` (no dedicated art — honest flagged reuses); the Gun has NO projectile art (procedural bullet tracer); `row_11` idle-rotation + `row_10` turnaround + the `row_01` title card / gun-aim reference were reference-only (excluded). No skins batch yet; no voice (separate future follow-ups).
> - **Regression:** `test:deathstroke` canonical **30/0** (STATIC every-sheet + portrait on-disk sweep + the honest-reuse + win-pose-gap assertions + gate/stats/`Adrenaline`-label/`dc` + movement [distinct walk vs run + REAL knockdown/getup art] + 5 normals connecting [up launches] + the crouch-stab variant + all 5 directional specials [sword/draw-cut/running-slash render+connect / Gun spawns a bullet projectile + connects / air Aerial Spin] + the promoted "Killing Stroke" ULT [casts dsUlt + guaranteed 198 from out of range + no-dup] + a 24-action fallback-box sweep). Per-stage `test:deathstroke-stage1/2/4/5` all green (25/15/18/10); Stage 3 = intentional no-op (no command-chain content). Neighbors clean — shared `triggerSpecial`/`triggerUltimate`-switch / cinematic-render / `createAttackFromMove` / `spawnProjectile` paths: `test:boruto` **15/0**, `test:onoki` **31/0**, `test:mayuri` **32/0**, `test:naoya` **34/0**, `test:spiderman` **38/0**. `test:credits` 14/2 = PRE-EXISTING (`toji`/`pain` unattributed — Deathstroke IS attributed, project-adapted bucket).

> **Brainiac (DC — the Coluan super-intelligence, 2nd `dc` char) — added 2026-08-17.** The methodical, FRAIL all-special ZONER: he does not brawl — his power is a deep toolbox of ranged/space-control tools (energy beams, metallic tentacles, a force-field, disc-levitation), on a slow, low-durability frame. The source sheet has **NO basic-strike art whatsoever** (every row is idle/movement/special/reaction) — the owner-locked resolution was to REPURPOSE the tentacle-strike frames (row_09/10) as the normals, turning the gap into a genuine long-reach zoner identity rather than inventing punches. **A VERSATILITY schema-exception (the Madara/Onoki/Saitama/Byakuya/Deathstroke class — flagged for BREADTH, not per-hit power): 5 tentacle normals + a crouch-tentacle attack variant + 5 directional specials (neutral Energy Beam projectile / Fwd Energy Blade advancing slash / Down Tentacle Sweep long low disjoint / Back Electric Shield defensive buff / Up Levitation rise+air-beam) + the "Sphere of Annihilation" Energy-Pillar ULTIMATE.** **ZERO damage-scale bypass — the ENTIRE damage kit runs through the ×0.60 choke-point:** the 5 normals + the crouch variant + both melee specials (Blade / Sweep) via `createAttackFromMove`; both beams (neutral Beam / Levitation air-beam) via `spawnProjectile` (a real travelling cyan beam bar carved from row_02 — genuine range, not a melee hitbox); and the Ultimate's guaranteed payoff via `applyScaledDamage` (no `bypassScale`, no manual-subtract, no persistent summon, no DoT). Electric Shield is a 0-damage defensive self-buff. The edge is REACH + space-control breadth, throttled by the shared 200 Intellect pool — NOT raw power.
> - Core: **HP 1100 · Energy 200 Intellect · Atk 88 · Def 82 · Spd 80 · mobility low · spriteScale 1.5.** Deliberately FRAIL-and-SLOW: **HP 1100 sits in the frail-technician band** (above Onoki/Naoya 1050–1120's floor, below the bruisers); **Spd 80 = roster-low-leaning** (Rick/Megumi tier, far below the 88–98 rushdown band — he is a stationary turret, NOT a mover); Atk 88 (mid, base Atk does not scale damage — flavor) · Def 82 (low, frail band). **NO axis is a roster record** — no offensive, defensive, speed, or HP extreme.
> - **RAW→EFF spot-checks (all ×0.60):** Normals — light 42→**25** / heavy 78→**46** / up-launcher 62→**37** / air 52→**31** / down_air 68→**40** / crouch-tentacle 42→**25**. Specials — Energy Beam 40→**24** (projectile, 20 cost) / Energy Blade 66→**39** (advancing melee, 22 cost) / Tentacle Sweep 72→**43** (rangeX 150 disjoint, 26 cost) / Levitation air-beam 36→**22** (projectile, 24 cost) / Electric Shield **0-dmg** defensive buff (30 cost). **Ultimate "Sphere of Annihilation" 330 raw→~198 EFF** (block chips to 25%) — squarely in the established cinematic-ult band (Mayuri/Naoya 198, Byakuya/Deathstroke 198–204).
> - **⚠️ WATCH-ITEM #1 — LONG disjoint normal reach.** Because the normals ARE the tentacle strikes, they carry a genuinely long, disjointed hitbox (rangeX **98–124**, vs. the ~60–90 melee band) — a real neutral advantage (he out-spaces most of the cast with basic buttons). **Why it is fair:** it is deliberately paid for by the FRAIL + SLOW frame (HP 1100 / Def 82 / Spd 80) — he wins the poke but loses every exchange he actually gets hit in, and cannot chase (no run/dash art, aliased to walk). **Knob if it reads as too dominant: trim the normal reach toward ~90–100 or shave startup/recovery, before touching damage (already in-band).**
> - **⚠️ WATCH-ITEM #2 — Electric Shield uptime.** The Back special raises `defenseMultiplier` ×1.6 (combat.js divides incoming damage by it → ~37.5% reduction, well clear of the 0.5 amp-floor) for 300f with a 360f recast cooldown — i.e. **~83% potential uptime** + a brief i-frame window, on a frail zoner. **Why it is not degenerate:** it deals no damage and each recast spends **30 Intellect from the SAME 200 pool the whole offense draws on** (a beam + a couple specials empties the bar), so it competes with his zoning rather than being free; and 37.5% reduction on a 1100-HP/Def-82 frame still leaves him among the squishier fighters even shielded. **Knob if it reads as too safe: raise the cooldown (360→~460) or lower the multiplier (1.6→~1.4) — both single-point, in `BRAINIAC_SHIELD`.**
> - **⚠️ WATCH-ITEM #3 — 5-special BREADTH.** The versatility (a projectile, an advancing slash, a long low disjoint, a defensive buff, and a repositioning rise) gives him an answer to most ranges. **Why it is fair (Madara/Onoki verdict):** every individual tool is scaled/in-band with no stat records, and the shared **200 Intellect pool is the self-limiting throttle** — specials cost 20–30 + the ult 100, so he cannot deploy the whole kit at once. More *options* widen his coverage; they do not raise his ceiling (set by per-hit EFF, all in-band). **Knob: special costs (raise to force selection) before damage.**
> - **Verdict: a FAIR versatility schema-exception — a frail, slow, long-reach zoner, honestly ×0.60-scaled end-to-end, with NO stat a roster record. The BREADTH is the defining trait and was flagged per the mandate rather than shipped silently; the three watch-items (normal reach / shield uptime / special count) are each single-point-tunable and none is a damage or durable-stat outlier. The one item that stands apart — the long disjoint normals — is directly counter-weighted by the frail-and-slow frame. No fix applied (diagnosis pass) — flagged for playtest scrutiny of the reach + shield-uptime specifically.**
> - **OPEN ART GAPS (deferred, not invented):** NO basic-strike art anywhere (normals repurpose the tentacle-strike frames — owner decision #1, honest flagged reuse); NO unique ultimate BODY frame (the caster holds the beam-fire pose while the promoted row_13 pillar VFX erupts — owner decision #2); NO win-pose or intro art (procedural fallback); the "Tentacle Whip" special from the Stage-0 kit list was DROPPED (its row_09/10 art now serves the normals — a separate whip special would be redundant); `run`/`dash` alias `walk` and `jump`/`fall`/`guard` alias `idle` (no dedicated art); `down_air` reuses the air tentacle-lash; the row_01 skull HUD glyph + turnaround + the duplicate row_11 idle were reference-only (excluded). No skins batch yet; no voice (BLOCKED — no clips; separate future follow-ups).
> - **Regression:** `test:brainiac` canonical **30/0** (STATIC every-sheet + portrait + 2 VFX on-disk sweep + the honest-reuse + special-pose assertions + gate/stats/`Intellect`-label/`dc` + movement [walk + REAL row_08 knockdown/getup art] + 5 tentacle normals connecting [up launches] + the crouch-tentacle variant + all 5 directional specials [Beam projectile / Blade / Sweep render+connect / Electric Shield raises defenseMultiplier / Levitation rises] + the "Sphere of Annihilation" ULT [casts + pillar cinematic active + guaranteed 198 from out of range + overlay actually draws + no-dup] + a 22-action fallback-box sweep). Per-stage `test:brainiac-stage1/2/4/5` all green (25/15/14/14); Stage 3 = intentional no-op (no command-chain content). > **Alternate Sukuna (JJK — an alternate-universe Ryomen Sukuna, a SEPARATE character from the existing `sukuna`) — added 2026-08-18.** Built from a DIFFERENT source rip (Cinontk sprites / Bitsverse644 sheet, `sukuna_row_*`) than the existing Sukuna. An aggressive cursed-technique BRUISER — NOT a schema-exception large kit. Stage-0 reconciliation cut the imprecise build-prompt's implied large zoner down to what the art actually supports: **a MODEST, honest kit — 5 normals + the Fwd+Heavy Dismantle/Cleave 2-stage red-crescent rekka (cancel-on-hit) + 3 directional specials (neutral Fūga: Fire Arrow projectile / Fwd Spinning Lunge Kick / Down Cursed Grab) + the "Domain Expansion: Malevolent Shrine" ULTIMATE.** No knee-slide / running-kick / evasive-dash were fabricated — the art had no distinct frames for them (honest cut, flagged). **ZERO damage-scale bypass — the ENTIRE damage kit runs through the ×0.60 choke-point:** the 5 normals + both Cleave rekka stages + the Spinning Lunge Kick via `createAttackFromMove`; the Fūga Fire Arrow via `spawnProjectile` (a real travelling pale-gold arrow — procedural, genuine range, not a melee hitbox); the Cursed Grab via the shared `resolveGrab`/`updateGrab` pipeline (`_grabThrowDmg` throw damage IS scaled — verified −72 = 120×0.60); and the Ultimate's guaranteed payoff via `applyScaledDamage` (no `bypassScale`, no manual-subtract, no persistent summon, no DoT, no buff-mode, no transformation).
> - **RAW→EFF spot-checks (all ×0.60):** Normals — light 48→**28** / heavy 96→**57** / up-launcher 72→**43** / air 66→**39** / down_air 86→**51** (reuses air art). Dismantle/Cleave string — Cleave1 55→**33** → cancel-on-hit → Cleave2 95→**57** (full string **−90**, whiff/block ends it). Specials — Fūga Fire Arrow 60→**36** (projectile, 24 cost) / Spinning Lunge Kick 92→**55** (advancing melee, 28 cost) / Cursed Grab 120→**72** (command-grab throw, 22 cost, reach 84). **Ultimate "Domain Expansion: Malevolent Shrine" 330 raw→EXACTLY 198 EFF** (guaranteed sure-hit, foe frozen via hitstop; block chips to 25%) — squarely in the established cinematic-ult band (Brainiac/Naoya/Deathstroke 198, Byakuya 204).
> - **HP 1200 / Def 86 / Spd 88 / EN 200 (Cursed Energy).** A mid-high bruiser frame — close to the existing Sukuna (HP1240/atk95/def87/spd86) but slightly leaner. **FAIR / in-band:** a modest 3-special kit on a balanced frame, no stat record anywhere, whole kit honestly scaled. Not flagged as a versatility outlier (unlike the Madara/Onoki/Saitama/Brainiac/Deathstroke BREADTH class) — it is deliberately the SMALLEST of the recent additions, sized to the art.
> - **⚠️ WATCH-ITEM — Cursed Grab command-throw (72 EFF, 22 cost) as a guaranteed non-blockable at reach 84.** It uses the shared grab pipeline (techable on the standard grab-tech window, blocked by the 28-frame grab cooldown) so it is not free — but a 72-EFF unblockable off a mobility-forward bruiser is worth watching for stagger-into-grab loops. **Why fair for now:** it costs 22 energy, has real recovery, is techable, and only lands at very short range (84px); the ×0.60 throw keeps it in the normal-throw band, not a burst spike. **Knob if it reads as too strong: raise cost 22→30 or trim reach 84→75.**

> **Green Lantern (Hal Jordan, DC — "the Emerald Knight", 3rd `dc` char) — added 2026-08-18.** The flying hard-light CONSTRUCT user: a versatile zoner/mixup whose edge is a shape for every situation (a ring limited only by willpower) plus flight mobility — NOT a single dominant tool. Built from 624 pre-sliced individual frames (`hal_sprite_###.png`, sheet by cf2364 / original by enzo) assembled via `tools/reslice_green_lantern.py`. **A VERSATILITY schema-exception (the Madara/Onoki/Saitama/Byakuya/Deathstroke/Brainiac BREADTH class — flagged for its BREADTH, not per-hit power): 5 normals + a Fwd+Heavy "Ring-Charged Spin Kick" command normal + a dual-use charge button (P-tap FLIGHT toggle / P-hold ENERGY BEAM projectile) + 6 fixed-slot directional CONSTRUCT specials (neutral Emerald Fist / Fwd Lion-Head Ram / Back Blade / Down Binding Tentacle / Up Spike Crown anti-air / air Wrecking Sphere) + the "Will Made Manifest" multi-construct ULTIMATE.** Owner-locked as Option B fixed special slots (not a construct-select stance); the muscle-transformation art (603–619) was DROPPED as non-canonical (Hal's power is willpower, not bulking). **ZERO damage-scale bypass — the ENTIRE damage kit runs through the ×0.60 choke-point:** the 5 normals + the Spin-Kick command normal via `createAttackFromMove`; the Energy Beam + all 6 constructs via `spawnProjectile` (each construct is a REAL travelling projectile carrying its own hard-light sprite — genuine range, not a melee hitbox); and the Ultimate's guaranteed payoff via `applyScaledDamage` (no `bypassScale`, no manual-subtract, no persistent summon, no DoT, no buff-mode, no transformation).
> - **RAW→EFF spot-checks (all ×0.60):** Normals — light 42→**25** / heavy 80→**48** / up-launcher 62→**37** / air 54→**32** / down_air 70→**42** (reuses air art). Command normal — Ring-Charged Spin Kick 74→**44** (FREE, cd-gated, forward-only). Energy Beam (P-hold, 30 cost) 84→**50** (fast bolt projectile). Constructs (projectiles, sprite-carrying) — Emerald Fist 100→**60** (heavy/slow, 26) / Lion Ram 88→**53** (advancing, 30) / Blade 66→**40** (long poke, 20) / Binding Tentacle 72→**43** (slow ground-level, 24) / Spike Crown 74→**44** (rising anti-air, 22) / Wrecking Sphere 90→**54** (air drop, 28). **Ultimate "Will Made Manifest" 330 raw→EXACTLY 198 EFF** (Fist 70 + Lion 70 + Blade 60 + giant Sphere 130 payoff; guaranteed sure-hit, foe frozen via hitstop; block chips to 25%) — squarely in the established cinematic-ult band (Brainiac/Naoya/Deathstroke 198, Byakuya 204).
> - **HP 1150 / Def 84 / Spd 94 / EN 200 (Willpower) / canFly.** A well-rounded, mobile frame — sturdier than the frail technicians (Onoki/Mayuri/Brainiac 1050–1120), below the bruisers; no stat record anywhere (matches Deathstroke's rounded-frame profile, +2 Spd for the flier). **FAIR:** the breadth is paid for by having no single dominant stat and by the shared 200 Willpower pool throttling the whole kit; the whole damage kit is honestly ×0.60-scaled.
> - **⚠️ WATCH-ITEM #1 — construct BREADTH: 6 directional specials + the Energy Beam = 7 ranged tools, ALL projectiles, on a FLYING frame.** This is a very complete zoning toolbox (a heavy anchor Fist, an advancing Lion, a long Blade poke, a low Tentacle, an anti-air Spike Crown, an air Sphere, plus the beam), and flight lets him pick his spacing at will. **Why it is fair:** every tool is honestly ×0.60-scaled, each costs 20–30 Willpower from ONE shared pool (sustained zoning drains fast), the constructs have real recovery + a cast pose (not instant), and none is a stat spike — the beam 50 / constructs 40–60 EFF are ordinary special values. The identity is COVERAGE, not per-hit power. **Knob if the zoning reads as too oppressive: raise construct costs (e.g. Blade 20→24, Sphere 28→32) or trim Blade lifetime/reach so full-screen control costs more meter.**
> - **⚠️ WATCH-ITEM #2 — flight on a projectile zoner (shared canFly system).** GL reuses the Omni-Man/Superman/Onoki flight system (P-tap toggle, energy-drained hover); combined with a full projectile kit this enables air-to-ground keepaway. **Why it is fair:** the SAME shared-pool Willpower feeds BOTH flight drain AND every construct/beam — he cannot both hover indefinitely and spam constructs; flight also disables the charge (`omniCantCharge`), so he can't beam while flying. **Knob: raise the flight drain or the beam/construct costs if air-keepaway dominates.** (Also: the P-hold-vs-tap beam/flight split uses `wasHeld && !wasTap` — the CORRECT form; the older Onoki branch uses bare `wasHeld`, a latent tap-eats-flight quirk left untouched.)
> - **Regression:** `test:green-lantern` canonical **39/0** (STATIC every-sheet [20 gl_*_uniform] + portrait on-disk sweep + gate/stats/`Willpower`-label/`dc`/`canFly` + movement [flight + honest reuses walk=dash=run / guard=getup=idle] + 5 normals connecting [up launches] + the Spin-Kick command normal + the dual-use charge button [P-tap Flight / P-hold Beam projectile] + all 6 construct specials [Fist/Lion/Blade/Tentacle spawn+connect / Spike rises anti-air / Sphere drops airborne] + the "Will Made Manifest" ULT [casts glBeam + guaranteed 198 from out of range + constructs manifest + no-dup] + a 19-action fallback-box sweep). Per-stage `test:green-lantern-stage1..6` all green (26/14/6/10/25/9). Neighbors clean — shared `triggerSpecial`/`triggerUltimate`-switch / `handleChargeRelease` / command-combat dispatch / cinematic-render / `createAttackFromMove` / `spawnProjectile` paths: `test:deathstroke` **30/0**, `test:brainiac` **30/0**, `test:onoki` **31/0**, `test:mayuri` **32/0**, `test:spiderman` **38/0**. `test:credits` 14/2 = PRE-EXISTING (`aoi_todo`/`toji`/`baki`/`naoya`/`pain`/`yamamoto`/`kurapika` unattributed — Green Lantern IS attributed to cf2364/enzo, SOURCED_ART bucket).

Helmet/Head construct + horse gallop + plainclothes-idle frames = unused (owner decisions; horse "movement mode" folded into the existing flight system).

> **Aoi Todo (Jujutsu Kaisen — `aoi_todo`, 2 sheets by akuma animation / MichelST) — added 2026-08-18.** The project's **most mechanically ambitious kit**: a Madara/Ichigo-class VERSATILITY scope exception **plus** a novel **tag-partner / position-swap system** ("Boogie Woogie"). Full kit: 5 normals + crouchLight · a 3-stage Fwd+Heavy rekka (elbow→hook/uppercut→roundhouse launcher) · **6 directional specials** (Gun projectile / Flying Fire Kick / Whip long-disjoint / Spin Backfist / Armored-Charge buff / air Dive) · the **Boogie Woogie Clap** (Charge+dir: summon/Boogie-swap the Yuji & Gojo cameos · enemy-swap · fake-clap bluff · self-swap marker · swap-out) · **Black Flash** timing bonus · a 3-way cinematic ult. This is EXACTLY the shape (two full-strength co-op allies + swaps + damage-amps) the mandate flagged as the **Megumi-unscaled-summon outlier risk class** — so it was built and audited defensively. **Verdict: fair VERSATILITY outlier, NOT a power outlier — every damage source is ×0.60-scaled and the whole swap/cameo economy is gated by a real resource meter. Ships with explicit watch-items + named levers.**
> - **Core: HP 1240 · Boogie 200 · Atk 95 · Def 86 · Spd 90 · scale 1.9.** Atk 95 and HP 1240 are on the HIGH side (HP below Saitama 1280 but above the bruiser mid-pack; Atk near the top of the melee band) — **deliberate (canonically a peak-physical bruiser), but this is LEVER #1** if he over-performs: drop HP toward ~1180 and Atk toward ~90 before touching any move. No outright records.
> - **Normals / rekka / specials: all honest ×0.60 at hit** (light 46→27 / heavy 94→56 / up 70→42 / rekka 52+62+98 raw / specials gun 50→30 / firekick 92→55 / whip 76→45 / spin 72→43 / dive 82→49). The **whip's rangeX 152 long disjoint** and the **Armored-Charge buff (dmg×1.2 / def×1.4, 6s, cd 12s)** are the two breadth items — both in-band vs. precedent (Brainiac reach, Mayuri/Brainiac timed buffs), not spikes.
> - **★THE CAMEO SYSTEM — why it is NOT the Megumi outlier:** the Yuji/Gojo cameos are persistent `summons.js` entities whose every strike routes through `applyScaledDamage` (`summons.js:566`, ×0.60) — **scaled, not the historical unscaled dogs.** Yuji 58→35/strike @48f, Gojo 54→32/strike @52f (≈ 80 combined DPS while both are out). **Gating is REAL (LEVER #2):** a bespoke **Boogie meter** — summon **45**, Boogie-swap **18**, self-swap **12**; **both cameos = 90 of 200** meter, so he cannot field both AND swap-spam AND ult; cameos auto-despawn after **5.5s**; a **can't-afford clap whiffs** (verified). Fake-clap and swap-out are intentionally FREE (mind-games / disengage cost no meter). If cameo pressure dominates, raise the summon cost or cut the duration.
> - **★BLACK FLASH:** a real swap ARMS a 26f window; the next CLEAN Todo hit adds **+0.9× base** (a heavy 56→~107). It is NOT free — arming costs a swap (**18 meter**) and demands the swap→strike timing (the intended skill ceiling). **LEVER #3:** lower the 0.9 bonus / shorten the window if swap-cancel bursts read as degenerate.
> - **Ultimate "Maximum: Black Flash" (100 meter):** inline freeze-cinematic (live fighter, no dup) — cameos cycle in, guaranteed 3-way (70+80+180 raw) → **EXACTLY 198 EFF**, block 25% (−49). **★Balance fix baked in:** the ult-summoned cameos have their auto-strike delayed past the cinematic (`attackTimer=-70`) so the 3-way damage is the guaranteed beats ONLY — **no cameo double-dip** (caught in testing: was 264, now 198). Top-ult band, no record.
> - **Health-checks caught (art-integrity):** the row_02 "gun" animation is a DIFFERENT character (green bowl-cut, suited) — owner chose "keep gun, render Todo" → Todo pose + procedural bullet (no stranger on screen). The "lose" art is arms-crossed-defiant (blue eye-glow), NOT the audit's claimed "slumped." No dedicated clap/ult art → clap reuses the guard frame, ult reuses attack poses + procedural Black Flash VFX (all flagged).
> - **⚠️ WATCH-ITEMS (not fixes):** (1) **total offensive density** — Todo + 2 scaled cameos + Black Flash can stack real pressure; the throttle is the shared 200 Boogie pool (levers #2/#3 above). (2) **HP 1240 / Atk 95** on a versatile char (lever #1). (3) **breadth** (6 specials + swap system) = the Madara/Ichigo VERSATILITY-exception precedent, self-limited by the meter. **Recommended nerf ORDER if he over-performs: meter costs (summon/swap) → Black Flash bonus → cameo damage/duration → core stats — damage per move LAST.**

> **Yuta Okkotsu (Jujutsu Kaisen — `yuta`, sheets by Soulfire / Petamynx / Dano / Santoryu; Rika V2 by shaulmorales) — added 2026-08-18.** The special-grade sword-and-cursed-technique technician: a versatile teen sorcerer whose edge is BREADTH (sword pressure + cursed-technique specials + a canonical self-heal + a persistent curse ally) on a rounded, agile frame — NOT a single dominant tool. **A VERSATILITY schema-exception (the Madara/Onoki/Deathstroke/Green Lantern/Aoi Todo BREADTH class — flagged for BREADTH, not per-hit power): 5 normals + a Fwd+Heavy 3-stage sword-combo rekka (Attack 1→2→3 thrust, cancel-on-hit) + 5 directional specials (neutral Cursed Energy Manipulation beam / Fwd Strong Attack advancing sword / Down Kick 4 dark-cursed-energy wing LAUNCHER / Up Cursed Speech short shout + heavy stun / Back Reverse Cursed Technique SELF-HEAL) + the "Rika's Invocation" AI-assist ULTIMATE.** Two mechanics carry genuine new-territory risk — the persistent AI Rika summon (the Megumi-outlier shape) and the self-heal — so both were built defensively and are ×0.60/gated. **Verdict: fair VERSATILITY outlier, NOT a power outlier — every damage source is ×0.60-scaled; Rika is a single 100-cost-ult summon (not a cheap spam), and the heal is expensive + slow + punishable. Ships with explicit watch-items + named levers.**
> - **Core: HP 1150 · Cursed Energy 200 · Atk 90 · Def 84 · Spd 94 · scale 1.9 (~100px).** Every value INSIDE existing bands, no record anywhere: HP 1150 = the rounded-frame tier (ties Deathstroke/Green Lantern, above the frail technicians Onoki/Mayuri/Naoya 1050–1120, below the bruisers); Atk 90 = mid (the 94–100 top is untouched — he is no power hitter); Def 84 = mid-low (a slim teen, = Maki/Naoya-adjacent, the intended soft spot); Spd 94 = upper-mid (ties Green Lantern, below the 96-tier Toji/Boruto). A rounded, mobile frame paid for by no dominant stat.
> - **Normals / rekka / specials: all honest ×0.60 at hit.** Normals — light 42→**25** / heavy 80→**48** / up-launcher 62→**37** / air 52→**31** / down_air 70→**42** (reuses air art). Sword-combo rekka — combo1 56→**34** → cancel-on-hit → combo2 66→**40** → combo3 96→**58** (full string **−132**, whiff/block ends it). Specials — Cursed Energy Manip beam 58→**35** (projectile, 24 cost) / Strong Attack 96→**58** (advancing melee, 26 cost) / Kick 4 88→**53** (dark-energy wing LAUNCHER, 28 cost) / Cursed Speech 42→**25** (short procedural shout, 22 cost). All melee via `createAttackFromMove`; both projectiles (CEM beam / Cursed Speech shout) via `spawnProjectile` (genuine range/reach, not melee hitboxes).
> - **★THE RIKA ASSIST — why it is NOT the Megumi outlier:** "Rika's Invocation" (owner decision #8 = AI assist-ally) spawns a PERSISTENT `summons.js` `rikaAssist` entity whose every strike routes through the ×0.60 summon choke-point (55→**33**/strike @50f, `oneHit:false`, ~6s life). Over the duration she lands ~5 strikes ≈ **−165–198 total** — squarely in the established cinematic-ult band (Naoya/Deathstroke/Green Lantern 198), NOT the historical unscaled Megumi dogs. **Gating is REAL (LEVER #1):** she costs the FULL 100-energy ULTIMATE (half the 200 pool) — not a cheap spammable special like Megumi's 20-cost dogs — is a SINGLE instance (`maxSimultaneous:1`), and auto-despawns after ~6s. If Rika over-performs: cut her `duration` (360→~300), lengthen `attackInterval` (50→~64), or trim per-strike damage (55→~48).
> - **★REVERSE CURSED TECHNIQUE — the genuinely powerful new mechanic (LEVER #2):** a real canonical SELF-HEAL (+120 HP ≈ 10% of max). It is deliberately NOT free: **cost 60** (30% of the pool), a **40f cast** + long recovery that leaves him fully punishable, a no-op at full HP (no waste), and NO i-frames / super-armor (it can be blown up mid-cast). This is the one lever to watch first if he out-sustains the roster — **raise the cost (60→~80), lower the heal (120→~90), or lengthen the recovery** before touching anything else. No other roster heal exists, so it has no direct precedent (hence the explicit flag).
> - **⚠️ WATCH-ITEMS (not fixes):** (1) **Rika total density** — a persistent scaled striker on top of Yuta's own offense (lever #1; already gated behind the 100-cost ult). (2) **RCT sustain** (lever #2). (3) **Cursed Speech 42f stun** — low damage (25) but a long stun setup; in-band vs precedent (Naoya frame-trap, Mayuri poison) but watch for loop potential — shorten to ~30f if it enables degenerate resets. (4) **breadth** (5 specials + rekka + summon-ult) = the Madara/Deathstroke VERSATILITY-exception precedent, self-limited by the shared 200 pool. **Recommended nerf ORDER: Rika duration/interval → RCT cost/heal → Cursed Speech stun → core stats — damage per move LAST.**

Portrait = a clean bust carved from the row_01 header (a real select asset). Kick chain (Kick 2→3) + aerial chain (Air 1–4) exist in the source but were DEFERRED — only the signature sword rekka was built.
