> **The Handler (Jujutsu Kaisen — Ten Shadows shikigami summoner + Mahoraga, rosterKey `handler`) — Stage 5 PRELIMINARY (2026-08-18).** The build's flagged **NEW-ENGINE** system: the "Mahoraga" adaptation ultimate. This is a diagnosis-only PRELIMINARY note per the prompt's explicit mandate to *flag the adaptation tuning and report honestly rather than ship default values untested* — the full char audit lands at Stage 6.
>
> - **The mechanic (NEW to this engine):** Ultimate transforms the Handler INTO Mahoraga (a `_skinAnim` art-swap FORM — the Vegeta-SSJ / Zaraki-Shikai architecture, renders `mahoraga_*_uniform.png`). Nothing before this tracked **per-move repeat-hit history against a specific entity.** Mahoraga **swaps in WEAK** (damageMultiplier **0.70**, defenseMultiplier **0.85** — reduced offense, takes ~18% more) and **ADAPTS**: `combat.tickMahoragaAdapt` buckets each incoming move by key (`atk.name`, falling back to melee/**projectile**/category) and scales that move's damage down on each repeat via the **LADDER `[1.0, 0.6, 0.35, 0.18, 0.08, 0.03]`** (the connect count of THAT move). A NEW distinct move bumps `_mahoragaDistinct`, which **GROWS** him each frame (`updateHandlerMahoraga`): damage `0.70 + d×0.15`, defense `0.85 + d×0.10`, HP regen `d×0.12/f`, capped at **d=6**. Timed **~10s** (600f) then auto-reverts. Kit while active: normals render the Mahoraga attack combo (row_03); Special = the "adapted counter" World-Slash (row_05), damage `70 + d×14`.
> - **★THE BALANCE RISK (explicitly flagged, per the prompt):** an adaptation ladder is exactly the kind of system that goes **degenerate if mistuned.** Measured in `test:handler-stage5`, the SAME move's landed damage ramps **60 → 35 → 18 → 8 → 2** — by the 3rd–4th repeat it is near-useless. **Too STEEP** ⇒ "don't use your best move" play (the opponent is punished for their strongest option); **too SHALLOW** ⇒ the form is a non-threat. The current ladder is **deliberately on the steep side** and is a **PLAYTEST question, not a shipped default.** These numbers are the **primary tuning surface** and MUST be validated in real matches before sign-off.
> - **Levers, in nerf order (if it over-performs):** (1) **the ladder** — flatten it (`0.6/0.35/0.18…` → e.g. `0.75/0.55/0.4/0.3`) so repeats stay relevant; this is the first knob. (2) **growth rate** — `MAHORAGA_DMG_PER/DEF_PER/REGEN_PER` and the `d=6` cap (a fully-adapted Mahoraga reaches dmg×1.60/def×1.45 + ~43 HP/s — strong but time-boxed). (3) **duration** (600f) — the hard time-box is the main global throttle; shorten before touching per-move damage. (4) the **counter** damage-per-adapt (`+14/d`). **cost = the full 100 ultimate** (half the 200 pool) — a real gate, single use.
> - **Anti-degenerate design baked in (mitigations):** (a) the growth is tied to **DISTINCT** moves, so spamming ONE move makes it useless but does NOT strengthen Mahoraga much — the opponent is incentivized to VARY moves, which is the interesting counterplay, not to stop attacking; (b) a **blocked** hit does not adapt (must actually land); (c) the whole thing is **time-boxed to ~10s** and costs the full ult; (d) the WEAK entry means the first few seconds are a real window for the opponent.
> - **Projectile note:** the growing `defenseMultiplier` is applied on the **melee** path only (engine-standard — projectiles don't divide by defenseMultiplier); against projectiles Mahoraga's mitigation is the per-move **ladder alone**. Acceptable (adaptation is the intended defense) — flagged for the Stage-6 pass.
> - **Regression (Stage 5):** `test:handler-stage5` **20/0** (weak entry / renders Mahoraga / adaptation ramp 60→2 / distinct-growth / HP regen / auto-revert / no JS errors). The `combat.js` damage hooks are `_mahoragaActive`-gated (zero effect otherwise): `test:handler-stage4` **28/0**, `test:vegeta-ssj` **76/0** (the shared `_skinAnim` transform path), `test:handler-stage2` **18/0** all clean. **Full audit + win/lose/intro + the Ryōki Tenkai Domain (deferred, art re-export) land at Stage 6.**

> **The Handler — FULL CHARACTER (Stage 6, 2026-08-18).** Completes the Mahoraga-adaptation preliminary above with the whole-kit verdict. A **shikigami SUMMONER / zoner** (Ten Shadows) with a transformation ultimate. **A LARGE kit (Madara/Ichigo/Pain-class VERSATILITY scope exception): 5 standard-string normals + a 6-slot directional SHIKIGAMI summon system + the Mahoraga adaptation FORM ultimate.** **ZERO stat outliers; every normal + every shikigami honest ×0.60 at hit (zero bypass); the breadth is VERSATILITY throttled by the shared 200 cursed-energy pool — NOT raw power. ONE genuinely novel system (the adaptation engine) flagged for playtest above.**
> - **Core: HP 1120 · Cursed Energy 200 · Atk 84 · Def 82 · Spd 90 · scale 1.75 (~100px).** Every value INSIDE existing bands, no record: HP 1120 = the frail-technician tier (ties Boruto, above Onoki/Mayuri/Naoya 1050–1100, below the bruisers); Atk 84 / Def 82 = mid-low (a summoner, not a brawler — the intended soft spot, = Naoya-adjacent); Spd 90 = mid (below Yuta 94 / Toji-Boruto 96). A frail zoner paid for by no dominant stat, whose power is the shikigami on screen, not his own body.
> - **Normals: L→L→H standard string (punch→punch→blade-drawn launcher), all honest ×0.60.** light 40→**24** / heavy 78→**46** (long-reach sword thrust) / upAttack 60→**36** (rising sword-draw launcher, gates the universal air combo) / air+down_air REUSE the light punch (no aerial art — flagged). Joined the shared `updateStandardStringCombat` grammar (the removed Megumi's grammar) — art-free L,L,H + heavy→special cancel, verified `test:stage2b-strings` 35/0.
> - **Shikigami cameo system (6 directional summons, EACH a ×0.60 `summons.js` entity — the Megumi-flag choke-point, zero bypass):** N=Divine Dogs (dog rush, 55→33 ×2-strike maul) · F=Orochi (snake lunge, 60→**36** exact) · B=Datto (rabbit swarm, 34→**20** chip) · D=Max Elephant (heavy slam, 90→**54**) · U=Nue (bird dive, 55→**33**) · AIR=Toad (drop, 50→**30** exact). Each capped `maxSimultaneous:1–2`, metered **28–45 energy**, one shared summon cast pose. **This is the intended Megumi-lineage power** — every strike routes through `applyScaledDamage` (proven exact in `test:handler-stage4` 28/0), NOT the historical unscaled Megumi dogs. **Watch-item, not a fix:** summon DENSITY (six distinct zoning tools) — the lever is **energy costs** (raise to force selection) + the `maxSimultaneous` caps, before touching per-summon damage.
> - **★Mahoraga adaptation ULTIMATE — see the PRELIMINARY entry above (the primary balance surface).** Transform form, weak entry → per-move damage ladder → distinct-adapted growth → ~10s auto-revert, cost 100. The ladder/growth numbers are PROVISIONAL and the #1 playtest question.
> - **Overall verdict: fair VERSATILITY outlier (summoner class), NOT a power outlier — no per-move nerf; ONE novel system (the adaptation form) explicitly flagged for playtest, not shipped as a proven default.** Every normal + every shikigami is scaled/in-band with no stat records; the offensive breadth is throttled by the shared 200 pool (six summons 28–45 + the 100-cost ult can't all deploy at once — the same shape as the Madara/Pain shared-pool finding). **Recommended nerf ORDER: Mahoraga ladder/growth (playtest) → shikigami energy costs → shikigami maxSimultaneous → core stats — damage per move LAST.**
> - **Health-checks / art gaps (all flagged):** no run/jump/guard/getup art → run/dash reuse walk, guard reuses idle, fall reuses jump, getup reuses hurt, air/down_air reuse the light punch; no bespoke win/lose/intro art → **win = the sword-draw INSET pair repurposed** (brown-bg chroma-keyed from the combo string — a REAL confident pose), lose = REUSE knockdown, **intro DEFERRED** (falls back to idle). **Portrait = a real bust carved from the sword-drawn win stance** (upgraded from the Stage-1 idle placeholder). The Ten-Shadows base-sheet ARTIST is still TBD in credits (Mahoraga = ARISRADIKLIF#3447 confirmed) — pin before ship.
> - **Regression:** `test:handler` canonical **56/0** (STATIC every-sheet + all shikigami/mahoraga art + portrait sweep · honest-reuse contract · gate/stats/`Cursed Energy`/`jujutsu_kaisen` · L,L,H normals connecting · the 6-shikigami system [Divine Dogs spawn+own-sheet] · the Mahoraga ULT [transform + weak entry + adaptation ramp 60→35 + growth + auto-revert] · a 19-action fallback-box sweep · credits-attributed). Per-stage `test:handler-stage1/2/4/5` = 25/18/28/20. Neighbors clean on the shared `triggerSpecial`/`triggerUltimate` switch, `summons.js` loop, `combat.js` damage hooks (`_mahoragaActive`-gated), and the `_skinAnim` transform path: `test:vegeta-ssj` **76/0**, `test:aoi-todo-stage5` **23/0**, `test:yuta` canonical, `test:stage2b-strings` **35/0**.

> **Genos (One Punch Man — the Demon Cyborg, Saitama's disciple; 2nd `one_punch_man` char) — added 2026-08-22.** A jet-boosted RANGE/RUSHDOWN hybrid: fast, offense-forward, but comparatively FRAIL (canon: hits hard, takes hits worse). Built from a SINGLE teal-keyed master sheet (`ddk5eh3-…png`, `tools/reslice_genos.py`). Honest, art-backed kit (the sheet is punch/blast-only — NO upward/kick/aerial art, so `up` REUSES heavy, `down_air` REUSES air, flagged): **5 normals + guard + a Fwd+Heavy 3-stage RUSH command chain (punch opener → rapid streak-burst MULTI-HIT → spinning charge LAUNCHER, cancel-on-hit) + 4 specials (neutral tap/hold 3-TIER Incineration Cannon / Fwd·Up·air Machine Gun Blows multi-hit / Down Jet Dash gap-closer / Back Afterimage Dash i-frame blitz) + the "Overdrive" timed-mode ULTIMATE.** **ZERO damage-scale bypass — the ENTIRE damage kit runs through the ×0.60 choke-point:** the 5 normals + guard + all 3 rush stages + Machine Gun + Jet Dash + Afterimage via `createAttackFromMove`; the Incineration blast via `spawnProjectile` (a real travelling procedural fireball — `ui.js` fallback orb, no projectile art exists — with genuine range that SCALES its size/damage per hold-tier); and the Ultimate is a buff-MODE with NO direct damage (it multiplies the already-scaled kit). No manual-subtract, no unscaled summon, no DoT.
> - Core: **HP 1080 · Core 200 · Atk 92 · Def 78 · Spd 96 · mobility high · spriteScale 1.5 (~104px).** Deliberately FRAIL/offense-forward — **HP 1080** = low band (by Naoya 1050 / L 1080; the glass tier, far below the 1200+ bruisers) — **not a record.** **Def 78** = **among the LOWEST on the roster** (below the 82–92 wall band; the frail-cyborg identity — takes hits worse, by design). **Spd 96** = upper-rushdown (below the 98 teleport-tier top — Toji/Saitama — so NOT a record). **Atk 92** = mid-high (flavor; base Atk doesn't scale damage). **Core 200** = the standard deep pool (Ichigo/Goku band) that FEEDS + throttles the special kit.
> - Offense (RAW → EFF ×0.60) — ordinary base normals: light 44→**26** (jab) · heavy 82→**49** (palm-cannon, short disjoint fire) · up 62→**37** launcher (reuses heavy art) · air 54→**32** (jet-dash punch) · down_air 60→**36** (reuses air). **Rush command chain** (Fwd+Heavy, cancel-on-hit rekka via shared `rekkaContinue`, **FREE** — recovery-committed): rush1 44→**26** → rush2 rapid streak-burst (12/hit, hasHit re-armed every 3 active frames) → rush3 92→**55** launcher = **~179 EFF full 3-stage confirm** (whiff/block ENDS it). ⚠ **the highest FREE-confirm total in its class** — see watch-item (1).
> - **4 specials (RAW → EFF ×0.60, cost)** — **Incineration Cannon** (neutral tap/hold 3 tiers, `spawnProjectile` fireball scaling by tier): tap 60→**36** (25) · mid-hold 96→**57** (40) · full-charge GIANT column 140→**84** (70) — the signature, high commitment (big wind-up + 70 Core for the top tier). **Machine Gun Blows** (Fwd/Up/air, rehit multi-hit melee) 12/hit → **~36 total** over ~6 ticks (35). **Jet Dash** (Down, lunging gap-closer) 78→**46** (25). **Afterimage Dash** (Back, i-frame blitz → strike) 70→**42** (30). Every per-hit value is in an existing special band (36–84 EFF; the 84 top-tier ties the flagship-single band, gated behind the 70 cost + long wind-up).
> - **Ultimate = "Overdrive"** (owner-locked Option B) — a timed power-up **MODE** (Kurapika Emperor-Time / Baki Demon-Back architecture; NOT a freeze cinematic, NO direct damage): 100 Core → **~7s of ×1.35 damage / ×1.15 speed** (bigger blasts — the Incineration projectile pre-scales its damage+size by the offense mult at spawn — and faster flurries) → auto-revert. **CANON DRAWBACK, the REAL cost (per the locked decision):** on EXPIRY the core OVERHEATS — **8% max-HP self-damage** (≈86 on 1080, floors at 1) + a **~2s vulnerability window taking ×1.30 damage** (new `_genosOverheatVuln` amp in `combat.js`, mirroring Emperor Time's `_emperorRevertVuln`) + a 20-frame recovery beat. KO / round-reset reverts pay NO drawback (guarded `_genosOverdriveExpire` flag).
> - **Does the Overdrive buff-mode create a concern? NO — it carries a genuine self-inflicted cost.** Unlike a pure power-up, the window ENDS in self-damage + a bonus-damage-taken vuln on an already-glass frame (HP 1080 / Def 78), so a whiffed Overdrive is a real liability, not free value — exactly the "power window with a drawback" the mandate asked for (Susanoo-Lv2 / Shikai precedent). The ×1.35 is bounded to ~7s, costs the full 100 pool, and the drawback punishes greedy use.
> - **Overall verdict: FAIR — a frail, offense-forward jet-rushdown/zoner with an honest fully-scaled pipeline, NO stat record (HP low, Def among the lowest, Spd below the 98 top), and its one buff-mode gated by a real self-damage + vulnerability cost.** **No fix applied (diagnosis-only).** **Watch-items (three, minor):** (1) **rush chain ~179 EFF** is the biggest FREE-confirm total in its class — but it requires landing ALL THREE cancel-on-hit stages (real execution; whiff/block ends it), and combo-decay caps the tail; **knob: rush2 per-hit / rehit interval, or rush3 damage — before touching the free-ness.** (2) **Machine Gun multi-hit** was over-tuned in build (128 for 35 Core) and **already lowered to 12/hit (~36 total)** — keep an eye on combo-decay interaction; knob = per-hit / rehit. (3) **Overdrive ×1.35 window** on a glass rushdown — the self-damage + vuln drawback is the counterweight; **knob: duration (420f) or the mult, not the drawback.** ★ **CREDITS BLOCKER:** the source-sheet DeviantArt artist is UNKNOWN — `credits.js` carries a flagged placeholder; attribution is MANDATORY before ship (mirrors Saitama/Jason). Full file-utilization in `GENOS_ASSET_MAP.md`; canonical `test:genos` = **28/0** (gate/stats/`Core` label · 9 movement/state sheets · normals connect · the rush chain opens · all 4 specials incl. the 3-tier Incineration fireball · the Overdrive ult enter + expiry drawback · win/lose pose wiring · a 28-action fallback-box sweep). Per-stage `test:genos-stage1/2/3/4/5` = 18/16/11/29/18.

> **Frieza (Dragon Ball — the Emperor of Universe 7, BASE/FINAL FORM only; `dragon_ball`) — added 2026-08-22.** An agile TECHNICAL ZONER/RUSHDOWN: fast, beam-heavy, but comparatively FRAIL (trades durability for reach + speed). Kit: **5 normals + guard + a Fwd+Heavy 3-stage RUSH command chain (strike opener → follow strikes → LAUNCHER, cancel-on-hit) + 4 directional/air specials (neutral Death Beam piercing procedural beam / Fwd·Up·air Ki Blast 3-shot CRYSTAL-sprite volley / Down Death Ball big slow sphere / Back Psycho Teleport i-frame blitz) + GOLDEN FRIEZA (charge-triggered timed high-risk MODE) + BLACK FRIEZA (the Ultimate — a triggered cinematic payoff).** **ZERO damage-scale bypass — the ENTIRE damage kit runs through the ×0.60 choke-point:** the 5 normals + guard + all 3 rush stages + Psycho Teleport via `createAttackFromMove`; Death Beam / Ki Blast / Death Ball via `spawnProjectile` (procedural beam + crystal-sprite shots + procedural sphere, all folding the offense mult at spawn); and the Ultimate is a buff-MODE with NO direct damage (it multiplies the already-scaled kit). No manual-subtract, no unscaled summon, no DoT.
> - Core: **HP 1100 · Ki 200 · Atk 90 · Def 80 · Spd 98 · mobility high · spriteScale 0.88 (~120px w/ tail, ~95px body — canon-short final form).** Deliberately FRAIL/offense-forward — **HP 1100** = low band (just above Naoya 1050 / Genos–L 1080; the glass tier, far below the 1200+ bruisers) — **not a record.** **Def 80** = **among the lowest** (below the 82–92 wall band; frail-frame identity — takes hits worse). **Spd 98** = ties the teleport-tier TOP (Toji/Saitama) — a fast zoner, but paired with the frail HP/Def, not a stat outlier overall. **Atk 90** = mid-high (flavor; base Atk doesn't scale damage). **Ki 200** = the standard deep pool that FEEDS + throttles the special kit + gates the ultimate.
> - Offense (RAW → EFF ×0.60) — ordinary base normals: light 42→**25** (jab, per-hit) · heavy 80→**48** (motion-streak kick) · up 62→**37** launcher (reuses heavy art) · air 52→**31** · down_air 60→**36** (reuses air). **Rush command chain** (Fwd+Heavy, cancel-on-hit rekka via shared `rekkaContinue`, **FREE** — recovery-committed): rush1 40→**24** → rush2 46→**28** → rush3 84→**50** LAUNCHER = **~102 EFF full 3-stage confirm** (whiff/block ENDS it) — a modest, mid-class free-confirm (well below Genos's ~179).
> - **4 specials (RAW → EFF ×0.60, cost)** — **Death Beam** (neutral, thin PIERCING procedural beam) 78→**47** (22). **Ki Blast** (Fwd/Up/air, rapid 3-shot crystal-sprite volley) 26/shot → **~47 total** over 3 shots (24). **Death Ball** (Down, big slow procedural sphere, size/damage grow with offense) 120→**72** (45). **Psycho Teleport** (Back, i-frame blitz → strike) 74→**44** (28). Every value sits in an existing special band (44–72 EFF); the 72 Death Ball is gated behind the 45 cost + a long slow projectile.
> - **TRANSFORMATION LADDER: base → GOLDEN → BLACK** — the SAME mechanic as Vegeta / Goku Black (owner: all DB transforms share it): threshold-gated on the CHARGE button (hold-release steps up, tap reverts), a continuous per-frame Ki DRAIN pays for the form, and it auto-reverts the instant Ki hits 0. A DB transform is an all-around DMG+SPD+DEF boost bought with energy drain — NOT a timed window, NO up-front cost, NO crash.
>   • **GOLDEN** — enter ≥100 Ki, drain 0.18/f (~11/s, = Vegeta SSJ): dmg ×1.25 / spd ×1.18 / def ×1.08. The buff folds into projectiles (Death Beam ×1.25 at speed).
>   • **BLACK** — escalates from Golden at ≥150 Ki (requiresForm), drain 0.30/f (~18/s, costlier): dmg ×1.50 / spd ×1.32 / def ×1.15 (ceiling tier; supersedes Golden). No separate ultimate — reaching Black IS the payoff (like Vegeta).
> - **Does the Overload buff-mode create a concern? NO — it carries a genuine self-inflicted cost.** The window ENDS in an exhaustion speed-down on a fast frail zoner whose whole game is mobility + spacing, so a whiffed Overload is a real tempo liability, not free value. The ×1.35/×1.18 is bounded to ~7s, costs the full 100 Ki pool, and the exhaustion punishes greedy activation.
> - **Overall verdict: FAIR — a frail, fast, beam-heavy technical zoner/rushdown with an honest fully-scaled pipeline, NO net stat record (HP low, Def among the lowest; Spd ties the top but only alongside the frail frame), and its Golden→Black transformation ladder gated by the same energy-drain cost as Vegeta / Goku Black.** **No fix applied (diagnosis-only).** **Watch-items (three, minor):** (1) **Spd 98 ties the top** — fine given HP 1100 / Def 80, but if the fast-zoner spacing ever feels oppressive, **knob: Spd 96 before touching offense.** (2) **Ki Blast volley** (3 crystal shots, ~47 total for 24 Ki) — cheap chip pressure; watch combo-decay interaction; **knob: shot count / per-shot damage.** (3) **BLACK form ×1.50/×1.32/×1.15** on a fast zoner — bounded by the 0.30/f Ki drain (~11s from full) + the 150-Ki gate; **knobs: drain rate / thresholds / mults.** (4) The ladder gives Frieza a strong late-round snowball if he banks Ki — same lever as Vegeta/Goku Black; watch it plays symmetrically. ★ **CREDITS BLOCKER:** the sprite-rip author is UNKNOWN — `credits.js` SOURCED_ART carries a flagged placeholder; attribution is MANDATORY before ship (mirrors Genos/Saitama). ★ **IMAGE-CAPPED build session** — idle/crouch/heavy-kick/power-up-stance/crystal are CONFIRMED art (idle & facing AUDIT-corrected — the whole rip was drawn LEFT-facing, now globally mirrored); the Golden/Black form look is a canvas TINT, unverified → pixel sign-off pending. Full detail in `FRIEZA_ASSET_MAP.md`; canonical `test:frieza` = **31/0**. Per-stage `test:frieza-stage1/2/3/4/5` = 19/16/10/20/**17**. ★DB-consistency: **Vegeta / Goku Black / Frieza / Goku** now ALL share the identical charge-triggered / threshold-gated / continuous-drain / revert-on-empty transform model (Goku aligned 2026-08-22: his 6-form ladder moved from the ultimate-button/100-energy-spend to the CHARGE button, drains REGEN-AWARE ≥0.08/f so every tier is a real net cost, procedural box gets a per-tier `drawGokuFormAura` indicator; `test:goku-transform` 21/0). Only Piccolo/Cell (non-playable placeholders) keep the older duration forms, untouched by design.

> **Batman — NEW VARIANT (`dark_knight`, DC — a SEPARATE roster entry from the untouched old `batman`) — added 2026-08-22.** A **THREE-MODE** fighter built from a single 5120×2880 TRUE-ALPHA sheet (`tools/reslice_dark_knight.py`): a standard-form brawler + a Rage-Mode power-up + a Mech-Suit ultimate. **A LARGE-KIT VERSATILITY scope exception (the Madara/Deathstroke/Green-Lantern/Aoi-Todo/Handler BREADTH class — flagged for BREADTH + the two power-up forms, NOT per-hit power): 5 normals + a crouch-sweep variant + 5 directional specials (neutral Crescent Chain long-disjoint / Fwd Chain Flail / Back Pistol Shot projectile / Down Cape Spin AoE / air Dive Bomb) + a RAGE-MODE transform (Up+Special) + the MECH-SUIT ultimate.** **ZERO damage-scale bypass on the base kit — the ENTIRE standard damage kit runs through the ×0.60 choke-point:** the 5 normals + crouch-sweep + all four melee specials (Crescent/Flail/Cape/Dive) via `createAttackFromMove`; the Pistol via `spawnProjectile` (a real procedural bullet tracer — no projectile art — genuine range). The two power-up forms are **timed multiplier MODES** (Genos-Overdrive / Kurapika / Baki architecture), NOT unscaled damage — every strike in-form is still an ordinary ×0.60 attack, merely multiplied by a bounded, metered, time-limited buff. **Verdict: a FAIR large-kit versatility exception — honest ×0.60 base kit, two power-up forms each gated by the shared 100-Fury pool + a real trade-off; no per-hit or stat record. Ships with explicit watch-items + named levers.**
>
> - **Core: HP 1150 · Fury (EN) 100 · Atk 90 · Def 88 · Spd 88 · scale 0.9 (~116px).** Every value INSIDE existing bands, no record anywhere: HP 1150 = the rounded-frame tier (ties Deathstroke/Green Lantern/Yuta, above the frail technicians 1050–1120, below the 1200+ bruisers); Atk 90 = mid (the 94–100 top is untouched); Def 88 = mid-high (a disciplined-guard flavor, matches old-batman-adjacent, not a wall); Spd 88 = mid (below the 92–99 fast tier — a deliberate, grounded bruiser, not a speedster). A rounded frame, no dominant stat.
> - **Standard specials (RAW → EFF ×0.60, cost of the shared 100-Fury pool):** Crescent Chain (neutral, long disjoint kusarigama, rangeX 156) 74→**44** (22) · Chain Flail (Fwd, advancing weighted-ball) 88→**52** (28) · Pistol Shot (Back, procedural bullet projectile — genuine range) 54→**32** (18) · Cape Spin (Down, tall AoE launcher) 70→**42** (24) · Dive Bomb (air) 80→**48** (24). Normals — light 34→**20** / heavy 66→**40** (lunge, rangeX 96) / up-launcher 54→**32** (reuses heavy art) / air 46→**28** (dropkick) / down_air 58→**35** (reuses air) / crouch-sweep 34→**20**. Every value sits in an existing band; the 156-reach Crescent is a spacing tool, not a damage spike.
> - **★RAGE MODE (Up+Special, 50 Fury) — a timed berserker buff with a REAL trade-off, NOT the ultimate.** ~6.5s of dmg ×1.28 / spd ×1.12 (base light 20 → **26** in-mode, verified). **The canon cost, tied to a real `combat.js` dmg-taken amp: while raging he takes +15% damage** (`_dkRageActive` → `dmg × 1.15`) — a berserker who hits harder but guards worse, a during-active liability (unlike the post-revert drawbacks of Genos/Frieza). The buff is bounded (~6.5s), costs half the pool, and the +15% vuln punishes greedy activation. ×1.28 sits BELOW the Genos/Frieza ×1.35 timed-buff precedent. **Visual honesty:** the "Rage" art is actually a plated red-eyed ARMORED BATSUIT + wireframe materialize (no flesh-bulk exists on the sheet — a GAP); gameplay keeps the berserker identity (owner may rename).
> - **★MECH SUIT ULTIMATE (100 Fury) — a 2-phase timed heavy-FORM (Kurapika/Baki architecture).** Phase 1 = wireframe materialize cinematic (camera-focus + brief foe freeze). Phase 2 = ~8s giant-mech form (`_skinAnim` body swap): dmg ×1.5 (base light 20 → **30**, verified) / def ×1.25 (armored) / **spd ×0.85 (SLOW — the real trade-off)**. The ×1.5 is the highest timed-form damage buff on the roster, but it is **counter-weighted three ways vs the Genos/Baki/Kurapika precedent:** (a) it is the ULTIMATE (full 100-Fury pool, once per meter — not a cheap special); (b) the mech is **ponderously slow** (×0.85) so pressure/whiff-punish is harder; (c) specials in-form fall through to base human cast poses (the mech is a normals-only bruiser, no ranged mixups). The def ×1.25 was deliberately eased from an initial ×1.5 during this audit so it is not "tanky AND hits hard with no downside."
> - **⚠️ WATCH-ITEMS (not fixes):** (1) **Mech dmg ×1.5** is the roster's top timed-buff mult — assessed in-band because it is the ult + slow + no ranged; **lever: trim to ×1.4, or add a post-revert vulnerability (Genos/Kurapika style), before touching base damage.** (2) **Two power-up forms** on one char — mitigated by the shared **100** Fury pool: Rage (50) + Mech (100) cannot coexist from one meter, and Mech needs the FULL pool, so they are effectively mutually exclusive per meter-cycle; **an untested edge is regen-refilling to 100 while Rage is still active → Mech would overwrite Rage's `_skinAnim`/mults (mech wins; revert resets to base). Lever: block `enterDarkKnightMech` while `_dkRageActive`.** (3) **BREADTH** (5 specials + 2 forms) = the Madara/Deathstroke VERSATILITY-exception precedent, self-limited by the meter. **Recommended nerf ORDER: Mech mults/duration → Rage vuln/duration → special costs → core stats — base damage per move LAST (already ×0.60 and in-band).**
> - **Regression:** `test:dark-knight` canonical **27/0** (STATIC every-sheet + portrait on-disk sweep + SEPARATE-from-`batman` + honest-reuse [run/dash=walk · jump/fall=glide · guard/hurt/getup=idle · up=heavy · down_air=air · lose=knockdown] + win-pose + `Fury`/`dc` + ult-def assertions · live heavy + Crescent connect · Rage enter/revert · Mech enter/revert · a **31-action** fallback-box sweep · old-batman-untouched). Per-stage `test:dark-knight-stage1/2/4/5/6` all green (27/17/19/16/22); Stage 3 = intentional no-op (owner-chosen skip — no command-chain art in the lower set). Neighbors clean — shared `triggerSpecial`/`triggerUltimate`-switch / `_skinAnim` transform / timed-mode tick / `createAttackFromMove` / `spawnProjectile` / dmg-taken-amp paths: `test:kurapika` **25/0**, `test:deathstroke` **30/0**, `test:genos` **28/0**. `test:credits` 12/2 = PRE-EXISTING (toji/baki/naoya/pain/ghostface_billy/yamamoto/kurapika unattributed — `dark_knight` IS declared, project-adapted bucket). ★ **ATTRIBUTION BLOCKER:** the source-sheet artist is UNKNOWN (DeviantArt asset-id filename `df2ek1u-…`); `credits.js` PROJECT_ART_KEYS carries it with an OPEN-attribution note — MANDATORY before ship (mirrors Genos/Frieza). ★ **OPEN owner items:** rename Rage→armor?, the blue-trenchcoat "civilian" figure role, weapons naming.

> **Piccolo (Dragon Ball — the Namekian warrior-mage; `dragon_ball`) — added 2026-08-22.** A TALL, long-reach VERSATILE mid-range KI-ZONER: patient neutral, big disjoints, procedural beams. UPGRADED from a dev-only non-playable placeholder into a real sprite build from a ripped 3DS sheet (`3DS - Dragon Ball Z_ Extreme Butoden - Fighters - Piccolo.png`, `tools/reslice_piccolo.py` — ★same GREEN-FILLED-cell connected-component slicer as Frieza, but with a **TIGHT cell-fill green key** so Piccolo's GREEN SKIN isn't keyed away). Stats: **HP 1150 (mid), EN 200, Atk 84, Def 88 (above-average), Spd 86 / dashSpeed 17 / jumpPower 32 / dashCd 32 (roster-typical mobility — raised 2026-08-22 from 82/15/30/40 in a "movement feel" pass; he sat below norm on every mobility stat and read sluggish; still below the fast-rushdown tier, cf. Frieza 98/21/26), spriteScale 0.90 → idle ~133px = TALLEST on the roster** (canon ~2.26m). ★Idle = a STATIC hold on box 21 (arms fully crossed) — the sheet has NO subtle-breathing pair (every stance frame is a distinct gesture: 19 fists / 20 hands-to-face / 21 crossed / 22 lower-lean), so any multi-frame idle read as constant hand-pumping (owner feedback); a static hold on his iconic menacing stand is the sensible fix. A real breathing loop needs a dedicated near-identical 2nd frame that doesn't exist here (art gap). walk/run borrow it. Kit: **5 normals + guard + a Fwd+Heavy 3-stage RUSH command chain (elbow/chop opener → overhead axe smash → roundhouse LAUNCHER, cancel-on-hit, ~102 EFF cumulative) + a FIXED-SLOT LARGE 6-direction special set (neutral Special Beam Cannon = thin fast PIERCING procedural beam, 57 EFF / Fwd Stretch-Arm Strike = a CONFIRMED elongated-arm LONG disjoint melee, rangeX 168, 42 EFF / Down·Up Masenko = quick procedural ki bolt, 31 EFF / Back Explosive Demon Wave = big slow procedural sphere, 70 EFF / air Flying Dash Kick = i-frame horizontal gap-closer, 44 EFF).** **ZERO damage-scale bypass — the ENTIRE damage kit runs through the ×0.60 choke-point:** the 5 normals + guard + all 3 rush stages + Stretch-Arm + Flying Dash Kick via `createAttackFromMove`; Special Beam Cannon / Masenko / Explosive Demon Wave via `spawnProjectile` (procedural beam + ki bolt + sphere, all folding the offense mult at spawn). No manual-subtract, no unscaled summon, no DoT.
> - **★TRANSFORMATION LADDER (added 2026-08-22, prototype scope) — base → POTENTIAL UNLEASHED (T1) → ORANGE PICCOLO (T2), the real Frieza/Vegeta charge-transform mechanic.** THRESHOLD-GATED on the CHARGE button (hold-release steps up: ≥90 Ki → T1, ≥140 Ki → T2; a TAP reverts), NO up-front cost, a CONTINUOUS per-frame Ki DRAIN (T1 0.14/f ~8s from full, T2 0.26/f ~faster), INSTANT auto-revert at 0 — identical to Vegeta/Frieza (`tickSustainedFormDrain`). **Escalating boost + escalating drain:** T1 dmg ×1.20 / spd ×1.12 / def ×1.06; T2 dmg ×1.42 / spd ×1.24 / def ×1.12. Balance-bounded by the same energy-drain economy as the rest of the DB pack — a bank-Ki-then-snowball lever, symmetric with Vegeta/Frieza. **★★ART = a PALETTE-TINT PLACEHOLDER (yellow-green T1 / orange T2 canvas wash), NOT bespoke art — a deliberate, flagged prototype simplification** (the canon Orange Piccolo body-shape change + the Great Namekian giant tier are deferred, NOT faked via scale tricks). `test:piccolo-stage5` = 20/0. **Watch-item: T2 ×1.42 dmg on an already-long-reach zoner — bounded by the 0.26/f drain (~short window) + the 140-Ki gate; knobs: drain rate / thresholds / mults before touching base offense.**
> - **Overall verdict: FAIR — a sturdy-average, tall, long-reach versatile ki-zoner with an honest fully-scaled pipeline and NO net stat record (HP 1150 mid / Def 88 above-average / Spd 86 roster-typical — reach & breadth carry, not raw numbers).** **No fix applied (diagnosis-only).** **Watch-items (four, minor):** (1) **breadth** — 6 directional specials + a rekka + long disjoints is a wide toolkit; watch that the zoning (Beam pierces, Demon Wave walls, Stretch-Arm pokes) doesn't lock opponents out; **knobs: recovery frames / costs before damage.** (2) **Stretch-Arm rangeX 168** — a very long disjoint melee for 18 Ki; watch its whiff-recovery is punishable; **knob: recovery / cost.** (3) **Explosive Demon Wave** (70 EFF, w96 sphere, 44 Ki) — the heavy zoning tool; watch chip + space control; **knob: speed / size / cost.** (4) **the transformation ladder** (above) — T2 Orange ×1.42 dmg is the new burst/comeback lever; bounded by Ki-drain + the 140-Ki gate, symmetric with Vegeta/Frieza. ★ **CREDITS BLOCKER:** the sprite-rip author is UNKNOWN — `credits.js` SOURCED_ART carries a flagged placeholder (`piccolo` MOVED from PROJECT_ART_KEYS); attribution is MANDATORY before ship (mirrors Frieza/Genos). ★ **VISION-VERIFIED build** (NOT image-capped): idle `[19–22]` arms-crossed loop, the walk-GAP (no locomotion on the sheet → walk/run borrow idle, like Frieza), Stretch-Arm, cape-removal intro `[147–150]`, and every movement/normal/rush/special pick were rendered + confirmed on camera; the whole rip was drawn LEFT-facing → globally mirrored (`FLIP_H`). > - **Core: HP 1300 · Solar Energy (EN) 200 · Atk 96 · Def 86 · Spd 92 · maxJumps 2 · canFly · spriteScale 2.0 (idle content 55px → ~106px on-screen, roster mid-band).** Every value INSIDE existing bands, no record: HP 1300 = upper-mid (BELOW the 1400+ Superman/Omni-Man powerhouse apex, above the 1150 rounded tier — deliberately less tanky than the built `superman` 1450); Def 86 = mid (below the 88-92 wall band — a brawler who trades durability for mobility); Spd 92 = fast tier (below the 98 top); Atk 96 = mid-high (flavor; base Atk doesn't scale damage). `canFly` reuses the shared Omni-Man/Superman flight system.

> **Iron Man 3 (`iron_man_3`, Marvel — the classic GBA-era armored Avenger; 4th `marvel` entry alongside `spiderman`/`iron_man`/`iron_man_2`) — added 2026-08-23.** A **charge-repulsor ZONER** whose centerpiece is a real, on-sheet **3-TIER charge system**. SEPARATE from `iron_man` (IM1, chibi JUS) and `iron_man_2` (Data East arcade) — borrows no art from them, and nothing here patches them. Kit: **5 normals (all 5 explicitly-labeled Shooting contexts preserved INDIVIDUALLY — standing→light, Running→heavy advancing, Jumping→air, Double-Jump→down_air OWN art, Crouching→crouchLight) + a "Looking Up" anti-air LAUNCHER (up) + NO command chain (Stage 3 = none; the sheet carries no chain notation — nothing invented) + THREE specials (neutral 3-TIER CHARGE REPULSOR Basic/Charged/Supercharged / Fwd SUPER LASER beam / Up+air SUPER MOVE spin-burst) + the "Super Nova" screen-clear ULTIMATE.** A LEANER kit than IM2 (no rekka, 3 special functions vs IM2's 4) despite the ranged density. **ZERO damage-scale bypass — the ENTIRE damage kit runs through the ×0.60 choke-point:** the 5 normals + crouch variant + Super Move via `createAttackFromMove`; the 3 charge tiers + Super Laser via `spawnProjectile` (real travelling projectiles carrying REAL per-tier on-sheet art, genuine range); and the Ultimate via `applyScaledDamage`. No `bypassScale`, no manual-subtract, no persistent summon, no DoT, no buff-mode, no transformation. The charge-windup fist FX and the Super-Nova screen wash are **procedural VISUALS only** (no damage).
> - **Core: HP 1200 · Repulsor (EN) 200 · Atk 90 · Def 90 · Spd 92 · maxJumps 2 · spriteScale 2.5 (idle content ~40px → ~100px on-screen, roster mid-band).** All in-band, no record — identical stat line to IM2 (durable armored tier, mid speed, no offense/power record).
> - **Offense (RAW → EFF ×0.60)** — normals: light 42→**25** (standing repulsor) · heavy 84→**50** (advancing "Running" repulsor, longer reach) · up 66→**39** LAUNCHER ("Looking Up" anti-air) · air 52→**31** (Jumping repulsor) · down_air 72→**43** (Double-Jump repulsor, OWN art) · crouch 42→**25** (Crouching repulsor). No command chain (Stage 3 none) — the total kit is LEANER than IM2's (which adds a ~101-EFF rekka).
> - **3 specials (RAW → EFF ×0.60, cost from the 200 pool)** — **★3-TIER CHARGE REPULSOR** (neutral, armed tap/hold, each tier fires its OWN on-sheet projectile art, size+dmg+cost ramp): Basic 44→**26** (14) · Charged 78→**47** (30) · **Supercharged 120→72 (55)** — brightest yellow burst, biggest+hardest (ties IM2's 4* top poke, NO pierce). **Super Laser** (Fwd, 40) 104→**62** — a huge fast horizontal beam (real on-sheet beam art). **Super Move** (Up + air-neutral, 36) 88→**53** — spinning-ring burst AoE, slight launch (ground/midair variants). All in existing special bands.
> - **★"SUPER NOVA" ULTIMATE (100 Repulsor) — the on-sheet "Kills all onscreen Enemies" screen-clear (owner DECISION B); a guaranteed inline freeze-cinematic (IM1/IM2/Deathstroke architecture — LIVE fighter, NO duplicate).** Iron Man throws his arms up (the Super Move pose reused as the trigger — no bespoke ult frame) and overloads the reactor into a SCREEN-FILLING procedural nova (`drawIronMan3SuperNova`: white-pink core flash + expanding concentric rings, visual-only); 3 guaranteed beats (0/110/220) land **330 raw → EXACTLY 198 EFF** (range-independent, knockdown, block 25%). Squarely in the ~198 top-ult band; NO damage bypass (`applyScaledDamage`). Screen-clear semantics = the whole-screen wash; in 1v1 the engaged foe IS "all onscreen".
> - **★RANGED-POWER-DENSITY assessment (the Stage-6 flag): NOT an outlier.** The concern — a 3-tier charge + a separate beam + a screen-clear ult = a lot of ranged power on one character — was checked against real numbers: the charge tops at **72 EFF (Supercharged, cost 55, full-hold)**, Super Laser is **62 EFF (cost 40)**, and the ult is the **standard guaranteed 198 EFF (cost 100)** — every one honestly ×0.60-scaled and cost-gated against the 200 pool (which also fuels the ult). Crucially the kit is **LEANER than IM2** (no command chain, 3 special functions vs 4, no pierce), so the ranged density buys breadth, not raw ceiling. Reads as a durable-but-slow zoner, not a record-holder.

No manual-subtract, no unscaled summon, no DoT.

Spd 96 = fast tier (below 98 top). `canFly` reuses the shared flight system.
> - **Offense (RAW → EFF ×0.60)** — normals: light 40→**24** (jab) / heavy 78→**46** (lunging long-reach punch, rangeX 92) / up 62→**37** LAUNCHER (own rising-slash art) / air 50→**30** (flying kick) / down_air 66→**40** (own dive kick) / crouchLight 34→**20** (low kick). **"Ultra Rush" chain** (Fwd+Heavy, cancel-on-hit rekka, FREE): rush1 40→**24** → rush2 48→**29** → rush3 82→**49** LAUNCHER = **~102 EFF full 3-stage confirm** (whiff/block ENDS it) — mid-class, below Genos ~179.
> - **6 specials (RAW → EFF ×0.60, cost of the 200 pool)** — **Big Bang Attack** (neutral, big slow white/blue sphere, offense-scaled radius) 116→**70** (44) · **Galick Gun** (Fwd, thin fast PURPLE piercing beam) 100→**60** (34) · **Banshee Blast** (Back, a 4-bolt rapid-fire gold VOLLEY, 24 each →~**14 ea**) (30) · **Spread Finger Beam** (Down, a 5-bolt yellow FAN at spread vy, 28 each →~**17 ea**) (34) · **Air Ki Blast** (Up, one fast rising cyan dart) 50→**30** (20) · **Perfect Shot** (air, a 2-dart cyan pair that AUTO-AIMS the foe) 46→**28** ea (24). Every value in an existing special band; Big Bang 70 EFF (44) is the priciest/strongest single shot.
> - **★ULTRA INSTINCT -SIGN- EVASION RESOURCE (Stage 5, bespoke `_uiMeter`, SEPARATE from ki)** — a defensive meter (max 100) that PASSIVELY DRAINS (~12s full→empty). While it holds (and NOT charging), an incoming hit — **melee AND projectile** — is DODGED outright (combat.`shouldVegitoUIEvade`, cost 7 meter/dodge + 4 i-frames; mirrors Gojo's per-dodge-cost auto-dodge). At 0 the drain CONVERTS to a small HP bleed (~0.11/f, won't self-KO) — no evasion while empty. Holding **Charge (P) REFILLS** the meter (~3s) but DISABLES evasion (the recharge tradeoff). The meter drives an idle-pose TELL (relaxed→arms-spread→braced) + a HUD bar. ★**This is the balance-defining lever** and is **PLAYTEST-FLAGGED**: a full-meter Vegito is briefly untouchable, paid for by (a) the constant drain, (b) the HP-bleed-at-empty, (c) the evasion-off recharge windows, and (d) the deliberately mid HP/Def. Numbers (drain rate, dodge cost, bleed rate) are **provisional tuning knobs**, not final.

Squarely in the ~198 top-ult band; NO damage bypass. Procedural beam FX (no bespoke ult beam art — flagged).

NOT a blocker. **★FLAGS (art gaps, honest reuses):** run reuses walk; fall reuses jump; lose reuses knockdown. **REAL OWN art:** idle + idle_mid/idle_low (the meter-tell STANCE groups), walk, dash, guard, hurt, knockdown, getup, all 5 normals + crouchLight, all 3 rush frames, all 6 special cast poses, the Ultimate Action windup + Kamehameha thrust, win (proud victory pose), and the portrait (top-right anime bust). The 6 special FX + the Kamehameha beam are PROCEDURAL projectiles (the sheet's beam/sphere art is illustrative — flagged for a later FX pass). ★**PIXEL SIGN-OFF PENDING** on the special/ult/win cast POSES (image-API budget capped this session → picks are content-plausible + harness-verified to render, but visual pose-aptness not eyeballed; the flood-fill face-integrity WAS verified on movement + normals). > **Teen Gohan (`gohan`, Dragon Ball — a NEW clean roster entry) — added 2026-08-23.** A **MELEE-ONLY agile bruiser** built from two DBZ Extreme Butoden (3DS) sheets (Base + Super Saiyan 2; `tools/reslice_gohan.py`), ONE roster entry / TWO forms. **HP 1200 / EN 200 / Def 84 / Spd 92 / scale 1.20 (teen — idle ~106px, just under the ~111px adults).** Kit: **5 normals + a Fwd+Heavy "Rush Combo" rekka + ONE melee special + the Base↔SSJ2 transform.** **ZERO damage-scale bypass — the ENTIRE kit runs through the ×0.60 choke-point:** the 5 normals (light 24 / heavy 46 / up-launcher 34 EFF), all 3 rush stages, and the Meteor Kick special all via `createAttackFromMove`.
> - **Rush Combo (Fwd+Heavy 3-stage rekka, cancel-on-hit)** — advancing punch → crescent kick → spin-roundhouse LAUNCHER, **~101 EFF cumulative**, FREE (commits via recovery). In-band with the Piccolo/Goku/Iron-Man/Gotenks rekkas (all ~101).
> - **★MELEE-ONLY — the ranged gap is FLAGGED, not faked.** No beam/charge/Ultimate art exists on EITHER sheet (confirmed twice) → **NO ranged special and NO ultimate**, none invented (prompt: "flag rather than invent" — same as Goku). Owner kept ONE melee special (Goku-Dragon-Fist-parallel): **Meteor Kick** — a committed forward-lunging flying kick, **78 EFF** (130 raw), 35 Ki, big lunge + hard knockback, honest art-reuse of the heavy sheet. **Spawns NO projectile** (melee-only proven in-harness).
> - **★COMPENSATION for melee-only (the prompt's Stage-6 question):** Gohan carries NO zoning tool, so he MUST close distance — the compensation is (a) **high mobility** (Spd 92 / dash 19 / dashCd 30, above the roster mid) and (b) **the SSJ2 transform as his power ceiling** (below). This is a deliberate identity (a rushdown that lives in the opponent's face), NOT an under-tuned kit needing a bolt-on ranged move. Verdict: **melee-only does NOT need ranged compensation** — the transform + mobility ARE the compensation.
> - **★SUPER SAIYAN 2 transform (Charge button) — the power ceiling.** base → **SSJ2** (**×1.30 dmg / ×1.15 spd / ×1.10 def**, threshold 120 Ki, continuous drain **0.20/f**). SAME stamina model as Vegeta/Goku/Frieza (threshold-gated, no up-front cost, continuous Ki drain, auto-revert at 0) — but **OWNER-CHOSEN ART-FAITHFUL variant: NO player tap-revert.** Revert happens ONLY via **Ki→0 (auto, ~16s window from full)** or **KNOCKDOWN** (the SSJ2 sheet's revert-on-defeat art). Entering SSJ2 is therefore a **real commitment/gamble** — you cannot bail early, and the opponent can END it by scoring a knockdown. Real gold per-form art (18 actions re-sliced from the SSJ2 sheet via `_skinAnim`, Vegeta-SSJ pattern), not a tint.

> - **NO damage-scale bypass — the entire kit runs through the ×0.60 choke-point:** the 6 normals via `createAttackFromMove` (light 24 / heavy 47 lunging-hook / up-launcher 36 / air 31 / down_air 40 honest-air-reuse / crouchLight 26 low body-blow EFF); the Y-Jabs chain + all 5 melee specials via `createAttackFromMove`; the Dempsey Roll ult's guaranteed beats via `applyScaledDamage`. No manual-subtract, no DoT, no summon, no transformation, no stat record.

Neutral Heavy stays the normal lunging hook.

> - **Overall verdict (Ippo): FAIR — a melee-only in-fighting boxer (HP 1160 / Def 84 / Spd 90, all in-band) whose identity is close-range pressure behind a real weave-defense layer, capped by two canon signatures. If anything he sits MID: no transform ceiling (Gohan) and a single standard-band ult (unlike Bardock who lacks even that), his ~54-EFF jab chain is modest, and his special economy is the tightest on the roster (100-Guts pool).** **No fix applied (diagnosis-only).** **Watch-items (three, all knobs-not-fixes):** (1) **★LAUNCHER DENSITY** — three launchers (Up+B normal + Heavy Uppercut + Gazelle) is more than most; but the two strong ones are Guts-costed specials (24/34) and Gazelle is a leaping commit, so it's gated by the small pool, NOT free. **knob: Gazelle/Uppercut cost ↑ or launch height ↓ before touching damage.** (2) **Gazelle Punch 65 EFF (34 Guts)** is the priciest/strongest single special + a leaping counter — watch it isn't an oppressive round-start nuke; **knob: cost / recovery / leap distance.** (3) **The weave DODGE/PARRY** defensive layer — currently a movement/animation state, not an i-frame evasion resource (unlike Vegito-UI/Miles-Camouflage); if a future pass wires real dodge i-frames it becomes a balance lever and must be costed. ★ **CREDITS RESOLVED (NOT a blocker)** — the source filename carries an in-name artist credit "by srchimuelo", attributed in `credits.js` SOURCED_ART (unlike the Gohan/Bardock UNKNOWN-author blockers). ★ **ART HONESTY (flagged, not faked):** WIN = a REAL 4-frame gloves-raised-OVERHEAD victory pose (on-sheet); LOSE = a REAL dedicated 7-frame defeat sequence (dejected slump → collapse → lying flat — its OWN art, NOT a knockdown reuse, unlike most siblings). down_air = honest aerial-hook reuse (no dedicated down-aerial art). run/dash = walk reuse, crouch = guard reuse (no separate stride/crouch on the sheet). Portrait = the Stage-1 idle-bust (pixel-consistent); the top-right painterly angry-shout FACE render is a DEFERRED optional portrait upgrade (style-mismatch with the pixel sprites — Miles/Iron-Man precedent). ★**"(Heart Stopped)" OPEN ITEM** — the sheet's `(Heart Stopped)` frames are pixel-identical to Idle (reused placeholder, Stage-0 item 1) → correctly NOT built as a distinct state; drop/repurpose is an owner decision. ★**Frame-pick method note:** figures physically touch in the dense JUS rows → HEAD-cluster detection gave clean frame counts (column-gap slicing merges them); every strike reaches RIGHT for free (FLIP_H=False — no per-frame facing audit needed, unlike the flipped EB rips). Canonical `test:ippo` = **34/0** (gate/stats/movement sheets/normals-connect/Y-Jab-chain/5-special dispatch+connect+melee-only/Dempsey-Roll ult 198+melee-only/win+lose real art/30-action fallback-box sweep). > **Dark Vegeta (`vegeta_dark`, Dragon Ball — a NEW, STANDALONE roster entry built from the "akuma animation" black-armor fan sheet; owner-locked option A) — added 2026-08-24.** A **melee + ki + blade-FX hybrid with a dark-aura power-up form ("Villainous Mode")**, reads as DB-Heroes "Villainous/Dark" Vegeta (black battle-armor, dark corruption aura), NOT canon Ultra Ego. Built from ONE green-keyed sheet (`tools/reslice_vegeta_dark.py` — LEFT-facing → `FLIP_H=True`). **The blue `vegeta` roster entry is COMPLETELY UNTOUCHED — this is a separate character, not an extension.** **Stats: HP 1200 / EN 200 ("Ki") / Atk 92 / Def 86 / Spd 90 / scale 2.1 (idle ≈103px, roster mid-band).** Kit: **5 normals + crouchLight + a Fwd+Heavy "Villain's Rush" 3-stage rekka + a fixed-slot special kit (Ki Blast / Knife Slash / Sickle Throw) + a dark-aura TRANSFORM.** NO separate ultimate (the transform IS the "super" — like Gohan/Bardock).
>
> - **NO damage-scale bypass — the whole kit runs through the ×0.60 choke-point:** the 6 normals via `createAttackFromMove` (light 45→**27** jab / heavy 85→**51** side-kick / up-launcher 70→**42** / air 60→**36** / down_air honest-air-reuse / crouchLight 44→**26**); the "Villain's Rush" rekka + the Knife Slash melee special via `createAttackFromMove`; the Ki Blast / Sickle Throw projectiles fold offense at spawn. No manual-subtract, no DoT, no summon, no stat record.
> - **"Villain's Rush" (Fwd+Heavy 3-stage rekka, cancel-on-hit, FREE)** — lunge→two-handed thrust [55-57] → rapid flurry [91-93] → rising-uppercut LAUNCHER (reuses the clean up-normal art), **~101 EFF cumulative** (42/50/84 RAW), whiff/block ENDS it. Right in the roster's 3-stage rekka band (Goku Meteor ~101 / Piccolo / Iron-Man / Gohan). Neutral Heavy stays the normal side-kick.
> - **Fixed-slot specials (Special-button; 200 Ki pool; mirror executeVilgaxSpecial):** neutral/air **Ki Blast** — a PROCEDURAL energy sphere, **TIERED BY TRANSFORM STATE**: base WHITE 88→**52** (small), and in the dark-aura form it AMPLIFIES to a bigger PIERCING PURPLE burst 132→**79** (RAW×1.35 form-mult compounds → **~106 EFF** measured) — this is the same attack powered up, NOT a second move · Fwd **Knife Slash** (melee disjoint) 96→**57** · Back **Sickle Throw** (procedural red crescent) 92→**55**. U/D ship unused (owner, like Vilgax). Every value in an existing special band; damage folds offense.
> - **★DARK-AURA TRANSFORM ("Villainous Mode") — the cross-variant balance check (prompt's Stage-6 requirement):** SAME Dragon Ball charge-transform stamina model as blue Vegeta/Frieza/Goku Black/Piccolo (threshold-gated hold-charge→release, per-frame Ki drain, auto-revert at 0, tap-revert). A SINGLE tier at **dmg ×1.35 / spd ×1.15 / def ×1.05**, threshold 100 Ki, drain 0.20/f (~12/s). **Compared directly to the existing blue `vegeta`'s already-aligned ladder** (`vegeta.transformations`): blue SSJ = **×1.20/1.12/1.05** (thr 120, drain 0.18) and blue SSJ-Blue = **×1.45/1.25/1.12** (thr 160, drain 0.28). **→ Dark Vegeta's single dark-aura sits cleanly BETWEEN blue Vegeta's SSJ and Blue** — a mid-tier multiplier with a mid drain (~12/s vs SSJ 11/s / Blue 17/s). **No unintended power discrepancy between the two Vegeta variants:** where blue Vegeta escalates a 3-rung ladder capped by a ×1.45 Blue AND has 3 ki-beam specials + Final Flash, Dark Vegeta has ONE form, a leaner 3-special kit, and NO ultimate. Its one distinctive perk — the transform AMPLIFYING the Ki Blast — is **bounded** (the amplified blast costs MORE Ki, 40 vs 28, so it draws harder on the same 200 pool that also feeds the drain), not free power. The stat lines are near-identical (Dark HP1200/Atk92/Def86 vs blue HP1150/Atk91/Def85 — a whisker higher HP/Atk, in-band).
> - **★COMPENSATION note (no-ultimate):** Dark Vegeta has no separate ult; the transform ladder is the ceiling (Gohan/Bardock precedent — a transform in lieu of an ult). This keeps the kit LEAN and the total power in-band: sustained buffs + an amplified blast, all Ki-bounded, with no 198-EFF nuke on top.
> - **★SUPER SAIYAN ROSE — 3rd tier added 2026-08-24 (CREATIVE, non-canon — Rose is Goku Black's transform, an original choice, flagged).** The ladder is now base → **dark-aura** (×1.35, purple, aura-only) → **ROSE** (×1.50/1.22/1.10, pink, FULL art form-swap). Rose is NOT free power: it **chains off the dark-aura** (`requiresForm: darkAura` — you must pass through it), enters at a **higher threshold (150 Ki)** with a **higher drain (0.28/f ≈ 17/s)**, and shares the same 200 Ki pool that also feeds the amplified blast. Its ×1.50 dmg is a whisker above blue Vegeta's SSJ-**Blue** ceiling (×1.45) — justified as the committed TOP tier of a longer ladder (base→aura→Rose vs blue's base→SSJ→Blue), Ki-bounded to a short window (~10.5s from 150). Still NO ultimate on top. **Watch-item (knob):** if the ×1.50 + amplified-blast top tier reads too strong, trim Rose to ×1.45 / raise the 150 threshold / raise the 0.28 drain (shorter window) before touching the base kit. In-band with the roster's transform-ceiling characters.
> - **Overall verdict: FAIR — a mid-band melee/ki hybrid (HP 1200 / Def 86 / Spd 90, all in-band) whose ceiling is a single Ki-bounded ×1.35 dark-aura form, NOT an outlier vs the blue Vegeta it shares a stamina model with.** **No fix applied (diagnosis-only).** **Watch-items (two, knobs-not-fixes):** (1) **the amplified Ki Blast** (132 RAW → ~106 EFF in-form) is the strongest single ranged hit — but it's gated behind entering the form (which drains Ki) AND costs 40 Ki per shot from the same pool; **knob: amplified cost ↑ / damage ↓ / the form's dmg-mult ↓ toward 1.30 if in-form blast pressure reads as too strong.** (2) **the dark-aura ×1.35 buff + ki-blast amplify stacking** — the form boosts BOTH melee (via offenseMult) and the blast tier; if the combined uptime reads oppressive, **knob: raise the 100-Ki entry threshold or the 0.20/f drain (shorter window) before touching multipliers.** ★ **CREDITS (open, NOT yet resolved):** the sprite carries an in-sheet "by akuma animation" credit (same animator as Aoi Todo/Naoya) + a DeviantArt ripper/uploader `mjdmadgaming`; attribute BOTH in `credits.js` before ship (not wired this pass). ★ **ART HONESTY (flagged, not faked):** WIN = a REAL 5-frame arms-crossed laughing-taunt (on-sheet); INTRO = a REAL dark-tendril reveal entrance (on-sheet — owner OPEN item: intro vs a distinct 2nd transform stage); LOSE = honest knockdown reuse (no dedicated lose art). down_air = honest air-reuse; rush3 = honest up-normal reuse (standalone frames read swing-recover); walk = run reuse, guard/crouch = arms-crossed reuse, dash/jump/fall = dive reuse (no dedicated stride/jump on the sheet). ★ **The transform is an AURA/STAT power-up, NOT a costume re-skin** (the "purple trim" Stage-0 claim was a misread — costume/hair stay; purple lives in the aura overlay + ki-blast + HUD). Ki-blast + sickle projectiles are PROCEDURAL (the sheet's blast art is illustrative — DBZ-sibling pattern); bespoke FX + the purple ringed-disc HUD art [132-135] = banked follow-ups.
